# 🌿 Le Labo Official E-commerce Mini-Program Implementation Specification

## 📋 Executive Summary

Based on Le Labo's authentic brand characteristics and successful WeChat mini-program deployment in China, this specification outlines the implementation of Le Labo's complete e-commerce experience on the existing Medusa platform, featuring authentic brand content structure, mini-program optimized design patterns, and immersive storytelling interactions.

---

## 🎯 Le Labo Brand Analysis

### 🌱 Core Brand Identity
- **Philosophy**: "The future of luxury lies in craftsmanship" - Wabi-sabi aesthetic embracing natural imperfections
- **Target Audience**: White-collar professionals, international-experienced customers, fashion/art/creative industry professionals
- **Brand Characteristics**: Gender-neutral, personalized, artisanal, minimal, authentic, premium craftsmanship

### 🎨 Visual Design Principles
- **Aesthetic**: Modern minimalist with Wabi-sabi elements, celebrating raw materials and natural imperfections
- **Typography**: Clean, modern sans-serif with handcraft-inspired labels
- **Color Palette**: Neutral earth tones, raw materials colors, understated elegance
- **Layout**: 12-column grid system, deliberate spacing, immersive storytelling focus

---

## 📱 Mini-Program Page Structure

### 🏠 1. Home Page (`/`)
**Purpose**: Brand immersion and product discovery  
**Layout**: Full-screen immersive experience with storytelling elements

#### Components:
```tsx

## 📊 Data Model Design

### 🌿 Product Data Structure
```typescript
interface LeLaboProduct {
  // Basic Product Info
  id: string;
  handle: string;
  title: string;
  subtitle?: string;
  
  // Brand-Specific Fields
  collection: 'classic' | 'city-exclusive' | 'home-creation' | 'limited-edition';
  fragrance_family: 'woody' | 'floral' | 'citrus' | 'oriental' | 'fresh';
  scent_number: number; // e.g., 33 for Santal 33
  
  // Storytelling Content
  philosophy: string;
  inspiration_story: string;
  perfumer_notes: string;
  scent_journey: {
    top_notes: string[];
    heart_notes: string[];
    base_notes: string[];
    development_timeline: string;
  };
  
  // Ingredients & Sourcing
  key_ingredients: {
    name: string;
    origin: string;
    story: string;
    percentage?: number;
  }[];
  raw_materials: string[];
  sourcing_story: string;
  
  // Product Variants
  variants: {
    id: string;
    size: '15ml' | '50ml' | '100ml';
    price: number;
    sku: string;
    inventory_quantity: number;
  }[];
  
  // Visual Assets
  images: {
    primary: string;
    gallery: string[];
    lifestyle: string[];
    ingredient_shots: string[];
    bottle_360: string[];
  };
  
  // Personalization Options
  personalization: {
    custom_labels: boolean;
    engraving_options: string[];
    gift_wrapping: boolean;
    lab_locations: string[];
  };
  
  // SEO & Marketing
  meta_title: string;
  meta_description: string;
  tags: string[];
  featured: boolean;
  bestseller: boolean;
  new_arrival: boolean;
  
  // Sensorial Data
  scent_intensity: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5;
  longevity: 'light' | 'moderate' | 'long-lasting' | 'all-day';
  season_recommendation: ('spring' | 'summer' | 'autumn' | 'winter')[];
  occasion_tags: string[];
  
  // Complementary Data
  recommended_products: string[];
  layering_suggestions: string[];
  sample_available: boolean;
}
```

### 🏠 Collection Data Structure
```typescript
interface LeLaboCollection {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  slug: string;
  description: string;
  story: string;
  hero_image: string;
  lifestyle_images: string[];
  featured_products: string[];
  meta_data: {
    title: string;
    description: string;
  };
}
```

### 🎨 Personalization Data Structure
```typescript
interface PersonalizationOptions {
  labels: {
    templates: {
      id: string;
      name: string;
      preview_image: string;
      customizable_fields: string[];
    }[];
    fonts: string[];
    colors: string[];
  };
  engraving: {
    max_characters: number;
    allowed_characters: string;
    fonts: string[];
  };
  gift_options: {
    wrapping_styles: string[];
    message_card: boolean;
    ribbon_colors: string[];
  };
}
```

## 🎨 Mini-Program Specific Interaction Logic

### 📱 Touch-Optimized Interactions

#### 1. **Swipe Navigation**
```typescript
// Product Gallery Swipe
const handleProductSwipe = {
  onSwipeLeft: () => nextImage(),
  onSwipeRight: () => previousImage(),
  onTouchLongPress: () => openImageModal(),
};

// Collection Browse
const handleCollectionSwipe = {
  onSwipeUp: () => scrollToNextCollection(),
  onSwipeDown: () => scrollToPreviousCollection(),
};
```

#### 2. **Progressive Loading**
```typescript
// Infinite Scroll for Product Lists
const useInfiniteScroll = () => {
  const [products, setProducts] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [hasMore, setHasMore] = useState(true);
  
  const loadMore = async () => {
    if (loading || !hasMore) return;
    setLoading(true);
    // Load more products
    setLoading(false);
  };
  
  return { products, loading, hasMore, loadMore };
};
```

#### 3. **Scent Visualization**
```typescript
// Interactive Scent Notes
const ScentVisualization = ({ product }: { product: LeLaboProduct }) => {
  const [activeNote, setActiveNote] = useState<string | null>(null);
  
  return (
    <div className="scent-wheel">
      {product.scent_journey.top_notes.map((note) => (
        <TouchableNote
          key={note}
          note={note}
          onPress={() => setActiveNote(note)}
          isActive={activeNote === note}
        />
      ))}
    </div>
  );
};
```

### 🔄 State Management Patterns

#### 1. **Cart Persistence**
```typescript
// Mini-program optimized cart storage
const usePersistedCart = () => {
  const [cart, setCart] = useState(() => {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('lelabo-cart') || '[]');
    }
    return [];
  });
  
  useEffect(() => {
    localStorage.setItem('lelabo-cart', JSON.stringify(cart));
  }, [cart]);
  
  return [cart, setCart];
};
```

#### 2. **Scent Preferences Tracking**
```typescript
// User scent preference learning
const useScentPreferences = () => {
  const [preferences, setPreferences] = useState({
    favoriteFamily: null,
    intensityPreference: null,
    viewedProducts: [],
    likedProducts: [],
  });
  
  const trackInteraction = (productId: string, action: string) => {
    // Track user behavior for recommendations
  };
  
  return { preferences, trackInteraction };
};
```

### 🎯 Conversion Optimization Features

#### 1. **Quick Add to Cart**
```typescript
const QuickAddButton = ({ product }: { product: LeLaboProduct }) => {
  const [isAdding, setIsAdding] = useState(false);
  const [showSuccess, setShowSuccess] = useState(false);
  
  const handleQuickAdd = async () => {
    setIsAdding(true);
    await addToCart(product.variants[0]); // Default size
    setIsAdding(false);
    setShowSuccess(true);
    setTimeout(() => setShowSuccess(false), 2000);
  };
  
  return (
    <Button
      onClick={handleQuickAdd}
      loading={isAdding}
      className={`quick-add ${showSuccess ? 'success' : ''}`}
    >
      {showSuccess ? '✓ Added' : 'Quick Add'}
    </Button>
  );
};
```

#### 2. **Sample Request Flow**
```typescript
const SampleRequestModal = ({ product }: { product: LeLaboProduct }) => {
  const [sampleCart, setSampleCart] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const maxSamples = 3;
  
  const addSample = (productId: string) => {
    if (sampleCart.length < maxSamples) {
      setSampleCart([...sampleCart, productId]);
    }
  };
  
  return (
    <Modal>
      <h3>Try Before You Buy</h3>
      <p>Select up to {maxSamples} samples</p>
      {/* Sample selection UI */}
    </Modal>
  );
};
```

### 🎯 Gesture Interactions
```tsx
// Pull-to-refresh for product lists
const usePullToRefresh = (onRefresh: () => Promise<void>) => {
  const [isRefreshing, setIsRefreshing] = useState(false);
  
  const handleRefresh = async () => {
    setIsRefreshing(true);
    await onRefresh();
    setIsRefreshing(false);
  };
  
  return { isRefreshing, handleRefresh };
};

// Long press for quick actions
const useLongPress = (onLongPress: () => void, delay = 500) => {
  const [longPressTriggered, setLongPressTriggered] = useState(false);
  const timeout = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout>();
  
  const start = useCallback(() => {
    timeout.current = setTimeout(() => {
      onLongPress();
      setLongPressTriggered(true);
    }, delay);
  }, [onLongPress, delay]);
  
  const clear = useCallback(() => {
    timeout.current && clearTimeout(timeout.current);
    setLongPressTriggered(false);
  }, []);
  
  return {
    onTouchStart: start,
    onTouchEnd: clear,
    onTouchCancel: clear,
    longPressTriggered,
  };
};
```

# 🌿 Le Labo 官方电商小程序页面架构设计

## 一、页面（按导航信息架构）

### 📱 主导航架构
```
Le Labo 小程序
├── 🏠 首页发现 (/)
├── 🔍 香调寻觅 (/scent-finder)  
├── 🛍️ 精选系列 (/collections)
├── 🛒 购物车 (/cart)
└── 👤 个人中心 (/account)
```

---

## 🏠 **首页发现** (`/`)
> **定位**: 品牌沉浸式体验中心，产品发现起点

### 页面结构
```tsx
首页发现页
├── 🎬 品牌沉浸区
│   ├── Hero 视频轮播 (Santal 33 | Another 13 | Rose 31)
│   ├── 品牌理念文案 "The future of luxury lies in craftsmanship"
│   └── 进入探索按钮
│
├── 🌟 经典系列展示
│   ├── Classic Collection 横滑卡片
│   │   ├── Santal 33 - "An open fire... The soft drift of smoke"
│   │   ├── Another 13 - "Understated, musky, skin scent" 
│   │   ├── Rose 31 - "Spicy, earthy roses, rugged, woody"
│   │   ├── Thé Matcha 26 - "Soul-soothing matcha accord"
│   │   └── Bergamote 22 - "Rich and citrusy, grounded"
│   └── 查看全部经典系列
│
├── 🏙️ 城市限定系列
│   ├── City Exclusive Collection 瀑布流
│   │   ├── New York 纽约限定
│   │   ├── Paris 巴黎限定  
│   │   ├── Tokyo 东京限定
│   │   └── Shanghai 上海限定
│   └── 发现城市香调
│
├── 🏠 家居香氛
│   ├── Home Creations 生活方式展示
│   │   ├── 香薰蜡烛系列
│   │   ├── 室内香氛喷雾
│   │   └── 藤条扩香器
│   └── 探索家居香氛
│
├── 🎯 个性定制服务
│   ├── Personalization 定制预览
│   │   ├── 个性化标签定制
│   │   ├── 刻字服务
│   │   └── 实验室现场调配
│   └── 开始定制
│
└── 🔍 香调寻觅工具
    ├── 智能香调推荐
    ├── 香调偏好测试
    └── 进入寻觅模式
```

### 核心功能组件
- **沉浸式品牌视频**: 全屏播放品牌理念影片
- **产品故事卡片**: 每款香水的独特故事展示
- **快速加购**: 一键添加到购物车
- **个性化推荐**: 基于用户行为的智能推荐

---

## 🔍 **香调寻觅** (`/scent-finder`)
> **定位**: 个性化香调发现与推荐引擎

### 页面结构
```tsx
香调寻觅页
├── 🎯 寻觅引导
│   ├── "发现你的专属香调" 标题
│   ├── 寻觅流程说明
│   └── 开始寻觅按钮
│
├── 📝 偏好问卷 (步骤式)
│   ├── 第1步: 香调偏好
│   │   ├── 木质调 (Woody) - 温暖、深邃
│   │   ├── 花香调 (Floral) - 优雅、浪漫  
│   │   ├── 柑橘调 (Citrus) - 清新、活力
│   │   ├── 东方调 (Oriental) - 神秘、奢华
│   │   └── 清新调 (Fresh) - 纯净、自然
│   │
│   ├── 第2步: 使用场景
│   │   ├── 日常通勤
│   │   ├── 约会场合
│   │   ├── 商务会议
│   │   ├── 居家放松
│   │   └── 特殊场合
│   │
│   ├── 第3步: 强度偏好
│   │   ├── 淡香 (轻盈持久)
│   │   ├── 中等 (平衡持久)
│   │   └── 浓郁 (强烈持久)
│   │
│   └── 第4步: 季节偏好
│       ├── 春季清新
│       ├── 夏季清爽
│       ├── 秋季温暖
│       └── 冬季深邃
│
├── 🎉 推荐结果
│   ├── 你的专属香调画像
│   ├── Top 3 推荐香水
│   │   ├── 匹配度显示
│   │   ├── 推荐理由
│   │   ├── 香调解析
│   │   └── 立即体验
│   └── 更多可能喜欢
│
└── 🧪 试香体验
    ├── 试香装申请
    ├── 线下实验室预约
    └── 虚拟试香 (AR体验)
```

## 🛍️ **精选系列** (`/collections`)
> **定位**: 产品浏览与深度探索中心

### 页面结构
```tsx
精选系列页
├── 🎨 系列导航
│   ├── Classic Collection (经典系列)
│   ├── City Exclusive (城市限定)
│   ├── Home Creations (家居香氛)
│   └── Limited Edition (限量版)
│
├── 🔽 筛选与排序
│   ├── 香调分类筛选
│   ├── 价格区间筛选
│   ├── 容量选择筛选
│   ├── 排序方式
│   │   ├── 推荐排序
│   │   ├── 价格升序
│   │   ├── 价格降序
│   │   ├── 新品优先
│   │   └── 热门优先
│   └── 筛选重置
│
├── 📦 产品网格展示
│   ├── 产品卡片 (每行2个)
│   │   ├── 产品高清图片
│   │   ├── 产品名称 + 编号
│   │   ├── 核心香调标签
│   │   ├── 价格显示
│   │   ├── 快速加购按钮
│   │   ├── 收藏按钮
│   │   └── 手势交互
│   │       ├── 点击进入详情
│   │       ├── 长按快速预览
│   │       └── 左滑快速收藏
│   └── 无限滚动加载
│
└── 🎯 智能推荐
    ├── "你可能喜欢" 横滑区域
    ├── "经常一起购买" 组合推荐
    └── "浏览历史" 快速回访
```

### 核心功能组件
- **动态筛选器**: 实时筛选，无需页面刷新
- **产品快预览**: 长按展示产品详细信息
- **智能排序**: 基于用户偏好的个性化排序
- **收藏管理**: 一键收藏，同步到个人中心

### 页面结构
```tsx
产品详情页 (/products/santal-33)

│   └── 购买操作区

## 🛒 **购物车** (`/cart`)

购物车页

这个页面架构设计完全基于Le Labo的真实品牌特性和产品结构，充分考虑了小程序的交互特点和用户习惯，为后续的前端功能实现、数据字段设计和交互逻辑提供了坚实的信息架构基础。

## 📱 小程序交互特性

### 🔄 **滚动与手势交互**
```typescript
// 首页滚动交互
const HomePageInteractions = {
  // 主横幅左右滑动
  heroBanner: {
    swipeLeft: () => nextSlide(),
    swipeRight: () => previousSlide(),
    autoPlay: true,
    interval: 5000,
  },
  
  // 精选系列网格
  collectionsGrid: {
    scrollDirection: 'vertical',
    lazyLoad: true,
    threshold: 0.5,
  },
  
  // 热卖产品横滑
  bestSellers: {
    scrollDirection: 'horizontal',
    snapToCenter: true,
    showScrollbar: false,
  },
  
  // 门店地图交互
  storeMap: {
    pinTap: (storeId) => showStoreDetails(storeId),
    zoom: true,
    drag: true,
  },
};
```

## 🔧 技术实现要点

### 📊 **状态管理**
```typescript
// 列表页状态管理
interface PLPState {
  // 筛选状态
  filters: {
    collections: string[];
    fragranceFamilies: string[];
    sizes: string[];
    priceRange: [number, number];
    isSpecial: {
      cityExclusive: boolean;
      labExclusive: boolean;
      limitedEdition: boolean;
      personalizationAvailable: boolean;
    };
    launchDate: string;
  };
  
  // 排序状态
  sorting: {
    field: 'popularity' | 'launch_date' | 'price' | 'sales' | 'rating' | 'name';
    direction: 'asc' | 'desc';
  };
  
  // 列表状态
  products: Product[];
  totalCount: number;
  currentPage: number;
  loading: boolean;
  hasMore: boolean;
  
  // UI状态
  showFilters: boolean;
  showSorting: boolean;
  quickPreview: Product | null;
}
```

### 🎛️ **筛选逻辑**
```typescript
// 筛选功能实现
const useProductFilters = () => {
  const applyFilters = (products: Product[], filters: FilterState) => {
    return products.filter(product => {
      // 系列筛选
      if (filters.collections.length > 0) {
        if (!filters.collections.includes(product.collection)) return false;
      }
      
      // 香调筛选
      if (filters.fragranceFamilies.length > 0) {
        if (!filters.fragranceFamilies.includes(product.fragrance_family)) return false;
      }
      
      // 容量筛选
      if (filters.sizes.length > 0) {
        const availableSizes = product.variants.map(v => v.size);
        if (!filters.sizes.some(size => availableSizes.includes(size))) return false;
      }
      
      // 价格区间筛选
      const minPrice = Math.min(...product.variants.map(v => v.price));
      const maxPrice = Math.max(...product.variants.map(v => v.price));
      if (minPrice > filters.priceRange[1] || maxPrice < filters.priceRange[0]) return false;
      
      // 特殊属性筛选
      if (filters.isSpecial.cityExclusive && product.collection !== 'city-exclusive') return false;
      if (filters.isSpecial.labExclusive && !product.lab_exclusive) return false;
      
      return true;
    });
  };
  
  return { applyFilters };
};
```

### 📱 **小程序优化**
```typescript
// 小程序性能优化
const PLPOptimizations = {
  // 虚拟滚动
  virtualScroll: {
    itemHeight: 280, // 产品卡片高度
    threshold: 3, // 预加载阈值
    recycleScrollTop: 1000, // 回收阈值
  },
  
  // 图片懒加载
  lazyLoad: {
    threshold: 0.3,
    rootMargin: '50px',
    placeholder: '/images/product-placeholder.jpg',
  },
  
  // 数据缓存
  cache: {
    products: 'lelabo-products-cache',
    filters: 'lelabo-filters-cache',
    userPreferences: 'lelabo-user-prefs',
    expiry: 30 * 60 * 1000, // 30分钟
  },
  
  // 防抖搜索
  debounce: {
    searchDelay: 300,
    filterDelay: 500,
  },
};
```

这个列表页设计完全基于Le Labo的真实产品结构，提供了完整的筛选、排序和浏览功能，充分考虑了小程序的交互特点和性能要求，为用户提供高效的产品发现和比较体验。

# 🎨 **个性化定制面板** - Le Labo PDP 内嵌定制系统

## 📱 定制面板结构设计

基于Le Labo真实个性化定制服务和小程序交互特点设计：

### 🎯 **定制面板入口**
```tsx

### 🔧 **输入验证与错误处理**
```typescript
// 定制表单验证逻辑
const PersonalizationValidation = {
  labelName: {
    // 字符长度验证
    validateLength: (input: string): ValidationResult => {
      const isChinese = /[\u4e00-\u9fa5]/.test(input);
      const maxLength = isChinese ? 11 : 22;
      
      // 计算实际字符数 (中文算1个，英文算1个)
      const actualLength = input.length;
      
      return {
        isValid: actualLength <= maxLength,
        errorMessage: actualLength > maxLength 
          ? `字符数超限，当前${actualLength}/${maxLength}` 
          : '',
        warningMessage: actualLength > maxLength * 0.8 
          ? `接近字符限制${actualLength}/${maxLength}` 
          : ''
      };
    },
    
    // 字符类型验证
    validateCharacters: (input: string): ValidationResult => {
      // 允许的字符：字母、数字、空格、中文、单引号
      const allowedPattern = /^[a-zA-Z0-9\s\u4e00-\u9fa5']*$/;
      const hasDisallowedChars = !allowedPattern.test(input);
      
      // 检查连续空格
      const hasMultipleSpaces = /\s{2,}/.test(input);
      
      // 检查开头结尾空格
      const hasLeadingTrailingSpaces = input !== input.trim();
      
      return {
        isValid: !hasDisallowedChars && !hasMultipleSpaces && !hasLeadingTrailingSpaces,
        errorMessage: hasDisallowedChars 
          ? '包含不允许的字符，仅支持字母、数字、空格、中文' 
          : hasMultipleSpaces 
          ? '不允许连续空格' 
          : hasLeadingTrailingSpaces 
          ? '开头和结尾不能有空格' 
          : ''
      };
    },
    
    // 实时输入过滤
    filterInput: (input: string): string => {
      // 移除不允许的字符
      return input
        .replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9\s\u4e00-\u9fa5']/g, '') // 只保留允许字符
        .replace(/\s+/g, ' ') // 多个空格变成单个
        .replace(/^\s+|\s+$/g, ''); // 移除开头结尾空格
    }
  },
  
  date: {
    validateFormat: (dateString: string): ValidationResult => {
      const datePattern = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/;
      if (!datePattern.test(dateString)) {
        return {
          isValid: false,
          errorMessage: '日期格式错误，请使用 YYYY-MM-DD 格式'
        };
      }
      
      const date = new Date(dateString);
      const isValidDate = date instanceof Date && !isNaN(date.getTime());
      
      return {
        isValid: isValidDate,
        errorMessage: !isValidDate ? '无效的日期' : ''
      };
    },
    
    validateRange: (dateString: string): ValidationResult => {
      const date = new Date(dateString);
      const minDate = new Date('1900-01-01');
      const maxDate = new Date('2099-12-31');
      
      const isInRange = date >= minDate && date <= maxDate;
      
      return {
        isValid: isInRange,
        errorMessage: !isInRange ? '日期超出有效范围 (1900-2099)' : ''
      };
    }
  },
  
  giftNote: {
    validateLength: (input: string): ValidationResult => {
      const maxLength = 120;
      const currentLength = input.length;
      
      return {
        isValid: currentLength <= maxLength,
        errorMessage: currentLength > maxLength 
          ? `寄语过长，当前${currentLength}/${maxLength}字符` 
          : '',
        warningMessage: currentLength > 100 
          ? `接近字符限制${currentLength}/${maxLength}` 
          : ''
      };
    },
    
    validateContent: (input: string): ValidationResult => {
      // 检查敏感词 (简化示例)
      const sensitiveWords = ['广告', '推广', '联系方式'];
      const hasSensitiveContent = sensitiveWords.some(word => input.includes(word));
      
      return {
        isValid: !hasSensitiveContent,
        errorMessage: hasSensitiveContent ? '寄语包含不当内容，请修改' : ''
      };
    }
  }
};

// 实时预览更新逻辑
const usePersonalizationPreview = () => {
  const [previewData, setPreviewData] = useState({
    labelName: '',
    date: '',
    giftNote: '',
    isValid: false
  });
  
  const updatePreview = (field: string, value: string) => {
    setPreviewData(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      [field]: value,
      isValid: validateAllFields({ ...prev, [field]: value })
    }));
  };
  
  const generatePreviewImage = () => {
    // 生成标签预览图逻辑
    return {
      labelImageUrl: generateLabelImage(previewData),
      giftCardImageUrl: generateGiftCardImage(previewData.giftNote)
    };
  };
  
  return { previewData, updatePreview, generatePreviewImage };
};
```

### 🔧 **技术实现要点**

```typescript
// 购物车状态管理
interface CartItem {
  id: string;
  productId: string;
  variantId: string;
  quantity: number;
  // 基础商品信息
  product: {
    name: string;
    handle: string;
    collection: string;
    fragranceFamily: string;
    images: string[];
  };
  variant: {
    size: string;
    price: number;
    sku: string;
    inventory: number;
  };
  // 个性化定制信息
  personalization?: {
    labelName: string;
    date?: string;
    giftNote?: string;
    cost: number;
    previewImage?: string;
  };
  // 附加服务
  addons: {
    giftWrapping?: {
      style: string;
      cost: number;
    };
    expeditedMaking?: {
      enabled: boolean;
      cost: number;
    };
    specialDelivery?: {
      type: string;
      cost: number;
    };
  };
  // 状态信息
  status: {
    inStock: boolean;
    priceChanged: boolean;
    validPersonalization: boolean;
  };
  // 时间戳
  addedAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
}

interface CartState {
  items: CartItem[];
  selectedItems: string[]; // 选中的商品ID
  totals: {
    subtotal: number;
    personalizationFee: number;
    addonFees: number;
    shipping: number;
    tax: number;
    discount: number;
    total: number;
  };
  coupons: string[];
  pointsUsed: number;
  shippingMethod: string;
  giftWrapping: boolean;
}

// 购物车操作
const useCart = () => {
  const [cart, setCart] = useState<CartState>(initialCart);
  
  // 添加商品到购物车
  const addItem = (product: Product, variant: Variant, personalization?: PersonalizationData) => {
    const existingItemIndex = cart.items.findIndex(
      item => item.productId === product.id && 
               item.variantId === variant.id &&
               (!personalization || JSON.stringify(item.personalization) === JSON.stringify(personalization))
    );
    
    if (existingItemIndex >= 0) {
      updateQuantity(cart.items[existingItemIndex].id, cart.items[existingItemIndex].quantity + 1);
    } else {
      const newItem: CartItem = {
        id: generateId(),
        productId: product.id,
        variantId: variant.id,
        quantity: 1,
        product: {
          name: product.title,
          handle: product.handle,
          collection: product.collection,
          fragranceFamily: product.fragrance_family,
          images: product.images.gallery,
        },
        variant: {
          size: variant.size,
          price: variant.price,
          sku: variant.sku,
          inventory: variant.inventory_quantity,
        },
        personalization,
        addons: {},
        status: {
          inStock: variant.inventory_quantity > 0,
          priceChanged: false,
          validPersonalization: personalization ? validatePersonalization(personalization) : true,
        },
        addedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      };
      
      setCart(prev => ({
        ...prev,
        items: [...prev.items, newItem],
      }));
    }
    
    // 自动显示 Mini Cart
    showMiniCart();
  };
  
  // 更新商品数量
  const updateQuantity = (itemId: string, newQuantity: number) => {
    if (newQuantity <= 0) {
      removeItem(itemId);
      return;
    }
    
    setCart(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      items: prev.items.map(item => 
        item.id === itemId 
          ? { ...item, quantity: newQuantity, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() }
          : item
      ),
    }));
  };
  
  // 删除商品
  const removeItem = (itemId: string) => {
    setCart(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      items: prev.items.filter(item => item.id !== itemId),
      selectedItems: prev.selectedItems.filter(id => id !== itemId),
    }));
  };
  
  // 编辑个性化定制
  const updatePersonalization = (itemId: string, personalization: PersonalizationData) => {
    setCart(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      items: prev.items.map(item => 
        item.id === itemId 
          ? { 
              ...item, 
              personalization,
              status: {
                ...item.status,
                validPersonalization: validatePersonalization(personalization),
              },
              updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
            }
          : item
      ),
    }));
  };
  
  // 批量操作
  const batchOperations = {
    selectAll: () => {
      setCart(prev => ({
        ...prev,
        selectedItems: prev.items.map(item => item.id),
      }));
    },
    
    deselectAll: () => {
      setCart(prev => ({
        ...prev,
        selectedItems: [],
      }));
    },
    
    removeSelected: () => {
      setCart(prev => ({
        ...prev,
        items: prev.items.filter(item => !prev.selectedItems.includes(item.id)),
        selectedItems: [],
      }));
    },
    
    moveToWishlist: (itemIds: string[]) => {
      // 移至心愿单逻辑
      itemIds.forEach(itemId => {
        const item = cart.items.find(i => i.id === itemId);
        if (item) {
          addToWishlist(item.productId, item.variantId);
        }
      });
      
      setCart(prev => ({
        ...prev,
        items: prev.items.filter(item => !itemIds.includes(item.id)),
        selectedItems: prev.selectedItems.filter(id => !itemIds.includes(id)),
      }));
    },
  };
  
  // 计算总价
  const calculateTotals = useCallback(() => {
    const selectedCartItems = cart.items.filter(item => 
      cart.selectedItems.includes(item.id)
    );
    
    const subtotal = selectedCartItems.reduce((sum, item) => 
      sum + (item.variant.price * item.quantity), 0
    );
    
    const personalizationFee = selectedCartItems.reduce((sum, item) => 
      sum + ((item.personalization?.cost || 0) * item.quantity), 0
    );
    
    const addonFees = selectedCartItems.reduce((sum, item) => {
      const giftWrapping = item.addons.giftWrapping?.cost || 0;
      const expedited = item.addons.expeditedMaking?.cost || 0;
      const specialDelivery = item.addons.specialDelivery?.cost || 0;
      return sum + ((giftWrapping + expedited + specialDelivery) * item.quantity);
    }, 0);
    
    // 计算运费
    const shipping = calculateShipping(subtotal, cart.shippingMethod);
    
    // 计算税费
    const tax = (subtotal + personalizationFee + addonFees) * 0.13;
    
    // 计算折扣
    const discount = calculateDiscount(subtotal, cart.coupons, cart.pointsUsed);
    
    // 最终总价
    const total = subtotal + personalizationFee + addonFees + shipping + tax - discount;
    
    return {
      subtotal,
      personalizationFee,
      addonFees,
      shipping,
      tax,
      discount,
      total,
    };
  }, [cart.items, cart.selectedItems, cart.shippingMethod, cart.coupons, cart.pointsUsed]);
  
  return {
    cart,
    addItem,
    updateQuantity,
    removeItem,
    updatePersonalization,
    batchOperations,
    totals: calculateTotals(),
  };
};
```

这个购物车系统完全基于Le Labo的真实产品结构和服务特色，充分考虑了个性化定制、附加服务、批量操作等复杂场景，为用户提供流畅的购物车管理体验。

Assistant:
；骨架屏/空态/错误提示；表单就地报错。

### 🔧 **技术实现要点**

```typescript
// 订单确认页面数据结构
interface OrderConfirmation {
  // 订单基础信息
  order: {
    id: string;
    orderNumber: string;
    createdAt: string;
    status: OrderStatus;
    totalAmount: number;
  };
  
  // 商品明细
  items: OrderItem[];
  
  // 个性化定制内容
  personalizations: PersonalizationDetail[];
  
  // 金额明细
  pricing: {
    subtotal: number;
    personalizationFee: number;
    addonFees: number;
    shipping: number;
    tax: number;
    discounts: Discount[];
    total: number;
  };
  
  // 配送信息
  delivery: {
    method: 'standard' | 'express' | 'pickup';
    address?: DeliveryAddress;
    store?: PickupStore;
    estimatedDelivery: string;
  };
  
  // 支付信息
  payment: {
    method: string;
    transactionId: string;
    paidAt: string;
    amount: number;
  };
  
  // 推荐商品
  recommendations: {
    bodyProducts: Product[];
    homeProducts: Product[];
    relatedProducts: Product[];
  };
}

// 订单确认页面逻辑
const useOrderConfirmation = (orderId: string) => {
  const [orderData, setOrderData] = useState<OrderConfirmation | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  
  useEffect(() => {
    fetchOrderConfirmation(orderId).then(data => {
      setOrderData(data);
      setLoading(false);
      
      // 订单成功埋点
      trackOrderSuccess(data);
      
      // 推荐算法数据收集
      collectUserPreferences(data);
    });
  }, [orderId]);
  
  const handleContinueShopping = () => {
    // 跳转到推荐页面或首页
    router.push('/');
  };
  
  const handleShareOrder = () => {
    // 生成分享内容
    const shareContent = generateShareContent(orderData);
    // 调用分享API
    shareToSocial(shareContent);
  };
  
  const handleContactService = () => {
    // 打开客服聊天
    openCustomerService(orderData.order.orderNumber);
  };
  
  return {
    orderData,
    loading,
    handleContinueShopping,
    handleShareOrder,
    handleContactService,
  };
};

// 个性化推荐算法
const generateRecommendations = (orderItems: OrderItem[]): RecommendationSet => {
  const purchasedFragrances = orderItems.map(item => item.product.fragranceFamily);
  const purchasedCollections = orderItems.map(item => item.product.collection);
  
  return {
    // Body Care 推荐：匹配相同香调
    bodyProducts: getBodyProductsByFragrance(purchasedFragrances),
    
    // Home 推荐：匹配相同香调
    homeProducts: getHomeProductsByFragrance(purchasedFragrances),
    
    // 相关推荐：同系列或互补香调
    relatedProducts: getRelatedProducts(purchasedCollections, purchasedFragrances),
  };
};

// 用户行为追踪
const trackOrderSuccess = (orderData: OrderConfirmation) => {
  // 转化事件追踪
  analytics.track('Order Completed', {
    orderId: orderData.order.id,
    revenue: orderData.pricing.total,
    products: orderData.items.map(item => ({
      productId: item.product.id,
      name: item.product.name,
      category: item.product.collection,
      price: item.variant.price,
      quantity: item.quantity,
      personalized: !!item.personalization,
    })),
    personalizationRevenue: orderData.pricing.personalizationFee,
  });
  
  // 个性化偏好学习
  const preferences = {
    fragrancePreferences: extractFragrancePreferences(orderData.items),
    personalizationUsage: orderData.personalizations.length > 0,
    priceRange: categorizeSpendingLevel(orderData.pricing.total),
    purchaseOccasion: inferPurchaseOccasion(orderData),
  };
  
  updateUserPreferences(preferences);
};
```

这个订单确认页面设计完全基于Le Labo的真实服务特色，突出了个性化定制内容的展示，并通过智能推荐引导用户继续购买相关产品，形成完整的购物闭环体验。

You: 
Assistant:
?所需）
	•	产品：id, name, collection, is_city_exclusive, is_store_only, story, notes_top/middle/base, ingredients, origin, images[]

    │       ├── 立即启动查找
    │       ├── 配送轨迹追溯
    │       ├── 重新发货安排
    │       └── 损失赔偿处理
    │
    ├── 🔧 解决方案
    │   ├── 客服专线支持
    │   ├── 快递公司协调
    │   ├── 门店协助处理
    │   └── 上级主管介入
    │
    └── 📞 投诉渠道
        ├── 公司投诉热线
        ├── 快递公司投诉
        ├── 监管部门举报
        └── 第三方平台投诉
```

这个FAQ/政策系统完全基于Le Labo的真实服务标准和电商行业最佳实践，为用户提供全面详细的政策说明和问题解答，确保透明度和用户权益保护。

## 📱 小程序开发基础规范

基于Le Labo品牌特色和小程序技术特点制定的统一开发标准：

### 🏗️ **项目架构规范**
```tsx
Le Labo 小程序项目结构
├── 📁 src/
│   ├── 📁 app/                    # Next.js 15 App Router
│   │   ├── 📁 (home)/            # 首页路由组
│   │   ├── 📁 products/          # 产品相关页面
│   │   ├── 📁 cart/              # 购物车页面
│   │   ├── 📁 checkout/          # 结账流程
│   │   ├── 📁 account/           # 个人中心
│   │   ├── 📁 stores/            # 门店定位
│   │   ├── 📁 help/              # 帮助中心

│   │   ├── loading.tsx           # 全局加载
│   │   ├── error.tsx             # 全局错误

│   │

│   │   │   ├── cart-context.tsx  # 购物车上下文
│   │   │   ├── auth-context.tsx  # 认证上下文

│   │   └── 📁 constants/         # 常量定义
│   │       ├── routes.ts         # 路由常量
│   │       ├── config.ts         # 配置常量

│   │

│   │

│   │   ├── product.ts            # 产品类型

│   │

│       ├── stores.json           # 门店数据

### 📱 **响应式设计规范**
```typescript
// 响应式设计断点和规则
const ResponsiveBreakpoints = {
  // 断点定义 (与Tailwind CSS一致)
  mobile: '640px',      // 0-640px
  tablet: '768px',      // 641-768px  
  laptop: '1024px',     // 769-1024px
  desktop: '1280px',    // 1025-1280px
  wide: '1536px',       // 1281px+
  
  // 容器最大宽度
  containerMaxWidth: {
    mobile: '100%',
    tablet: '640px',
    laptop: '768px', 
    desktop: '1024px',
    wide: '1280px',
  },
  
  // 网格系统
  gridColumns: {
    mobile: 2,          // 2列布局
    tablet: 3,          // 3列布局
    laptop: 4,          // 4列布局
    desktop: 5,         // 5列布局
    wide: 6,            // 6列布局
  },
  
  // 字体缩放
  fontScale: {
    mobile: 0.875,      // 14px base
    tablet: 1,          // 16px base
    laptop: 1,          // 16px base
    desktop: 1.125,     // 18px base
    wide: 1.25,         // 20px base
  },
  
  // 间距缩放
  spacingScale: {
    mobile: 0.75,       // 12px 基础间距
    tablet: 1,          // 16px 基础间距
    laptop: 1,          // 16px 基础间距
    desktop: 1.25,      // 20px 基础间距
    wide: 1.5,          // 24px 基础间距
  }
};

// 响应式工具类
export const responsive = {
  // 媒体查询工具
  mobile: (styles: string) => `@media (max-width: ${ResponsiveBreakpoints.mobile}) { ${styles} }`,
  tablet: (styles: string) => `@media (min-width: 641px) and (max-width: ${ResponsiveBreakpoints.tablet}) { ${styles} }`,
  laptop: (styles: string) => `@media (min-width: 769px) and (max-width: ${ResponsiveBreakpoints.laptop}) { ${styles} }`,
  desktop: (styles: string) => `@media (min-width: 1025px) and (max-width: ${ResponsiveBreakpoints.desktop}) { ${styles} }`,
  wide: (styles: string) => `@media (min-width: 1281px) { ${styles} }`,
  
  // 容器组件
  Container: ({ children, className = '' }: { children: React.ReactNode; className?: string }) => (
    <div className={`mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 max-w-7xl ${className}`}>
      {children}
    </div>
  ),
  
  // 网格组件
  Grid: ({ children, className = '' }: { children: React.ReactNode; className?: string }) => (
    <div className={`grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4 xl:grid-cols-5 2xl:grid-cols-6 gap-4 ${className}`}>
      {children}
    </div>
  ),
};
```

### 🧩 **组件开发规范**
```typescript
// 组件开发标准模板
interface ComponentProps {
  // Props类型定义
  children?: React.ReactNode;
  className?: string;
  variant?: 'primary' | 'secondary' | 'accent';
  size?: 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg';
  disabled?: boolean;
  loading?: boolean;
  onClick?: () => void;
}

// 组件实现示例
export const LeLaboButton: React.FC<ComponentProps> = ({
  children,
  className = '',
  variant = 'primary',
  size = 'md',
  disabled = false,
  loading = false,
  onClick,
  ...props
}) => {
  // 样式变体
  const variants = {
    primary: 'bg-lelabo-primary text-white hover:bg-lelabo-primary-dark',
    secondary: 'bg-lelabo-secondary text-lelabo-primary hover:bg-lelabo-secondary-dark',
    accent: 'bg-lelabo-accent text-white hover:bg-lelabo-accent-dark',
  };
  
  const sizes = {
    sm: 'px-3 py-1.5 text-sm',
    md: 'px-4 py-2 text-base', 
    lg: 'px-6 py-3 text-lg',
  };
  
  // 基础样式
  const baseStyles = 'inline-flex items-center justify-center font-medium rounded-md transition-colors duration-200 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed';
  
  // 组合样式
  const combinedStyles = `
    ${baseStyles}
    ${variants[variant]}
    ${sizes[size]}
    ${disabled ? 'opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed' : ''}
    ${loading ? 'cursor-wait' : ''}
    ${className}
  `.trim();
  
  return (
    <button
      className={combinedStyles}
      disabled={disabled || loading}
      onClick={onClick}
      {...props}
    >
      {loading && (
        <svg className="animate-spin -ml-1 mr-2 h-4 w-4" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
          <circle className="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="4" />
          <path className="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z" />
        </svg>
      )}
      {children}
    </button>
  );
};

// 组件使用示例
const ExampleUsage = () => (
  <div className="space-y-4">
    <LeLaboButton variant="primary" size="lg">
      立即购买
    </LeLaboButton>
    <LeLaboButton variant="secondary" loading>
      加载中...
    </LeLaboButton>
    <LeLaboButton variant="accent" disabled>
      已售罄
    </LeLaboButton>
  </div>
);
```

### 🎯 **交互行为规范**
```typescript
// 用户交互标准
const InteractionStandards = {
  // 触摸目标最小尺寸
  touchTarget: {
    minSize: '44px',        // 最小触摸面积 44x44px
    recommendedSize: '48px', // 推荐触摸面积 48x48px
    spacing: '8px',         // 触摸目标间距
  },
  
  // 动画时长标准
  animation: {
    instant: '75ms',        // 即时反馈
    fast: '150ms',          // 快速交互
    normal: '300ms',        // 标准动画
    slow: '500ms',          // 慢速动画
    pageTransition: '700ms', // 页面转场
  },
  
  // 反馈时机
  feedback: {
    hover: '0ms',           // 悬停立即反馈
    click: '75ms',          // 点击反馈
    loading: '300ms',       // 加载状态延迟
    success: '150ms',       // 成功状态
    error: '100ms',         // 错误状态
  },
  
  // 手势交互
  gestures: {
    tap: {
      duration: '300ms',
      feedback: 'visual + haptic',
      ripple: true,
    },
    longPress: {
      duration: '500ms',
      feedback: 'haptic',
      previewDelay: '150ms',
    },
    swipe: {
      threshold: '50px',
      velocity: '0.3px/ms',
      friction: '0.98',
    },
    pinch: {
      threshold: '1.1x',
      maxZoom: '5x',
      minZoom: '0.5x',
    },
  },
  
  // 状态管理
  states: {
    default: 'cursor-default',
    hover: 'cursor-pointer transform hover:scale-105',
    active: 'transform scale-95',
    disabled: 'opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed',
    loading: 'cursor-wait animate-pulse',
    focus: 'ring-2 ring-lelabo-primary ring-offset-2',
  },
};

// 交互钩子函数
export const useInteraction = () => {
  const [isHovered, setIsHovered] = useState(false);
  const [isPressed, setIsPressed] = useState(false);
  const [isFocused, setIsFocused] = useState(false);
  
  const handlers = {
    onMouseEnter: () => setIsHovered(true),
    onMouseLeave: () => setIsHovered(false),
    onMouseDown: () => setIsPressed(true),
    onMouseUp: () => setIsPressed(false),
    onFocus: () => setIsFocused(true),
    onBlur: () => setIsFocused(false),
    onTouchStart: () => setIsPressed(true),
    onTouchEnd: () => setIsPressed(false),
  };
  
  const getStateClasses = () => {
    let classes = InteractionStandards.states.default;
    
    if (isFocused) classes += ` ${InteractionStandards.states.focus}`;
    if (isHovered) classes += ` ${InteractionStandards.states.hover}`;
    if (isPressed) classes += ` ${InteractionStandards.states.active}`;
    
    return classes;
  };
  
  return {
    handlers,
    state: { isHovered, isPressed, isFocused },
    classes: getStateClasses(),
  };
};
```

const PerformanceStandards = {
  // Core Web Vitals 目标
  coreWebVitals: {
    LCP: '2.5s',           // Largest Contentful Paint
    FID: '100ms',          // First Input Delay

    FCP: '1.8s',           // First Contentful Paint
    TTFB: '800ms',         // Time to First Byte
  },
  
  // 图片优化
  images: {
    formats: ['webp', 'avif', 'jpg'], // 优先格式
    lazy: true,                       // 默认懒加载
    placeholder: 'blur',              // 模糊占位符
    sizes: {
      thumbnail: '150x150',
      small: '300x300', 
      medium: '600x600',
      large: '1200x1200',
      hero: '1920x1080',
    },
    quality: {
      thumbnail: 75,
      product: 85,
      hero: 90,
    },
  },
  
  // 代码分割
  codeSplitting: {
    route: true,              // 路由级分割
    component: true,          // 组件级分割
    vendor: true,             // 第三方库分割
    chunkSize: '244kb',       // 建议chunk大小
  },
  
  // 缓存策略
  caching: {
    static: '1y',             // 静态资源
    api: '5m',                // API响应
    page: '1h',               // 页面缓存
    image: '1y',              // 图片缓存
  },
  
  // 预加载策略
  preloading: {
    critical: ['fonts', 'hero-images'],
    prefetch: ['next-page', 'likely-products'],
    preconnect: ['api-domain', 'cdn-domain'],
  },
};

// 性能监控钩子
export const usePerformanceMonitor = () => {
  useEffect(() => {
    // 监控Core Web Vitals
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && 'performance' in window) {
      // LCP监控
      new PerformanceObserver((list) => {
        const entries = list.getEntries();
        const lastEntry = entries[entries.length - 1];
        console.log('LCP:', lastEntry.startTime);
      }).observe({ entryTypes: ['largest-contentful-paint'] });
      
      // FID监控  
      new PerformanceObserver((list) => {
        const entries = list.getEntries();
        entries.forEach((entry) => {
          console.log('FID:', entry.processingStart - entry.startTime);
        });
      }).observe({ entryTypes: ['first-input'] });
      
      // CLS监控
      new PerformanceObserver((list) => {
        let clsValue = 0;
        const entries = list.getEntries();
        entries.forEach((entry) => {
          if (!entry.hadRecentInput) {
            clsValue += entry.value;
          }
        });
        console.log('CLS:', clsValue);
      }).observe({ entryTypes: ['layout-shift'] });
    }
  }, []);
};

### ♿ **无障碍设计规范**
```typescript
// 无障碍设计标准 (WCAG 2.1 AA)
const AccessibilityStandards = {
  // 颜色对比度
  colorContrast: {
    normalText: '4.5:1',      // 普通文字最小对比度
    largeText: '3:1',         // 大文字最小对比度
    nonText: '3:1',           // 非文字元素
    enhanced: '7:1',          // AAA级标准
  },
  
  // 文字大小
  textSize: {
    minimum: '16px',          // 最小字体大小
    comfortable: '18px',      // 舒适阅读大小
    lineHeight: '1.5',        // 最小行高
    letterSpacing: '0.03em',  // 字符间距
  },
  
  // 交互元素
  interactive: {
    minTarget: '44px',        // 最小触摸目标
    spacing: '8px',           // 元素间距
    focusRing: '2px',         // 焦点环宽度
    timeout: '20s',           // 最小超时时间
  },
  
  // 键盘导航
  keyboard: {
    tabOrder: 'logical',      // 逻辑Tab顺序
    skipLinks: true,          // 跳过链接
    focusManagement: true,    // 焦点管理
    shortcuts: {
      search: 'Ctrl+K',
      menu: 'Alt+M',
      cart: 'Alt+C',
      account: 'Alt+A',
    },
  },
  
  // 屏幕阅读器
  screenReader: {
    labels: 'required',       // 必须标签
    descriptions: 'helpful',  // 有用描述
    landmarks: 'semantic',    // 语义化标记
    liveRegions: 'dynamic',   // 动态区域
  },
};

// 无障碍钩子函数
export const useAccessibility = () => {
  const [reducedMotion, setReducedMotion] = useState(false);
  const [highContrast, setHighContrast] = useState(false);
  const [fontSize, setFontSize] = useState('normal');
  
  useEffect(() => {
    // 检测用户偏好
    const mediaQuery = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)');
    setReducedMotion(mediaQuery.matches);
    
    const contrastQuery = window.matchMedia('(prefers-contrast: high)');
    setHighContrast(contrastQuery.matches);
    
    // 监听变化
    mediaQuery.addEventListener('change', (e) => setReducedMotion(e.matches));
    contrastQuery.addEventListener('change', (e) => setHighContrast(e.matches));
    
    return () => {
      mediaQuery.removeEventListener('change', (e) => setReducedMotion(e.matches));
      contrastQuery.removeEventListener('change', (e) => setHighContrast(e.matches));
    };
  }, []);
  
  return {
    reducedMotion,
    highContrast,
    fontSize,
    setFontSize,
  };
};

// 无障碍组件示例
export const AccessibleButton: React.FC<{
  children: React.ReactNode;
  onClick: () => void;
  ariaLabel?: string;
  ariaDescribedBy?: string;
  disabled?: boolean;
}> = ({ children, onClick, ariaLabel, ariaDescribedBy, disabled = false }) => {
  const { reducedMotion } = useAccessibility();
  
  return (
    <button
      onClick={onClick}
      disabled={disabled}
      aria-label={ariaLabel}
      aria-describedby={ariaDescribedBy}
      className={`
        px-4 py-2 rounded-md
        bg-lelabo-primary text-white
        hover:bg-lelabo-primary-dark
        focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-lelabo-primary focus:ring-offset-2
        disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed
        ${reducedMotion ? '' : 'transition-colors duration-200'}
      `}
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
};

// 跳过导航组件
export const SkipToContent: React.FC = () => (
  <a
    href="#main-content"
    className="sr-only focus:not-sr-only focus:absolute focus:top-4 focus:left-4 bg-lelabo-primary text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-md z-50"
  >
    跳转到主要内容
  </a>
);
```

// Le Labo 品牌文案占位符标准
const LeLaboBrandCopyPlaceholders = {

// 文案替换工具函数
export const replaceBrandCopy = (
  template: string, 
  replacements: Record<string, string>
): string => {
  let result = template;
  
  Object.entries(replacements).forEach(([key, value]) => {
    const placeholder = `[${key.toUpperCase()}_PLACEHOLDER: ${key}]`;
    result = result.replace(new RegExp(placeholder, 'g'), value);
  });
  
  return result;
};

// 使用示例
const productDescription = replaceBrandCopy(
  LeLaboBrandCopyPlaceholders.productStories.fragranceStory,
  {
    'FRAGRANCE_STORY': 'SANTAL 33诞生于对美国西部广袤平原的深度感悟，温暖的檀香与烟熏木质调完美融合，创造出令人难忘的嗅觉体验。'
  }
);
```

### 🎯 **品牌文案规范指南**
```typescript
// Le Labo 品牌文案撰写规范
const BrandCopyGuidelines = {
  // === 语调与风格 ===
  tone: {
    style: 'minimal_artistic', // 简约艺术化
    personality: [
      'authentic', // 真实的
      'crafted', // 精心制作的  
      'intimate', // 亲密的
      'sophisticated', // 精致的
      'understated' // 低调的
    ],
    avoidance: [
      'overly commercial', // 避免过度商业化
      'flashy language', // 避免华丽辞藻
      'hard selling', // 避免硬销售
      'generic beauty copy' // 避免通用美妆文案
    ]
  },
  
  // === 关键词库 ===
  vocabulary: {
    core: [
      'craftsmanship', // 工艺
      'authenticity', // 真实性
      'artisanal', // 手工艺的
      'bespoke', // 定制的
      'raw materials', // 原材料
      'fresh made', // 新鲜制作
      'laboratory', // 实验室
      'sensorial' // 感官的
    ],
    emotions: [
      'intimate', // 亲密的
      'sensual', // 感性的
      'contemplative', // 沉思的
      'addictive', // 令人上瘾的
      'comforting', // 令人安慰的
      'intoxicating', // 令人陶醉的
      'transformative', // 转化的
      'transcendent' // 超越的
    ],
    experience: [
      'journey', // 旅程
      'discovery', // 发现
      'ritual', // 仪式
      'moment', // 时刻
      'memory', // 记忆
      'connection', // 连接
      'expression', // 表达
      'identity' // 身份
    ]
  },
  
  // === 文案结构模板 ===
  structure: {
    productStory: {
      opening: '[emotional_hook + sensory_description]',
      development: '[ingredient_story + craft_process]', 
      conclusion: '[usage_inspiration + brand_connection]',
      length: '150-300 characters',
      lengthZh: '100-200字符'
    },
    collectionIntro: {
      concept: '[collection_philosophy + design_vision]',
      products: '[representative_products + unique_features]',
      invitation: '[user_invitation + discovery_journey]',
      length: '200-400 characters',
      lengthZh: '150-300字符'
    },
    serviceDescription: {
      value: '[service_unique_value + user_benefit]',
      process: '[service_process + quality_assurance]',
      outcome: '[expected_result + emotional_reward]',
      length: '100-200 characters', 
      lengthZh: '80-150字符'
    }
  }
};
```

这个真实品牌内容规范完全基于Le Labo的官方产品线、服务特色和品牌价值观，为后续的具体实现提供了准确的内容框架和文案占位符系统，确保所有实现都符合品牌的真实特征。

# 🎨 **视觉与排版规范** - Le Labo 设计系统标准

## 📱 视觉设计原则

基于Le Labo品牌美学和小程序用户体验最佳实践：

### 🌬️ **高留白设计系统**
```css
/* Le Labo 高留白设计标准 */
:root {
  /* === 留白层级系统 === */
  /* 微观留白 - 组件内部间距 */
  --spacing-micro-xs: 2px;    /* 极小间距 */
  --spacing-micro-sm: 4px;    /* 小间距 */
  --spacing-micro-md: 8px;    /* 中等间距 */
  --spacing-micro-lg: 12px;   /* 大间距 */
  
  /* 局部留白 - 组件之间间距 */
  --spacing-local-xs: 16px;   /* 相关元素间距 */
  --spacing-local-sm: 24px;   /* 组件间距 */
  --spacing-local-md: 32px;   /* 区块间距 */
  --spacing-local-lg: 48px;   /* 大区块间距 */
  
  /* 宏观留白 - 页面级间距 */
  --spacing-macro-sm: 64px;   /* 页面区域间距 */
  --spacing-macro-md: 96px;   /* 大页面区域间距 */
  --spacing-macro-lg: 128px;  /* 页面顶部/底部 */
  --spacing-macro-xl: 160px;  /* 特大区域间距 */
  
  /* === 容器内边距系统 === */
  --container-padding-mobile: 16px;    /* 移动端容器内边距 */
  --container-padding-tablet: 24px;    /* 平板端容器内边距 */
  --container-padding-desktop: 32px;   /* 桌面端容器内边距 */
  
  /* === 响应式留白缩放 === */
  --spacing-scale-mobile: 0.75;        /* 移动端留白缩放 */
  --spacing-scale-tablet: 1;           /* 平板端留白标准 */
  --spacing-scale-desktop: 1.25;       /* 桌面端留白放大 */
}

/* 留白工具类 */
.spacing-micro { margin: var(--spacing-micro-md); }
.spacing-local { margin: var(--spacing-local-md); }
.spacing-macro { margin: var(--spacing-macro-md); }

.padding-container {
  padding-left: var(--container-padding-mobile);
  padding-right: var(--container-padding-mobile);
}

@media (min-width: 768px) {
  .padding-container {
    padding-left: var(--container-padding-tablet);
    padding-right: var(--container-padding-tablet);
  }
}

@media (min-width: 1024px) {
  .padding-container {
    padding-left: var(--container-padding-desktop);
    padding-right: var(--container-padding-desktop);
  }
}

/* 响应式留白调整 */
@media (max-width: 640px) {
  :root {
    --spacing-local-sm: calc(24px * var(--spacing-scale-mobile));
    --spacing-local-md: calc(32px * var(--spacing-scale-mobile));
    --spacing-macro-sm: calc(64px * var(--spacing-scale-mobile));
  }
}

@media (min-width: 1024px) {
  :root {
    --spacing-local-sm: calc(24px * var(--spacing-scale-desktop));
    --spacing-local-md: calc(32px * var(--spacing-scale-desktop));
    --spacing-macro-sm: calc(64px * var(--spacing-scale-desktop));
  }
}
```

### 📊 **信息层级系统**
```css
/* Le Labo 信息层级设计 */
:root {
  /* === 标题层级 === */
  --heading-h1: 3rem;         /* 48px - 页面主标题 */
  --heading-h1-mobile: 2.25rem; /* 36px - 移动端主标题 */
  --heading-h2: 2.25rem;      /* 36px - 区域标题 */
  --heading-h2-mobile: 1.875rem; /* 30px - 移动端区域标题 */
  --heading-h3: 1.875rem;     /* 30px - 子区域标题 */
  --heading-h3-mobile: 1.5rem; /* 24px - 移动端子标题 */
  --heading-h4: 1.5rem;       /* 24px - 组件标题 */
  --heading-h4-mobile: 1.25rem; /* 20px - 移动端组件标题 */
  --heading-h5: 1.25rem;      /* 20px - 小标题 */
  --heading-h6: 1.125rem;     /* 18px - 最小标题 */
  
  /* === 正文层级 === */
  --text-lead: 1.25rem;       /* 20px - 引导文字 */
  --text-body: 1rem;          /* 16px - 正文 */
  --text-small: 0.875rem;     /* 14px - 小字 */
  --text-caption: 0.75rem;    /* 12px - 说明文字 */
  
  /* === 行高层级 === */
  --line-height-tight: 1.25;  /* 紧密行高 - 标题 */
  --line-height-normal: 1.5;  /* 标准行高 - 正文 */
  --line-height-relaxed: 1.75; /* 宽松行高 - 长文本 */
  
  /* === 字重层级 === */
  --font-weight-light: 300;   /* 细体 - 装饰文字 */
  --font-weight-normal: 400;  /* 常规 - 正文 */
  --font-weight-medium: 500;  /* 中等 - 强调 */
  --font-weight-semibold: 600; /* 半粗 - 小标题 */
  --font-weight-bold: 700;    /* 粗体 - 大标题 */
}

/* 层级样式类 */
.text-hierarchy-1 {
  font-size: var(--heading-h1);
  font-weight: var(--font-weight-light);
  line-height: var(--line-height-tight);
  letter-spacing: -0.025em;
  color: var(--lelabo-text-primary);
}

.text-hierarchy-2 {
  font-size: var(--heading-h2);
  font-weight: var(--font-weight-normal);
  line-height: var(--line-height-tight);
  letter-spacing: -0.015em;
  color: var(--lelabo-text-primary);
}

.text-hierarchy-3 {
  font-size: var(--heading-h3);
  font-weight: var(--font-weight-medium);
  line-height: var(--line-height-normal);
  color: var(--lelabo-text-primary);
}

.text-body-primary {
  font-size: var(--text-body);
  font-weight: var(--font-weight-normal);
  line-height: var(--line-height-normal);
  color: var(--lelabo-text-primary);
}

.text-body-secondary {
  font-size: var(--text-body);
  font-weight: var(--font-weight-normal);
  line-height: var(--line-height-normal);
  color: var(--lelabo-text-secondary);
}

.text-caption {
  font-size: var(--text-caption);
  font-weight: var(--font-weight-normal);
  line-height: var(--line-height-normal);
  color: var(--lelabo-text-muted);
}

/* 移动端层级调整 */
@media (max-width: 640px) {
  .text-hierarchy-1 { font-size: var(--heading-h1-mobile); }
  .text-hierarchy-2 { font-size: var(--heading-h2-mobile); }
  .text-hierarchy-3 { font-size: var(--heading-h3-mobile); }
  .text-hierarchy-4 { font-size: var(--heading-h4-mobile); }
}

### 📱 **移动端优先设计**
```typescript
// Mobile First 设计组件
const MobileFirstComponents = {
  // === 触摸友好的交互元素 ===
  TouchOptimized: {
    // 最小触摸面积 44x44px
    minTouchTarget: '44px',
    recommendedTouchTarget: '48px',
    
    // 触摸间距
    touchSpacing: '8px',
    comfortableSpacing: '12px',
    
    // 手势识别区域
    gestureThreshold: {
      tap: '300ms',
      longPress: '500ms', 
      swipe: '50px',
      pinch: '1.1x'
    }
  },
  
  // === 移动端布局模式 ===
  LayoutPatterns: {
    // 卡片布局
    cardLayout: {
      mobile: 'grid-cols-1', // 单列
      tablet: 'grid-cols-2', // 双列  
      desktop: 'grid-cols-3' // 三列
    },
    
    // 产品网格
    productGrid: {
      mobile: 'grid-cols-2',     // 2列产品
      tablet: 'grid-cols-3',     // 3列产品
      desktop: 'grid-cols-4',    // 4列产品
      wide: 'grid-cols-5'        // 5列产品
    },
    
    // 内容布局
    contentLayout: {
      mobile: 'flex-col',        // 垂直堆叠
      tablet: 'flex-col',        // 垂直堆叠
      desktop: 'flex-row'        // 水平排列
    }
  },
  
  // === 文字排版适配 ===
  TypographyAdaptation: {
    // 标题缩放比例
    headingScale: {
      mobile: 0.8,      // 移动端标题缩小
      tablet: 0.9,      // 平板端标题稍小
      desktop: 1.0      // 桌面端标题标准
    },
    
    // 行距调整
    lineHeightAdjustment: {
      mobile: '+0.1',   // 移动端行距增加
      tablet: '0',      // 平板端行距标准  
      desktop: '0'      // 桌面端行距标准
    },
    
    // 段落间距
    paragraphSpacing: {
      mobile: '16px',   // 移动端段落间距
      tablet: '20px',   // 平板端段落间距
      desktop: '24px'   // 桌面端段落间距
    }
  }
};

// 移动优先响应式组件示例
export const ResponsiveContainer: React.FC<{
  children: React.ReactNode;
  className?: string;
}> = ({ children, className = '' }) => {
  return (
    <div className={`
      /* 移动端优先 */
      px-4 py-6
      /* 平板端适配 */
      md:px-6 md:py-8
      /* 桌面端增强 */
      lg:px-8 lg:py-12
      /* 宽屏优化 */
      xl:px-12 xl:py-16
      /* 自定义样式 */
      ${className}
    `}>
      {children}
    </div>
  );
};

// 移动端导航组件
export const MobileNavigation: React.FC = () => {
  return (
    <nav className="
      fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 z-50
      bg-white border-t border-lelabo-border
      px-2 py-1
      safe-area-inset-bottom
    ">
      <div className="flex justify-around items-center">
        {/* 导航项目，每个44px高度保证触摸友好 */}
        <NavItem icon="home" label="发现" href="/" />
        <NavItem icon="search" label="寻觅" href="/scent-finder" />
        <NavItem icon="heart" label="收藏" href="/wishlist" />
        <NavItem icon="shopping-bag" label="购物车" href="/cart" />
        <NavItem icon="user" label="我的" href="/account" />
      </div>
    </nav>
  );
};
```

### 🗂️ **空态设计系统**
```tsx
// Le Labo 空态组件设计
export const LeLaboEmptyStates = {
  // === 购物车为空 ===
  EmptyCart: () => (

        购物车空空如也
      </h3>

        [EMPTY_CART_MESSAGE: 探索Le Labo的精致香水世界，发现属于你的独特香调]
      </p>

        >
          开始探索
        </LeLaboButton>

          size="md" 
          className="w-full"

        </LeLaboButton>
      </div>

        未找到 "{searchTerm}" 相关商品
      </h3>
      <p className="text-body-secondary mb-6 max-w-sm">
        [NO_SEARCH_RESULTS_MESSAGE: 尝试其他关键词或浏览我们的精选系列]
      </p>
      
      {/* 搜索建议 */}
      <div className="w-full max-w-sm space-y-3">
        <div className="text-sm text-lelabo-text-secondary">搜索建议:</div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <SearchSuggestionTag term="SANTAL 33" />
          <SearchSuggestionTag term="木质调" />
          <SearchSuggestionTag term="经典系列" />
          <SearchSuggestionTag term="身体护理" />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  ),
  
  // === 订单为空 ===
  EmptyOrders: () => (
    <div className="
      flex flex-col items-center justify-center
      px-8 py-16 text-center
      min-h-[50vh]
    ">
      <div className="
        w-24 h-24 mb-6
        bg-lelabo-bg-secondary rounded-full
        flex items-center justify-center
      ">
        <DocumentTextIcon className="w-12 h-12 text-lelabo-text-muted" />
      </div>
      
      <h3 className="text-hierarchy-3 mb-2">
        暂无订单记录
      </h3>
      <p className="text-body-secondary mb-8 max-w-sm">
        [EMPTY_ORDERS_MESSAGE: 开始你的Le Labo香氛之旅，创造第一个美好订单]
      </p>
      
      <LeLaboButton 
        variant="primary" 
        size="lg"
        onClick={() => router.push('/')}
      >
        开始购物
      </LeLaboButton>
    </div>
  ),
  
  // === 网络错误 ===
  NetworkError: ({ onRetry }: { onRetry: () => void }) => (
    <div className="
      flex flex-col items-center justify-center
      px-8 py-12 text-center
    ">
      <div className="
        w-20 h-20 mb-4
        bg-lelabo-bg-secondary rounded-full
        flex items-center justify-center
      ">
        <WifiXMarkIcon className="w-10 h-10 text-lelabo-text-muted" />
      </div>
      
      <h3 className="text-hierarchy-4 mb-2">
        网络连接异常
      </h3>
      <p className="text-body-secondary mb-6 max-w-sm">
        [NETWORK_ERROR_MESSAGE: 请检查网络连接，然后重试]
      </p>
      
      <LeLaboButton 
        variant="primary" 
        size="md"
        onClick={onRetry}
      >
        重新加载
      </LeLaboButton>
    </div>
  )
};

// 通用空态容器组件
export const EmptyStateContainer: React.FC<{
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  actions?: React.ReactNode;
  recommendations?: React.ReactNode;
}> = ({ icon, title, description, actions, recommendations }) => {
  return (
    <div className="
      flex flex-col items-center justify-center
      px-8 py-16 text-center
      min-h-[60vh]
    ">
      {/* 图标容器 */}
      <div className="
        w-24 h-24 mb-6
        bg-lelabo-bg-secondary rounded-full
        flex items-center justify-center
      ">
        {icon}
      </div>
      
      {/* 文字内容 */}
      <h3 className="text-hierarchy-3 mb-2">{title}</h3>
      <p className="text-body-secondary mb-8 max-w-sm">{description}</p>
      
      {/* 操作按钮 */}
      {actions && (
        <div className="space-y-3 w-full max-w-xs mb-8">
          {actions}
        </div>
      )}
      
      {/* 推荐内容 */}
      {recommendations && (
        <div className="w-full max-w-md">
          {recommendations}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};
```

```tsx
// Le Labo 错误提示组件系统
export const LeLaboErrorStates = {
  // === 页面级错误 ===
  PageError: ({ 
    title = '页面出现问题',
    message = '[PAGE_ERROR_MESSAGE: 页面暂时无法访问，请稍后重试]',
    onRetry 
  }: {
    title?: string;
    message?: string;
    onRetry?: () => void;
  }) => (
    <div className="
      min-h-screen flex flex-col items-center justify-center
      px-8 py-16 text-center bg-lelabo-bg-primary
    ">
      {/* 错误图标 */}
      <div className="
        w-24 h-24 mb-6
        bg-red-50 rounded-full
        flex items-center justify-center
      ">
        <ExclamationTriangleIcon className="w-12 h-12 text-red-400" />
      </div>
      
      {/* 错误信息 */}
      <h1 className="text-hierarchy-2 mb-2 text-lelabo-text-primary">
        {title}
      </h1>
      <p className="text-body-secondary mb-8 max-w-md">
        {message}
      </p>
      
      {/* 操作按钮 */}
      <div className="space-y-3 w-full max-w-xs">
        {onRetry && (
          <LeLaboButton 
            variant="primary" 
            size="lg" 
            className="w-full"
            onClick={onRetry}
          >
            重新加载
          </LeLaboButton>
        )}
        <LeLaboButton 
          variant="secondary" 
          size="md" 
          className="w-full"
          onClick={() => router.push('/')}
        >
          返回首页
        </LeLaboButton>
        <button 
          className="text-sm text-lelabo-text-muted underline"
          onClick={() => window.location.reload()}
        >
          刷新页面
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  ),
  
  // === 组件级错误 ===
  ComponentError: ({ 
    message = '[COMPONENT_ERROR_MESSAGE: 内容加载失败]',
    onRetry,
    compact = false 
  }: {
    message?: string;
    onRetry?: () => void;
    compact?: boolean;
  }) => (
    <div className={`
      flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center
      ${compact ? 'py-8' : 'py-12'}
    `}>
      <ExclamationCircleIcon className={`
        text-red-400 mb-3
        ${compact ? 'w-8 h-8' : 'w-12 h-12'}
      `} />
      
      <p className={`
        text-lelabo-text-secondary mb-4
        ${compact ? 'text-sm' : 'text-base'}
      `}>
        {message}
      </p>
      
      {onRetry && (
        <LeLaboButton 
          variant="secondary" 
          size={compact ? 'sm' : 'md'}
          onClick={onRetry}
        >
          重试
        </LeLaboButton>
      )}
    </div>
  ),
  
  // === 产品缺货错误 ===
  OutOfStock: ({ product }: { product: string }) => (
    <div className="
      bg-orange-50 border border-orange-200 rounded-lg
      p-4 text-center
    ">
      <ClockIcon className="w-8 h-8 text-orange-400 mx-auto mb-2" />
      <h4 className="font-medium text-orange-800 mb-1">
        {product} 暂时缺货
      </h4>
      <p className="text-sm text-orange-600 mb-3">
        [OUT_OF_STOCK_MESSAGE: 商品正在补货中，您可以设置到货提醒]
      </p>
      
      <div className="space-y-2">
        <LeLaboButton 
          variant="secondary" 
          size="sm"
          className="w-full"
        >
          到货提醒
        </LeLaboButton>
        <button className="text-xs text-orange-600 underline">
          查看相似商品
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  ),
  
  // === 支付失败错误 ===
  PaymentError: ({ 
    errorCode,
    onRetry 
  }: { 
    errorCode?: string;
    onRetry: () => void;
  }) => (
    <div className="
      bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg
      p-6 text-center
    ">
      <XCircleIcon className="w-12 h-12 text-red-400 mx-auto mb-3" />
      <h3 className="text-lg font-medium text-red-800 mb-2">
        支付失败
      </h3>
      <p className="text-sm text-red-600 mb-4">
        [PAYMENT_ERROR_MESSAGE: 支付过程中出现问题，请重试或更换支付方式]
      </p>
      
      {errorCode && (
        <p className="text-xs text-red-500 mb-4 font-mono">
          错误代码: {errorCode}
        </p>
      )}
      
      <div className="space-y-2">
        <LeLaboButton 
          variant="primary" 
          size="md"
          className="w-full"
          onClick={onRetry}
        >
          重新支付
        </LeLaboButton>

          更换支付方式
        </button>
        <button className="text-xs text-red-500 underline">
          联系客服
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
};

### 📝 **表单就地报错系统**
```tsx
// Le Labo 表单验证与错误提示
export const LeLaboFormValidation = {
  // === 实时验证组件 ===
  ValidatedInput: React.forwardRef<HTMLInputElement, {
    label: string;
    type?: string;
    placeholder?: string;
    value: string;
    onChange: (value: string) => void;
    validation?: (value: string) => ValidationResult;
    required?: boolean;
    className?: string;
  }>(({ 
    label, 
    type = 'text', 
    placeholder, 
    value, 
    onChange, 
    validation,
    required = false,
    className = ''
  }, ref) => {
    const [error, setError] = useState<string>('');
    const [touched, setTouched] = useState(false);
    const [isValid, setIsValid] = useState(true);
    
    // 实时验证
    useEffect(() => {
      if (touched && validation) {
        const result = validation(value);
        setError(result.error || '');
        setIsValid(result.isValid);
      }
    }, [value, touched, validation]);
    
    const handleBlur = () => {
      setTouched(true);
      if (validation) {
        const result = validation(value);
        setError(result.error || '');
        setIsValid(result.isValid);
      }
    };
    
    const getInputStyles = () => {
      let styles = `
        w-full px-4 py-3 rounded-lg border transition-colors duration-200
        text-lelabo-text-primary placeholder-lelabo-text-muted
        focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2
      `;
      
      if (touched) {
        if (isValid) {
          styles += ' border-lelabo-success focus:border-lelabo-success focus:ring-lelabo-success';
        } else {
          styles += ' border-lelabo-error focus:border-lelabo-error focus:ring-lelabo-error';
        }
      } else {
        styles += ' border-lelabo-border focus:border-lelabo-primary focus:ring-lelabo-primary';
      }
      
      return styles;
    };
    
    return (
      <div className={`space-y-1 ${className}`}>
        {/* 标签 */}
        <label className="
          block text-sm font-medium text-lelabo-text-primary
          mb-1
        ">
          {label}
          {required && <span className="text-lelabo-error ml-1">*</span>}
        </label>
        
        {/* 输入框容器 */}
        <div className="relative">
          <input
            ref={ref}
            type={type}
            placeholder={placeholder}
            value={value}
            onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
            onBlur={handleBlur}
            className={getInputStyles()}
            aria-invalid={touched && !isValid}
            aria-describedby={error ? `${label}-error` : undefined}
          />
          
          {/* 验证状态图标 */}
          {touched && (
            <div className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 transform -translate-y-1/2">
              {isValid ? (
                <CheckCircleIcon className="w-5 h-5 text-lelabo-success" />
              ) : (
                <XCircleIcon className="w-5 h-5 text-lelabo-error" />
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
        
        {/* 错误提示 */}
        {touched && error && (
          <div 
            id={`${label}-error`}
            className="
              flex items-start space-x-2 text-sm text-lelabo-error
              animate-slideInDown
            "
            role="alert"
          >
            <ExclamationCircleIcon className="w-4 h-4 mt-0.5 flex-shrink-0" />
            <span>{error}</span>
          </div>
        )}
        
        {/* 成功提示 */}
        {touched && isValid && value && (
          <div className="
            flex items-center space-x-2 text-sm text-lelabo-success
            animate-slideInDown
          ">
            <CheckCircleIcon className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>输入正确</span>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  }),
  
  // === 表单验证规则 ===
  ValidationRules: {
    // 个性化标签验证
    labelName: (value: string): ValidationResult => {
      if (!value.trim()) {
        return { isValid: false, error: '请输入标签名称' };
      }
      
      // 检测语言类型
      const isChinese = /[\u4e00-\u9fa5]/.test(value);
      const maxLength = isChinese ? 11 : 22;
      
      if (value.length > maxLength) {
        return { 
          isValid: false, 
          error: `标签名称过长，最多${maxLength}个字符，当前${value.length}个` 
        };
      }
      
      // 字符类型检查
      const allowedPattern = /^[a-zA-Z0-9\s\u4e00-\u9fa5']*$/;
      if (!allowedPattern.test(value)) {
        return { 
          isValid: false, 
          error: '只允许使用字母、数字、空格、中文和单引号' 
        };
      }
      
      // 连续空格检查
      if (/\s{2,}/.test(value)) {
        return { isValid: false, error: '不允许连续空格' };
      }
      
      // 开头结尾空格检查
      if (value !== value.trim()) {
        return { isValid: false, error: '开头和结尾不能有空格' };
      }
      
      return { isValid: true };
    },
    
    // 手机号验证
    phoneNumber: (value: string): ValidationResult => {
      if (!value) {
        return { isValid: false, error: '请输入手机号码' };
      }
      
      const phonePattern = /^1[3-9]\d{9}$/;
      if (!phonePattern.test(value)) {
        return { isValid: false, error: '请输入正确的手机号码' };
      }
      
      return { isValid: true };
    },
    
    // 邮箱验证
    email: (value: string): ValidationResult => {
      if (!value) {
        return { isValid: false, error: '请输入邮箱地址' };
      }
      
      const emailPattern = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;
      if (!emailPattern.test(value)) {
        return { isValid: false, error: '请输入正确的邮箱地址' };
      }
      
      return { isValid: true };
    },
    
    // 密码验证
    password: (value: string): ValidationResult => {
      if (!value) {
        return { isValid: false, error: '请输入密码' };
      }
      
      if (value.length < 8) {
        return { isValid: false, error: '密码长度至少8位' };
      }
      
      if (!/(?=.*[a-z])(?=.*[A-Z])(?=.*\d)/.test(value)) {
        return { 
          isValid: false, 
          error: '密码需包含大小写字母和数字' 
        };
      }
      
      return { isValid: true };
    },
    
    // 收货地址验证
    address: (value: string): ValidationResult => {
      if (!value.trim()) {
        return { isValid: false, error: '请输入详细地址' };
      }
      
      if (value.trim().length < 10) {
        return { 
          isValid: false, 
          error: '地址信息过于简单，请详细填写' 
        };
      }
      
      return { isValid: true };
    }
  },
  
  // === 表单错误摘要 ===
  FormErrorSummary: ({ errors }: { errors: string[] }) => {
    if (errors.length === 0) return null;
    
    return (
      <div className="
        bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg
        p-4 mb-6 animate-slideInDown
      " role="alert">
        <div className="flex items-start space-x-3">
          <ExclamationTriangleIcon className="w-5 h-5 text-red-400 mt-0.5 flex-shrink-0" />
          <div>
            <h4 className="text-sm font-medium text-red-800 mb-2">
              请修正以下错误:
            </h4>
            <ul className="text-sm text-red-700 space-y-1">
              {errors.map((error, index) => (
                <li key={index} className="flex items-start space-x-2">
                  <span className="text-red-400">•</span>
                  <span>{error}</span>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  },
  
  // === 成功状态提示 ===
  SuccessMessage: ({ 
    message,
    autoHide = true,
    duration = 3000 
  }: {
    message: string;
    autoHide?: boolean;
    duration?: number;
  }) => {
    const [visible, setVisible] = useState(true);
    
    useEffect(() => {
      if (autoHide) {
        const timer = setTimeout(() => setVisible(false), duration);
        return () => clearTimeout(timer);
      }
    }, [autoHide, duration]);
    
    if (!visible) return null;
    
    return (
      <div className="
        bg-green-50 border border-green-200 rounded-lg
        p-4 animate-slideInUp
      " role="alert">
        <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
          <CheckCircleIcon className="w-5 h-5 text-green-400 flex-shrink-0" />
          <p className="text-sm text-green-800">{message}</p>
          {!autoHide && (
            <button 
              onClick={() => setVisible(false)}
              className="ml-auto text-green-400 hover:text-green-600"
            >
              <XMarkIcon className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }
};

// 表单错误动画
@keyframes slideInDown {
  from {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(-10px);
  }
  to {
    opacity: 1;
    transform: translateY(0);
  }
}

@keyframes slideInUp {
  from {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(10px);
  }
  to {
    opacity: 1;
    transform: translateY(0);
  }
}

.animate-slideInDown {
  animation: slideInDown 0.3s ease-out;
}

.animate-slideInUp {
  animation: slideInUp 0.3s ease-out;
}

// 类型定义
interface ValidationResult {
  isValid: boolean;
  error?: string;
  warning?: string;
}
```

## 📱 产品可用性状态管理

基于Le Labo真实业务规则和用户体验最佳实践设计：

### 📦 **容量选择控制系统**
```tsx
// 容量选择状态管理
export const CapacitySelectionControl = {
  // === 容量选择状态定义 ===
  SelectionStates: {
    unselected: 'CAPACITY_UNSELECTED',     // 未选择容量
    selected: 'CAPACITY_SELECTED',         // 已选择容量
    unavailable: 'CAPACITY_UNAVAILABLE',   // 容量不可用
    outOfStock: 'CAPACITY_OUT_OF_STOCK'    // 容量缺货
  },
  
  // === 购买按钮控制逻辑 ===
  PurchaseButtonControl: ({
    selectedCapacity,
    stockStatus,
    productAvailability
  }: {
    selectedCapacity: string | null;
    stockStatus: StockStatus;
    productAvailability: ProductAvailability;
  }) => {
    const [buttonState, setButtonState] = useState<ButtonState>('disabled');
    const [buttonText, setButtonText] = useState<string>('');
    const [disabledReason, setDisabledReason] = useState<string>('');
    
    useEffect(() => {
      // 容量未选择状态
      if (!selectedCapacity) {
        setButtonState('disabled');
        setButtonText('请选择容量');
        setDisabledReason('CAPACITY_NOT_SELECTED');
        return;
      }
      
      // 检查库存状态
      const selectedVariant = productAvailability.variants.find(
        v => v.capacity === selectedCapacity
      );
      
      if (!selectedVariant) {
        setButtonState('disabled');
        setButtonText('容量不可用');
        setDisabledReason('CAPACITY_UNAVAILABLE');
        return;
      }
      
      // 库存检查
      if (selectedVariant.inventory <= 0) {
        setButtonState('disabled');
        setButtonText('暂时缺货');
        setDisabledReason('OUT_OF_STOCK');
        return;
      }
      
      // 库存紧张提醒
      if (selectedVariant.inventory <= 3) {
        setButtonState('enabled');
        setButtonText(`立即购买 (仅剩${selectedVariant.inventory}件)`);
        setDisabledReason('');
        return;
      }
      
      // 正常可购买状态
      setButtonState('enabled');
      setButtonText('立即购买');
      setDisabledReason('');
    }, [selectedCapacity, stockStatus, productAvailability]);
    
    return (
      <div className="space-y-3">
        {/* 主购买按钮 */}
        <LeLaboButton
          variant="primary"
          size="lg"
          className={`
            w-full transition-all duration-200
            ${buttonState === 'disabled' 
              ? 'opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed' 
              : 'hover:scale-105 active:scale-95'
            }
          `}
          disabled={buttonState === 'disabled'}
          onClick={() => handlePurchase(selectedCapacity)}
        >
          {buttonText}
        </LeLaboButton>
        
        {/* 禁用原因提示 */}
        {disabledReason && (
          <div className="text-center">
            <DisabledReasonMessage reason={disabledReason} />
          </div>
        )}
        
        {/* 加入购物车按钮 */}
        <LeLaboButton
          variant="secondary"
          size="md"
          className="w-full"
          disabled={buttonState === 'disabled'}
          onClick={() => handleAddToCart(selectedCapacity)}
        >
          {buttonState === 'disabled' ? '请先选择容量' : '加入购物车'}
        </LeLaboButton>
      </div>
    );
  },
  
  // === 容量选择器组件 ===
  CapacitySelector: ({
    product,
    selectedCapacity,
    onCapacityChange
  }: {
    product: Product;
    selectedCapacity: string | null;
    onCapacityChange: (capacity: string) => void;
  }) => {
    return (
      <div className="space-y-4">
        <h4 className="text-hierarchy-4">选择容量</h4>
        
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">
          {product.variants.map((variant) => {
            const isSelected = selectedCapacity === variant.capacity;
            const isAvailable = variant.inventory > 0;
            const isLowStock = variant.inventory <= 3 && variant.inventory > 0;
            
            return (
              <button
                key={variant.id}
                onClick={() => isAvailable && onCapacityChange(variant.capacity)}
                disabled={!isAvailable}
                className={`
                  relative p-4 rounded-lg border-2 transition-all duration-200
                  ${isSelected 
                    ? 'border-lelabo-primary bg-lelabo-primary text-white' 
                    : isAvailable
                    ? 'border-lelabo-border hover:border-lelabo-primary bg-white'
                    : 'border-lelabo-border bg-gray-50 opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed'
                  }
                `}
              >
                {/* 容量信息 */}
                <div className="text-center">
                  <div className={`
                    font-medium text-sm
                    ${isSelected ? 'text-white' : 'text-lelabo-text-primary'}
                  `}>
                    {variant.capacity}
                  </div>
                  <div className={`
                    text-xs mt-1
                    ${isSelected ? 'text-white opacity-90' : 'text-lelabo-text-secondary'}
                  `}>
                    {variant.priceFormatted}
                  </div>
                </div>
                
                {/* 库存状态标识 */}
                {!isAvailable && (
                  <div className="
                    absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center
                    bg-white bg-opacity-90 rounded-lg
                  ">
                    <span className="text-xs text-red-500 font-medium">
                      缺货
                    </span>
                  </div>
                )}
                
                {isLowStock && isAvailable && (
                  <div className="
                    absolute -top-1 -right-1
                    bg-orange-400 text-white text-xs px-1 py-0.5 rounded
                  ">
                    仅剩{variant.inventory}
                  </div>
                )}
                
                {/* 推荐标识 */}
                {variant.recommended && (
                  <div className="
                    absolute -top-1 -left-1
                    bg-lelabo-accent text-white text-xs px-1 py-0.5 rounded
                  ">
                    推荐
                  </div>
                )}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
        
        {/* 容量说明 */}
        <CapacityDescription selectedCapacity={selectedCapacity} />
      </div>
    );
  }
};

// 禁用原因提示组件
const DisabledReasonMessage: React.FC<{ reason: string }> = ({ reason }) => {
  const messages = {
    'CAPACITY_NOT_SELECTED': {
      icon: <InformationCircleIcon className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-400" />,
      text: '请先选择您需要的容量规格',
      bgColor: 'bg-blue-50',
      textColor: 'text-blue-700',
      borderColor: 'border-blue-200'
    },
    'CAPACITY_UNAVAILABLE': {
      icon: <ExclamationTriangleIcon className="w-4 h-4 text-orange-400" />,
      text: '选择的容量规格暂不可用',
      bgColor: 'bg-orange-50',
      textColor: 'text-orange-700', 
      borderColor: 'border-orange-200'
    },
    'OUT_OF_STOCK': {
      icon: <ClockIcon className="w-4 h-4 text-red-400" />,
      text: '选择的容量暂时缺货，可设置到货提醒',
      bgColor: 'bg-red-50',
      textColor: 'text-red-700',
      borderColor: 'border-red-200'
    }
  };
  
  const config = messages[reason as keyof typeof messages];
  if (!config) return null;
  
  return (
    <div className={`
      flex items-center justify-center space-x-2
      px-3 py-2 rounded-md border
      ${config.bgColor} ${config.textColor} ${config.borderColor}
    `}>
      {config.icon}
      <span className="text-sm">{config.text}</span>
    </div>
  );
};
```
