```typescript
}
```

这个精简的数据字段结构专注于前端展示和交互的核心需求，确保数据结构简洁、易于维护，同时满足Le Labo小程序的所有功能要求。

You: 
Assistant:
态保持。
	•	表单校验明确、错误定位到字段；失败可恢复且不?# 🎨 **个性化数据字段** - Le Labo 定制服务数据结构

## 📝 个性化定制字段规范

基于Le Labo真实个性化服务和严格校验规则设计：

### 🏷️ **个性化定制核心字段 (Personalization)**
```typescript
interface Personalization {
  // === 标签名称字段 ===
  label_name: string;            // 个性化标签名称
  
  // === 纪念日期字段 ===  
  label_date?: string;           // 可选日期字段 (YYYY-MM-DD格式或null)
  
  // === 礼品寄语字段 ===
  gift_note?: string;            // 可选礼品寄语 (≤120字符或null)
}

// 详细个性化字段扩展
interface PersonalizationDetailed {
  // === 标签名称 (必填) ===
  label_name: string;
  label_name_validation: {
    is_valid: boolean;           // 是否通过验证
    error_message?: string;      // 错误提示
    character_count: number;     // 当前字符数
    max_characters: number;      // 最大字符数 (动态：英文22/中文11)
    detected_language: 'en' | 'zh' | 'mixed'; // 检测到的语言类型
  };
  
  // === 纪念日期 (可选) ===
  label_date?: string;           // YYYY-MM-DD格式或null
  label_date_validation?: {
    is_valid: boolean;           // 日期格式是否有效
    error_message?: string;      // 日期错误提示
    formatted_display: string;   // 格式化显示 "2024年12月25日" | "25 DEC 2024"
    is_future_date: boolean;     // 是否未来日期
    is_reasonable_date: boolean; // 是否合理日期范围
  };
  
  // === 礼品寄语 (可选) ===
  gift_note?: string;            // 礼品寄语内容或null
  gift_note_validation?: {
    is_valid: boolean;           // 是否通过验证
    error_message?: string;      // 错误提示
    character_count: number;     // 当前字符数
    max_characters: 120;         // 最大字符限制
    line_count: number;          // 行数统计
    max_lines: 6;                // 最大行数限制
    contains_sensitive_words: boolean; // 是否包含敏感词
  };
  
  // === 预览数据 ===
  preview: {
    label_image_url?: string;    // 标签预览图URL
    gift_card_image_url?: string; // 礼品卡预览图URL
    full_product_preview_url?: string; // 完整产品预览图URL
  };
  
  // === 费用信息 ===
  cost: {
    personalization_fee: number; // 个性化费用 180
    gift_packaging_fee?: number; // 礼品包装费 50 (如果有gift_note)
    total_additional_cost: number; // 总附加费用
  };
  
  // === 制作信息 ===
  production: {
    estimated_days: string;      // 预计制作时间 "5-7个工作日"
    priority_available: boolean; // 是否可加急制作
    priority_cost?: number;      // 加急费用
    priority_days?: string;      // 加急制作时间 "2-3个工作日"
  };
}
```

### ✅ **字段校验规则详细定义**
```typescript
// 个性化字段验证规则
export const PersonalizationValidation = {
  // === label_name 校验规则 ===
  labelName: {
    // 字符长度校验
    validateLength: (input: string): ValidationResult => {
      // 检测语言类型
      const hasChineseChars = /[\u4e00-\u9fa5]/.test(input);
      const hasEnglishChars = /[a-zA-Z]/.test(input);
      
      let maxLength: number;
      let language: 'en' | 'zh' | 'mixed';
      
      if (hasChineseChars && hasEnglishChars) {
        // 混合语言：按中文规则 (更严格)
        maxLength = 11;
        language = 'mixed';
      } else if (hasChineseChars) {
        // 纯中文：11个字符
        maxLength = 11;
        language = 'zh';
      } else {
        // 英文/数字：22个字符
        maxLength = 22;
        language = 'en';
      }
      
      const currentLength = input.length;
      
      return {
        is_valid: currentLength > 0 && currentLength <= maxLength,
        error_message: currentLength === 0 
          ? '请输入标签名称'
          : currentLength > maxLength 
          ? `标签名称过长，${language === 'zh' ? '中文' : '英文'}模式最多${maxLength}个字符，当前${currentLength}个`
          : undefined,
        warning_message: currentLength > maxLength * 0.8 
          ? `接近字符限制 ${currentLength}/${maxLength}`
          : undefined,
        meta: {
          character_count: currentLength,
          max_characters: maxLength,
          detected_language: language
        }
      };
    },
    
    // 字符类型校验
    validateCharacters: (input: string): ValidationResult => {
      // 允许字符：字母、数字、空格、中文、单引号(英文所有格)
      const allowedPattern = /^[a-zA-Z0-9\s\u4e00-\u9fa5']*$/;
      
      // 检查不允许的字符
      const disallowedChars = input.match(/[^a-zA-Z0-9\s\u4e00-\u9fa5']/g);
      
      // 检查连续空格
      const hasMultipleSpaces = /\s{2,}/.test(input);
      
      // 检查开头结尾空格
      const hasLeadingTrailingSpaces = input !== input.trim();
      
      if (disallowedChars) {
        return {
          is_valid: false,
          error_message: `包含不允许的字符: ${[...new Set(disallowedChars)].join(', ')}。仅支持字母、数字、空格、中文和单引号。`
        };
      }
      
      if (hasMultipleSpaces) {
        return {
          is_valid: false,
          error_message: '不允许连续空格，请使用单个空格分隔'
        };
      }
      
      if (hasLeadingTrailingSpaces) {
        return {
          is_valid: false,
          error_message: '标签名称开头和结尾不能有空格'
        };
      }
      
      return { is_valid: true };
    },
    
    // 实时输入过滤器
    filterInput: (input: string): string => {
      return input
        // 移除不允许的字符
        .replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9\s\u4e00-\u9fa5']/g, '')
        // 连续空格变单个空格
        .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
        // 移除开头结尾空格
        .trim();
    },
    
    // 推荐模板
    templates: [
      "SARAH'S SANTAL",
      "FOR MY LOVE",
      "BIRTHDAY 2024",
      "MEMORY OF PARIS",
      "LOVE STORY",
      "ANNIVERSARY",
      "GRADUATION DAY",
      "WEDDING GIFT"
    ],
    
    // 中文模板
    templatesZh: [
      "专属香水",
      "生日快乐", 
      "纪念日",
      "爱的礼物",
      "独一无二",
      "美好时光",
      "永恒瞬间",
      "心意满满"
    ]
  },
  
  // === label_date 校验规则 ===
  labelDate: {
    // 日期格式校验
    validateFormat: (dateString: string): ValidationResult => {
      if (!dateString) {
        return { is_valid: true }; // 可选字段，空值有效
      }
      
      // 严格的YYYY-MM-DD格式检查
      const datePattern = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/;
      const match = dateString.match(datePattern);
      
      if (!match) {
        return {
          is_valid: false,
          error_message: '日期格式错误，请使用 YYYY-MM-DD 格式，例如：2024-12-25'
        };
      }
      
      const [, year, month, day] = match;
      const yearNum = parseInt(year);
      const monthNum = parseInt(month);
      const dayNum = parseInt(day);
      
      // 基础范围检查
      if (monthNum < 1 || monthNum > 12) {
        return {
          is_valid: false,
          error_message: '月份无效，请输入 01-12 之间的月份'
        };
      }
      
      if (dayNum < 1 || dayNum > 31) {
        return {
          is_valid: false,
          error_message: '日期无效，请输入 01-31 之间的日期'
        };
      }
      
      // JavaScript Date对象验证
      const date = new Date(yearNum, monthNum - 1, dayNum);
      const isValidDate = date.getFullYear() === yearNum && 
                         date.getMonth() === monthNum - 1 && 
                         date.getDate() === dayNum;
      
      if (!isValidDate) {
        return {
          is_valid: false,
          error_message: '无效的日期，请检查年月日是否正确'
        };
      }
      
      return { is_valid: true };
    },
    
    // 日期范围校验
    validateRange: (dateString: string): ValidationResult => {
      if (!dateString) return { is_valid: true };
      
      const date = new Date(dateString);
      const minDate = new Date('1900-01-01');
      const maxDate = new Date('2099-12-31');
      const today = new Date();
      
      if (date < minDate || date > maxDate) {
        return {
          is_valid: false,
          error_message: '日期超出有效范围，请选择 1900-2099 年之间的日期'
        };
      }
      
      // 未来日期提醒 (不阻止，只提醒)
      if (date > today) {
        return {
          is_valid: true,
          warning_message: '您选择了未来日期，请确认是否正确'
        };
      }
      
      return { is_valid: true };
    },
    
    // 日期快捷选择选项
    quickOptions: [
      {
        label: '今天',
        labelEn: 'Today',
        value: () => new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]
      },
      {
        label: '明天',
        labelEn: 'Tomorrow', 
        value: () => {
          const tomorrow = new Date();
          tomorrow.setDate(tomorrow.getDate() + 1);
          return tomorrow.toISOString().split('T')[0];
        }
      },
      {
        label: '一周后',
        labelEn: 'One Week Later',
        value: () => {
          const nextWeek = new Date();
          nextWeek.setDate(nextWeek.getDate() + 7);
          return nextWeek.toISOString().split('T')[0];
        }
      },
      {
        label: '一个月后',
        labelEn: 'One Month Later',
        value: () => {
          const nextMonth = new Date();
          nextMonth.setMonth(nextMonth.getMonth() + 1);
          return nextMonth.toISOString().split('T')[0];
        }
      }
    ]
  },
  
  // === gift_note 校验规则 ===
  giftNote: {
    // 字符长度校验
    validateLength: (input: string): ValidationResult => {
      if (!input) {
        return { is_valid: true }; // 可选字段，空值有效
      }
      
      const maxLength = 120;
      const currentLength = input.length;
      
      return {
        is_valid: currentLength <= maxLength,
        error_message: currentLength > maxLength 
          ? `寄语过长，最多${maxLength}个字符，当前${currentLength}个字符`
          : undefined,
        warning_message: currentLength > 100 
          ? `接近字符限制 ${currentLength}/${maxLength}`
          : undefined,
        meta: {
          character_count: currentLength,
          max_characters: maxLength,
          remaining_characters: maxLength - currentLength
        }
      };
    },
    
    // 内容安全校验
    validateContent: (input: string): ValidationResult => {
      if (!input) return { is_valid: true };
      
      // 敏感词检测 (简化示例)
      const sensitiveWords = [
        '广告', '推广', '联系方式', '电话', '微信', 'QQ',
        '网址', 'http', 'www', '.com', '.cn'
      ];
      
      const foundSensitiveWords = sensitiveWords.filter(word => 
        input.toLowerCase().includes(word.toLowerCase())
      );
      
      if (foundSensitiveWords.length > 0) {
        return {
          is_valid: false,
          error_message: `寄语包含不当内容: ${foundSensitiveWords.join(', ')}，请修改后重试`
        };
      }
      
      // 检查过度重复字符
      const hasRepeatedChars = /(.)\1{4,}/.test(input); // 连续5个相同字符
      if (hasRepeatedChars) {
        return {
          is_valid: false,
          error_message: '寄语包含过多重复字符，请重新编写'
        };
      }
      
      return { is_valid: true };
    },
    
    // 行数统计与校验
    validateLines: (input: string): ValidationResult => {
      if (!input) return { is_valid: true };
      
      const lines = input.split('\n');
      const maxLines = 6;
      
      return {
        is_valid: lines.length <= maxLines,
        error_message: lines.length > maxLines 
          ? `寄语行数过多，最多${maxLines}行，当前${lines.length}行`
          : undefined,
        meta: {
          line_count: lines.length,
          max_lines: maxLines
        }
      };
    },
    
    // 寄语模板分类
    templates: {
      // 生日祝福
      birthday: [
        "生日快乐！愿这份独特的香水为你带来一整年的美好心情。",
        "在你的特别日子里，希望这个香水能成为美好回忆的一部分。",
        "Happy Birthday! May this fragrance bring you joy every day.",
        "Wishing you a birthday as special as this unique scent."
      ],
      
      // 爱情表达
      love: [
        "每一次闻到这个香味，都会想起我们在一起的美好时光。",
        "这是我为你选择的香水，就像我选择了你一样，独一无二。",
        "Love is in the air, and so is this beautiful fragrance for you.",
        "You are as unique and precious as this scent."
      ],
      
      // 节日祝福
      holiday: [
        "节日快乐！愿这份香水为你的节日增添更多芬芳。",
        "圣诞快乐！这是我精心为你挑选的礼物。",
        "Happy Holidays! Hope this fragrance makes your season bright.",
        "May this special scent bring warmth to your celebrations."
      ],
      
      // 友谊纪念
      friendship: [
        "友谊如香，越久越醇。感谢你一直以来的陪伴。",
        "希望这个香水能时刻提醒你，我们的友谊有多珍贵。",
        "True friendship, like this fragrance, leaves a lasting impression.",
        "For a friend who makes life more beautiful."
      ],
      
      // 毕业纪念
      graduation: [
        "毕业快乐！愿你的未来如这香水般精彩独特。",
        "新的开始，新的香味，新的可能。",
        "Congratulations! Your future is as bright as this fragrance.",
        "New chapter, new adventures, new signature scent."
      ],
      
      // 纪念意义
      memorial: [
        "纪念这个特别的时刻，让香味保存美好回忆。",
        "时光会流逝，但这份香味会永远记住这一刻。",
        "A scent to remember this precious moment forever.",
        "Capturing memories in every drop of this fragrance."
      ]
    }
  }
};

// 实时校验组件示例
export const PersonalizationForm: React.FC<{
  initialData?: Personalization;
  onValidationChange: (isValid: boolean) => void;
  onDataChange: (data: Personalization) => void;
}> = ({ initialData, onValidationChange, onDataChange }) => {
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState<Personalization>({
    label_name: initialData?.label_name || '',
    label_date: initialData?.label_date || undefined,
    gift_note: initialData?.gift_note || undefined
  });
  
  const [validation, setValidation] = useState({
    label_name: { is_valid: false, error_message: '' },
    label_date: { is_valid: true, error_message: '' },
    gift_note: { is_valid: true, error_message: '' }
  });
  
  // 标签名称校验
  const validateLabelName = useCallback((value: string) => {
    const lengthResult = PersonalizationValidation.labelName.validateLength(value);
    const charactersResult = PersonalizationValidation.labelName.validateCharacters(value);
    
    const isValid = lengthResult.is_valid && charactersResult.is_valid;
    const errorMessage = lengthResult.error_message || charactersResult.error_message || '';
    
    setValidation(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      label_name: { is_valid: isValid, error_message: errorMessage }
    }));
    
    return isValid;
  }, []);
  
  // 日期校验
  const validateDate = useCallback((value: string) => {
    if (!value) {
      setValidation(prev => ({
        ...prev,
        label_date: { is_valid: true, error_message: '' }
      }));
      return true;
    }
    
    const formatResult = PersonalizationValidation.labelDate.validateFormat(value);
    const rangeResult = PersonalizationValidation.labelDate.validateRange(value);
    
    const isValid = formatResult.is_valid && rangeResult.is_valid;
    const errorMessage = formatResult.error_message || rangeResult.error_message || '';
    
    setValidation(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      label_date: { is_valid: isValid, error_message: errorMessage }
    }));
    
    return isValid;
  }, []);
  
  // 寄语校验
  const validateGiftNote = useCallback((value: string) => {
    if (!value) {
      setValidation(prev => ({
        ...prev,
        gift_note: { is_valid: true, error_message: '' }
      }));
      return true;
    }
    
    const lengthResult = PersonalizationValidation.giftNote.validateLength(value);
    const contentResult = PersonalizationValidation.giftNote.validateContent(value);
    const linesResult = PersonalizationValidation.giftNote.validateLines(value);
    
    const isValid = lengthResult.is_valid && contentResult.is_valid && linesResult.is_valid;
    const errorMessage = lengthResult.error_message || contentResult.error_message || linesResult.error_message || '';
    
    setValidation(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      gift_note: { is_valid: isValid, error_message: errorMessage }
    }));
    
    return isValid;
  }, []);
  
  // 整体表单校验
  useEffect(() => {
    const isFormValid = validation.label_name.is_valid && 
                       validation.label_date.is_valid && 
                       validation.gift_note.is_valid;
    
    onValidationChange(isFormValid);
    onDataChange(formData);
  }, [formData, validation, onValidationChange, onDataChange]);
  
  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 p-6">
      {/* 标签名称输入 */}
      <div className="space-y-2">
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-lelabo-text-primary">
          标签名称 <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
        </label>
        <input
          type="text"
          value={formData.label_name}
          onChange={(e) => {
            const filteredValue = PersonalizationValidation.labelName.filterInput(e.target.value);
            setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, label_name: filteredValue }));
            validateLabelName(filteredValue);
          }}
          placeholder="例如: SARAH'S SANTAL"
          className={`
            w-full px-4 py-3 rounded-lg border transition-colors
            ${validation.label_name.is_valid 
              ? 'border-green-300 focus:border-green-500' 
              : formData.label_name 
              ? 'border-red-300 focus:border-red-500'
              : 'border-lelabo-border focus:border-lelabo-primary'
            }
          `}
          maxLength={22} // 前端限制最大长度
        />
        
        {/* 字符计数器 */}
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-xs">
          <span className={`
            ${validation.label_name.error_message ? 'text-red-600' : 'text-lelabo-text-muted'}
          `}>
            {validation.label_name.error_message || 
             `${formData.label_name.length}/${/[\u4e00-\u9fa5]/.test(formData.label_name) ? '11' : '22'} 字符`}
          </span>
          
          {formData.label_name && validation.label_name.is_valid && (
            <span className="text-green-600">✓ 格式正确</span>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
      
      {/* 纪念日期输入 */}
      <div className="space-y-2">
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-lelabo-text-primary">
          纪念日期 <span className="text-lelabo-text-muted">(可选)</span>
        </label>
        <input
          type="date"
          value={formData.label_date || ''}
          onChange={(e) => {
            setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, label_date: e.target.value || undefined }));
            validateDate(e.target.value);
          }}
          min="1900-01-01"
          max="2099-12-31"
          className={`
            w-full px-4 py-3 rounded-lg border transition-colors
            ${validation.label_date.is_valid 
              ? 'border-lelabo-border focus:border-lelabo-primary' 
              : 'border-red-300 focus:border-red-500'
            }
          `}
        />
        
        {validation.label_date.error_message && (
          <p className="text-xs text-red-600">
            {validation.label_date.error_message}
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
      
      {/* 礼品寄语输入 */}
      <div className="space-y-2">
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-lelabo-text-primary">
          礼品寄语 <span className="text-lelabo-text-muted">(可选)</span>
        </label>
        <textarea
          value={formData.gift_note || ''}
          onChange={(e) => {
            setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, gift_note: e.target.value || undefined }));
            validateGiftNote(e.target.value);
          }}
          placeholder="为您的礼品添加温馨寄语..."
          rows={4}
          maxLength={120}
          className={`
            w-full px-4 py-3 rounded-lg border transition-colors resize-none
            ${validation.gift_note.is_valid 
              ? 'border-lelabo-border focus:border-lelabo-primary' 
              : 'border-red-300 focus:border-red-500'
            }
          `}
        />
        
        {/* 字符计数器 */}
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-xs">
          <span className={`
            ${validation.gift_note.error_message ? 'text-red-600' : 'text-lelabo-text-muted'}
          `}>
            {validation.gift_note.error_message || 
             `${formData.gift_note?.length || 0}/120 字符`}
          </span>
          
          {formData.gift_note && validation.gift_note.is_valid && (
            <span className="text-green-600">✓ 格式正确</span>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

// 校验结果接口
interface ValidationResult {
  is_valid: boolean;             // 是否通过校验
  error_message?: string;        // 错误提示信息
  warning_message?: string;      // 警告提示信息
  meta?: any;                    // 附加元数据
}
```

### 💾 **个性化数据存储结构**
```typescript
// 个性化数据在不同场景的存储格式
export const PersonalizationStorage = {
  // === 购物车中的个性化数据 ===
  inCart: {
    label_name: string;          // 必填，已通过校验
    label_date?: string;         // 可选，YYYY-MM-DD格式或undefined
    gift_note?: string;          // 可选，≤120字符或undefined
  },
  
  // === 订单中的个性化数据 ===
  inOrder: {
    label_name: string;          // 订单确认时的标签名称
    label_date?: string;         // 订单确认时的日期
    gift_note?: string;          // 订单确认时的寄语
    preview_image_url?: string;  // 生成的预览图URL
    production_status: 'pending' | 'in_progress' | 'completed'; // 制作状态
  },
  
  // === 用户草稿数据 ===
  draft: {
    label_name: string;          // 当前输入内容 (可能未完成)
    label_date?: string;         // 当前选择日期
    gift_note?: string;          // 当前寄语内容
    validation_status: {
      label_name_valid: boolean;
      label_date_valid: boolean;
      gift_note_valid: boolean;
      overall_valid: boolean;
    };
    last_saved: string;          // 最后保存时间
  }
};
```

这个个性化数据字段结构严格按照用户需求的校验规则设计，确保标签名称、日期和礼品寄语的准确性和安全性，为Le Labo的个性化定制服务提供完整的数据支持。

# 🔍 **列表筛选状态** - Le Labo 产品筛选数据结构

## 📊 筛选状态字段定义

基于Le Labo产品分类和用户筛选需求的精简筛选状态：

### 🎛️ **筛选状态核心字段 (FilterState)**
```typescript
interface FilterState {
  // === 系列筛选 ===
  collection: string[];          // 已选系列数组 ["classic", "city-exclusive", "body"]
  
  // === 香调筛选 ===
  note_family: string[];         // 已选香调数组 ["woody", "floral", "citrus"]
  
  // === 容量筛选 ===
  size: string[];                // 已选容量数组 ["15ml", "50ml", "100ml"]
  
  // === 价格区间筛选 ===
  price_range: [number, number]; // 价格区间 [最低价, 最高价] [520, 2380]
  
  // === 城市限定筛选 ===
  city_exclusive: boolean;       // 是否只显示城市限定商品
}

// 筛选选项配置
interface FilterOptions {
  // === 系列选项配置 ===
  collection_options: Array<{
    value: string;               // "classic"
    label: string;               // "CLASSIC COLLECTION"
    label_zh: string;            // "经典系列"
    count: number;               // 该系列商品数量 13
    description: string;         // "[COLLECTION_DESC_PLACEHOLDER]"
  }>;
  
  // === 香调选项配置 ===
  note_family_options: Array<{
    value: string;               // "woody"
    label: string;               // "Woody"
    label_zh: string;            // "木质调"
    color: string;               // 香调代表色 "#8B4513"
    count: number;               // 该香调商品数量 8
    representative_products: string[]; // 代表产品 ["SANTAL 33", "THÉ NOIR 29"]
  }>;
  
  // === 容量选项配置 ===
  size_options: Array<{
    value: string;               // "50ml"
    label: string;               // "50ml Standard"
    label_zh: string;            // "50ml 标准装"
    type: 'travel' | 'standard' | 'large'; // 容量类型
    count: number;               // 该容量商品数量 23
    price_range: string;         // 价格区间 "¥1,180-¥1,280"
  }>;
  
  // === 价格区间配置 ===
  price_range_config: {
    min: number;                 // 最低价格 520
    max: number;                 // 最高价格 2380
    step: number;                // 价格步进 10
    currency: string;            // "CNY"
    quick_ranges: Array<{        // 快捷价格区间
      label: string;             // "¥500-800"
      label_zh: string;          // "便携装价位"
      range: [number, number];   // [500, 800]
    }>;
  };
  
  // === 城市限定配置 ===
  city_exclusive_config: {
    available: boolean;          // 是否有城市限定商品
    count: number;               // 城市限定商品总数 8
    current_season: boolean;     // 当前是否销售季 (8-9月)
    next_season_date?: string;   // 下次销售时间 "2025-08-01"
  };
}
```

### 🎯 **筛选状态管理逻辑**
```typescript
// 筛选状态管理钩子
export const useFilterState = (initialFilters?: Partial<FilterState>) => {
  const [filters, setFilters] = useState<FilterState>({
    collection: initialFilters?.collection || [],
    note_family: initialFilters?.note_family || [],
    size: initialFilters?.size || [],
    price_range: initialFilters?.price_range || [520, 2380], // 默认全价格范围
    city_exclusive: initialFilters?.city_exclusive || false
  });
  
  // === 筛选操作方法 ===
  const filterActions = {
    // 系列筛选切换
    toggleCollection: (collectionId: string) => {
      setFilters(prev => ({
        ...prev,
        collection: prev.collection.includes(collectionId)
          ? prev.collection.filter(id => id !== collectionId)  // 移除
          : [...prev.collection, collectionId]                 // 添加
      }));
    },
    
    // 香调筛选切换
    toggleNoteFamily: (familyId: string) => {
      setFilters(prev => ({
        ...prev,
        note_family: prev.note_family.includes(familyId)
          ? prev.note_family.filter(id => id !== familyId)     // 移除
          : [...prev.note_family, familyId]                    // 添加
      }));
    },
    
    // 容量筛选切换
    toggleSize: (sizeValue: string) => {
      setFilters(prev => ({
        ...prev,
        size: prev.size.includes(sizeValue)
          ? prev.size.filter(s => s !== sizeValue)             // 移除
          : [...prev.size, sizeValue]                          // 添加
      }));
    },
    
    // 价格区间设置
    setPriceRange: (min: number, max: number) => {
      setFilters(prev => ({
        ...prev,
        price_range: [min, max]
      }));
    },
    
    // 城市限定切换
    toggleCityExclusive: () => {
      setFilters(prev => ({
        ...prev,
        city_exclusive: !prev.city_exclusive
      }));
    },
    
    // 重置所有筛选
    resetFilters: () => {
      setFilters({
        collection: [],
        note_family: [],
        size: [],
        price_range: [520, 2380],
        city_exclusive: false
      });
    },
    
    // 重置单个筛选类型
    resetFilterType: (type: keyof FilterState) => {
      switch (type) {
        case 'collection':
          setFilters(prev => ({ ...prev, collection: [] }));
          break;
        case 'note_family':
          setFilters(prev => ({ ...prev, note_family: [] }));
          break;
        case 'size':
          setFilters(prev => ({ ...prev, size: [] }));
          break;
        case 'price_range':
          setFilters(prev => ({ ...prev, price_range: [520, 2380] }));
          break;
        case 'city_exclusive':
          setFilters(prev => ({ ...prev, city_exclusive: false }));
          break;
      }
    }
  };
  
  // === 筛选状态计算 ===
  const filterStatus = {
    // 是否有任何筛选条件
    hasActiveFilters: 
      filters.collection.length > 0 || 
      filters.note_family.length > 0 || 
      filters.size.length > 0 || 
      filters.price_range[0] > 520 || 
      filters.price_range[1] < 2380 || 
      filters.city_exclusive,
    
    // 筛选条件数量统计
    activeFilterCount: 
      filters.collection.length + 
      filters.note_family.length + 
      filters.size.length + 
      (filters.price_range[0] > 520 || filters.price_range[1] < 2380 ? 1 : 0) +
      (filters.city_exclusive ? 1 : 0),
    
    // 筛选标签数组 (用于显示已选择的筛选条件)
    activeFilterTags: [
      ...filters.collection.map(id => ({ type: 'collection', id, removable: true })),
      ...filters.note_family.map(id => ({ type: 'note_family', id, removable: true })),
      ...filters.size.map(id => ({ type: 'size', id, removable: true })),
      ...(filters.price_range[0] > 520 || filters.price_range[1] < 2380 
        ? [{ type: 'price_range', id: `¥${filters.price_range[0]}-${filters.price_range[1]}`, removable: true }] 
        : []),
      ...(filters.city_exclusive 
        ? [{ type: 'city_exclusive', id: '城市限定', removable: true }] 
        : [])
    ]
  };
  
  return {
    filters,
    filterActions,
    filterStatus
  };
};

// 筛选组件使用示例
export const ProductFilterPanel: React.FC<{
  filterOptions: FilterOptions;
  onFiltersChange: (filters: FilterState) => void;
}> = ({ filterOptions, onFiltersChange }) => {
  const { filters, filterActions, filterStatus } = useFilterState();
  
  // 同步筛选变化到父组件
  useEffect(() => {
    onFiltersChange(filters);
  }, [filters, onFiltersChange]);
  
  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-lelabo-border p-4 space-y-6">
      {/* 筛选头部 */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <h3 className="text-lg font-medium text-lelabo-text-primary">筛选条件</h3>
        {filterStatus.hasActiveFilters && (
          <button
            onClick={filterActions.resetFilters}
            className="text-sm text-lelabo-text-muted hover:text-lelabo-primary"
          >
            重置 ({filterStatus.activeFilterCount})
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
      
      {/* 系列筛选 */}
      <div className="space-y-3">
        <h4 className="text-sm font-medium text-lelabo-text-primary">产品系列</h4>
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {filterOptions.collection_options.map((option) => (
            <label key={option.value} className="flex items-center space-x-3">
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={filters.collection.includes(option.value)}
                onChange={() => filterActions.toggleCollection(option.value)}
                className="w-4 h-4 text-lelabo-primary rounded border-lelabo-border"
              />
              <span className="text-sm text-lelabo-text-primary">
                {option.label_zh} ({option.count})
              </span>
            </label>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
      
      {/* 香调筛选 */}
      <div className="space-y-3">
        <h4 className="text-sm font-medium text-lelabo-text-primary">香调分类</h4>
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {filterOptions.note_family_options.map((option) => (
            <label key={option.value} className="flex items-center space-x-3">
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={filters.note_family.includes(option.value)}
                onChange={() => filterActions.toggleNoteFamily(option.value)}
                className="w-4 h-4 text-lelabo-primary rounded border-lelabo-border"
              />
              <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                <div 
                  className="w-3 h-3 rounded-full"
                  style={{ backgroundColor: option.color }}
                />
                <span className="text-sm text-lelabo-text-primary">
                  {option.label_zh} ({option.count})
                </span>
              </div>
            </label>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
      
      {/* 容量筛选 */}
      <div className="space-y-3">
        <h4 className="text-sm font-medium text-lelabo-text-primary">容量规格</h4>
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {filterOptions.size_options.map((option) => (
            <label key={option.value} className="flex items-center space-x-3">
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={filters.size.includes(option.value)}
                onChange={() => filterActions.toggleSize(option.value)}
                className="w-4 h-4 text-lelabo-primary rounded border-lelabo-border"
              />
              <span className="text-sm text-lelabo-text-primary">
                {option.label_zh} ({option.count})
              </span>
            </label>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
      
      {/* 价格区间筛选 */}
      <div className="space-y-3">
        <h4 className="text-sm font-medium text-lelabo-text-primary">价格区间</h4>
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {/* 双滑块价格选择器 */}
          <div className="px-2">
            <input
              type="range"
              min={filterOptions.price_range_config.min}
              max={filterOptions.price_range_config.max}
              step={filterOptions.price_range_config.step}
              value={filters.price_range[0]}
              onChange={(e) => filterActions.setPriceRange(
                parseInt(e.target.value), 
                filters.price_range[1]
              )}
              className="w-full"
            />
            <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-lelabo-text-muted mt-1">
              <span>¥{filters.price_range[0]}</span>
              <span>¥{filters.price_range[1]}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          
          {/* 快捷价格区间 */}
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {filterOptions.price_range_config.quick_ranges.map((range) => (
              <button
                key={range.label}
                onClick={() => filterActions.setPriceRange(range.range[0], range.range[1])}
                className={`
                  px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs border transition-colors
                  ${filters.price_range[0] === range.range[0] && filters.price_range[1] === range.range[1]
                    ? 'bg-lelabo-primary text-white border-lelabo-primary'
                    : 'bg-white text-lelabo-text-secondary border-lelabo-border hover:border-lelabo-primary'
                  }
                `}
              >
                {range.label_zh}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      {/* 城市限定筛选 */}
      <div className="space-y-3">
        <h4 className="text-sm font-medium text-lelabo-text-primary">特殊商品</h4>
        <label className="flex items-center space-x-3">
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            checked={filters.city_exclusive}
            onChange={filterActions.toggleCityExclusive}
            className="w-4 h-4 text-lelabo-primary rounded border-lelabo-border"
          />
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <div className="w-3 h-3 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-yellow-400 to-orange-400" />
            <span className="text-sm text-lelabo-text-primary">
              仅显示城市限定 ({filterOptions.city_exclusive_config.count})
            </span>
          </div>
        </label>
        
        {/* 城市限定说明 */}
        {filters.city_exclusive && (
          <div className="ml-7 text-xs text-lelabo-text-muted">
            {filterOptions.city_exclusive_config.current_season 
              ? "当前可购买城市限定商品" 
              : `下次销售时间: ${filterOptions.city_exclusive_config.next_season_date}`
            }
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
```

### 🔄 **筛选状态持久化**
```typescript
// 筛选状态本地存储
export const FilterStatePersistence = {
  // === 存储键名 ===
  STORAGE_KEY: 'lelabo-filter-state',
  
  // === 保存筛选状态 ===
  saveFilterState: (filters: FilterState) => {
    try {
      const serializedFilters = JSON.stringify({
        ...filters,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
      });
      localStorage.setItem(FilterStatePersistence.STORAGE_KEY, serializedFilters);
    } catch (error) {
      console.warn('Failed to save filter state:', error);
    }
  },
  
  // === 加载筛选状态 ===
  loadFilterState: (): FilterState | null => {
    try {
      const stored = localStorage.getItem(FilterStatePersistence.STORAGE_KEY);
      if (!stored) return null;
      
      const parsed = JSON.parse(stored);
      
      // 检查存储时间，超过24小时则重置
      const timestamp = new Date(parsed.timestamp);
      const now = new Date();
      const hoursDiff = (now.getTime() - timestamp.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60);
      
      if (hoursDiff > 24) {
        localStorage.removeItem(FilterStatePersistence.STORAGE_KEY);
        return null;
      }
      
      // 返回筛选状态 (移除timestamp)
      const { timestamp: _, ...filterState } = parsed;
      return filterState as FilterState;
    } catch (error) {
      console.warn('Failed to load filter state:', error);
      return null;
    }
  },
  
  // === 清除筛选状态 ===
  clearFilterState: () => {
    localStorage.removeItem(FilterStatePersistence.STORAGE_KEY);
  }
};

// URL状态同步
export const FilterStateURLSync = {
  // === 筛选状态转URL参数 ===
  filtersToURLParams: (filters: FilterState): URLSearchParams => {
    const params = new URLSearchParams();
    
    if (filters.collection.length > 0) {
      params.set('collection', filters.collection.join(','));
    }
    
    if (filters.note_family.length > 0) {
      params.set('note_family', filters.note_family.join(','));
    }
    
    if (filters.size.length > 0) {
      params.set('size', filters.size.join(','));
    }
    
    if (filters.price_range[0] > 520 || filters.price_range[1] < 2380) {
      params.set('price_min', filters.price_range[0].toString());
      params.set('price_max', filters.price_range[1].toString());
    }
    
    if (filters.city_exclusive) {
      params.set('city_exclusive', 'true');
    }
    
    return params;
  },
  
  // === URL参数转筛选状态 ===
  URLParamsToFilters: (searchParams: URLSearchParams): FilterState => {
    return {
      collection: searchParams.get('collection')?.split(',') || [],
      note_family: searchParams.get('note_family')?.split(',') || [],
      size: searchParams.get('size')?.split(',') || [],
      price_range: [
        parseInt(searchParams.get('price_min') || '520'),
        parseInt(searchParams.get('price_max') || '2380')
      ],
      city_exclusive: searchParams.get('city_exclusive') === 'true'
    };
  }
};
```

### 📊 **筛选结果统计**
```typescript
// 筛选结果数据结构
interface FilterResults {
  // === 结果统计 ===
  total_count: number;           // 筛选结果总数
  product_ids: string[];         // 符合条件的产品ID数组
  
  // === 分类统计 ===
  by_collection: Record<string, number>; // 各系列商品数量
  by_note_family: Record<string, number>; // 各香调商品数量
  by_size: Record<string, number>; // 各容量商品数量
  by_price_range: {
    under_1000: number;          // ¥1000以下商品数
    range_1000_1500: number;     // ¥1000-1500商品数
    range_1500_2000: number;     // ¥1500-2000商品数
    above_2000: number;          // ¥2000以上商品数
  };
  
  // === 可用性统计 ===
  availability: {
    in_stock_count: number;      // 有库存商品数
    out_of_stock_count: number;  // 缺货商品数
    city_exclusive_count: number; // 城市限定商品数
    store_only_count: number;    // 门店专享商品数
  };
  
  // === 筛选提示 ===
  suggestions: {
    remove_filter_for_more: string[]; // 建议移除的筛选条件
    related_collections: string[]; // 相关系列推荐
    alternative_sizes: string[]; // 替代容量建议
    price_adjustment_suggestion?: string; // 价格调整建议
  };
}

// 筛选应用逻辑
export const applyFilters = (
  products: Product[], 
  filters: FilterState
): FilterResults => {
  let filteredProducts = [...products];
  
  // 系列筛选
  if (filters.collection.length > 0) {
    filteredProducts = filteredProducts.filter(product => 
      filters.collection.includes(product.collection)
    );
  }
  
  // 香调筛选
  if (filters.note_family.length > 0) {
    filteredProducts = filteredProducts.filter(product => 
      filters.note_family.some(family => 
        product.notes_top.some(note => note.includes(family)) ||
        product.notes_middle.some(note => note.includes(family)) ||
        product.notes_base.some(note => note.includes(family))
      )
    );
  }
  
  // 容量筛选
  if (filters.size.length > 0) {
    filteredProducts = filteredProducts.filter(product => 
      product.variants.some(variant => 
        filters.size.includes(`${variant.size_ml}ml`)
      )
    );
  }
  
  // 价格区间筛选
  filteredProducts = filteredProducts.filter(product => {
    const prices = product.variants.map(variant => 
      parseFloat(variant.price_display.replace('¥', '').replace(',', ''))
    );
    const minPrice = Math.min(...prices);
    const maxPrice = Math.max(...prices);
    
    return minPrice <= filters.price_range[1] && maxPrice >= filters.price_range[0];
  });
  
  // 城市限定筛选
  if (filters.city_exclusive) {
    filteredProducts = filteredProducts.filter(product => 
      product.is_city_exclusive === true
    );
  }
  
  return {
    total_count: filteredProducts.length,
    product_ids: filteredProducts.map(p => p.id),
    by_collection: getCollectionCounts(filteredProducts),
    by_note_family: getNoteFamilyCounts(filteredProducts),
    by_size: getSizeCounts(filteredProducts),
    by_price_range: getPriceRangeCounts(filteredProducts),
    availability: getAvailabilityCounts(filteredProducts),
    suggestions: generateFilterSuggestions(filters, filteredProducts)
  };
};
```

### 🏷️ **筛选标签显示组件**
```typescript
// 已选筛选条件标签显示
export const ActiveFilterTags: React.FC<{
  filters: FilterState;
  filterOptions: FilterOptions;
  onRemoveFilter: (type: keyof FilterState, value?: string) => void;
}> = ({ filters, filterOptions, onRemoveFilter }) => {
  if (!filters || Object.keys(filters).length === 0) return null;
  
  const tags = [];
  
  // 系列标签
  filters.collection.forEach(collectionId => {
    const option = filterOptions.collection_options.find(opt => opt.value === collectionId);
    if (option) {
      tags.push({
        type: 'collection' as const,
        value: collectionId,
        label: option.label_zh,
        removable: true
      });
    }
  });
  
  // 香调标签
  filters.note_family.forEach(familyId => {
    const option = filterOptions.note_family_options.find(opt => opt.value === familyId);
    if (option) {
      tags.push({
        type: 'note_family' as const,
        value: familyId,
        label: option.label_zh,
        color: option.color,
        removable: true
      });
    }
  });
  
  // 容量标签
  filters.size.forEach(sizeValue => {
    const option = filterOptions.size_options.find(opt => opt.value === sizeValue);
    if (option) {
      tags.push({
        type: 'size' as const,
        value: sizeValue,
        label: option.label_zh,
        removable: true
      });
    }
  });
  
  // 价格区间标签
  if (filters.price_range[0] > 520 || filters.price_range[1] < 2380) {
    tags.push({
      type: 'price_range' as const,
      label: `¥${filters.price_range[0]} - ¥${filters.price_range[1]}`,
      removable: true
    });
  }
  
  // 城市限定标签
  if (filters.city_exclusive) {
    tags.push({
      type: 'city_exclusive' as const,
      label: '城市限定',
      removable: true
    });
  }
  
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 p-4 bg-lelabo-bg-secondary">
      {tags.map((tag, index) => (
        <div
          key={`${tag.type}-${tag.value || 'flag'}-${index}`}
          className="
            inline-flex items-center space-x-2
            bg-white border border-lelabo-border rounded-full
            px-3 py-1
          "
        >
          {/* 香调颜色标识 */}
          {tag.color && (
            <div 
              className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full"
              style={{ backgroundColor: tag.color }}
            />
          )}
          
          {/* 标签文字 */}
          <span className="text-sm text-lelabo-text-primary">
            {tag.label}
          </span>
          
          {/* 移除按钮 */}
          {tag.removable && (
            <button
              onClick={() => onRemoveFilter(tag.type, tag.value)}
              className="
                w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-lelabo-text-muted
                flex items-center justify-center
                hover:bg-lelabo-text-primary transition-colors
              "
              aria-label={`移除${tag.label}筛选条件`}
            >
              <XMarkIcon className="w-3 h-3 text-white" />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      ))}
      
      {/* 清除所有筛选 */}
      {tags.length > 1 && (
        <button
          onClick={() => onRemoveFilter('all')}
          className="
            text-sm text-lelabo-text-muted hover:text-lelabo-primary
            underline transition-colors
          "
        >
          清除所有 ({tags.length})
        </button>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};
```

这个筛选状态数据结构专门为Le Labo产品列表页面设计，涵盖了所有核心筛选维度，确保用户能够精确找到符合需求的产品，同时保持数据结构的简洁性和易用性。

# 📋 **订单展示数据字段** - Le Labo 订单信息结构

## 📊 订单展示核心字段

基于Le Labo订单管理和用户查看需求的完整订单数据结构：

### 📦 **订单展示数据结构 (OrderDisplay)**
```typescript
interface OrderDisplay {
  // === 订单基础信息 ===
  order_id: string;              // 订单唯一标识符
  created_at: string;            // 订单创建时间 ISO 8601格式
  
  // === 订单商品列表 ===
  items: OrderItem[];            // 订单商品项数组
  
  // === 金额汇总信息 ===
  totals: OrderTotals;           // 订单金额汇总
  
  // === 配送信息摘要 ===
  shipping_summary: ShippingSummary; // 配送相关信息
  
  // === 账单信息摘要 ===
  billing_summary: BillingSummary; // 支付账单信息
  
  // === 个性化定制摘要 ===
  personalization_summary: PersonalizationSummary; // 定制服务摘要
}
```

### 🛍️ **订单商品项字段 (OrderItem)**
```typescript
interface OrderItem {
  // === 商品基础信息 ===
  item_id: string;               // 订单项唯一ID
  product_id: string;            // 产品ID
  product_name: string;          // 产品名称 "SANTAL 33"
  product_type: string;          // 产品类型 "Eau de Parfum"
  collection: string;            // 产品系列 "classic"
  
  // === 变体信息 ===
  variant_id: string;            // 变体ID
  capacity: string;              // 容量 "50ml"
  sku: string;                   // SKU "LLB-SAN33-50ML"
  
  // === 购买信息 ===
  quantity: number;              // 购买数量
  unit_price: number;            // 单价 (购买时价格快照)
  unit_price_formatted: string; // 格式化单价 "¥1,680"
  
  // === 个性化信息 (如有) ===
  personalization?: {
    label_name: string;          // 定制标签名称
    label_date?: string;         // 纪念日期 YYYY-MM-DD
    gift_note?: string;          // 礼品寄语
    personalization_cost: number; // 个性化费用
    preview_image_url?: string;  // 定制效果预览图
    production_status: 'pending' | 'in_production' | 'completed'; // 制作状态
    estimated_completion: string; // 预计完成时间
  };
  
  // === 附加服务 (如有) ===
  addons?: {
    gift_wrapping?: {
      style: string;             // 包装样式 "经典礼盒"
      cost: number;              // 包装费用
      ribbon_color?: string;     // 丝带颜色
    };
    expedited_production?: {
      enabled: boolean;          // 是否加急制作
      cost: number;              // 加急费用
      time_saved: string;        // 节省时间 "2-3个工作日"
    };
  };
  
  // === 商品小计 ===
  line_total: number;            // 该商品行总价
  line_total_formatted: string; // 格式化行总价 "¥1,910"
  
  // === 商品图片 ===
  image_url: string;             // 商品图片URL
  thumbnail_url: string;         // 缩略图URL
  
  // === 履行状态 ===
  fulfillment_status: 'pending' | 'processing' | 'shipped' | 'delivered'; // 履行状态
  tracking_number?: string;      // 物流单号 (已发货商品)
}
```

### 💰 **订单金额汇总 (OrderTotals)**
```typescript
interface OrderTotals {
  // === 商品费用 ===
  subtotal: number;              // 商品小计 (不含个性化和附加费)
  subtotal_formatted: string;   // "¥5,040"
  
  // === 个性化费用 ===
  personalization_fees: number; // 个性化定制费用总计
  personalization_fees_formatted: string; // "¥360"
  personalization_item_count: number; // 定制商品数量
  
  // === 附加服务费用 ===
  addon_fees: number;            // 附加服务费用总计 (礼品包装、加急等)
  addon_fees_formatted: string; // "¥130"
  
  // === 配送费用 ===
  shipping_cost: number;         // 配送费用
  shipping_cost_formatted: string; // "¥0" (免费) 或 "¥30" (加急)
  free_shipping_applied: boolean; // 是否享受免邮
  
  // === 税费 ===
  tax: number;                   // 税费 (已包含在商品价格中)
  tax_formatted: string;        // "¥465"
  tax_rate: number;              // 税率 0.13 (13%)
  
  // === 折扣优惠 ===
  discounts: {
    member_discount: number;     // 会员折扣金额
    coupon_discount: number;     // 优惠券折扣
    promotion_discount: number;  // 促销活动折扣
    points_discount: number;     // 积分抵扣金额
    total_discount: number;      // 总折扣金额
    total_discount_formatted: string; // "¥635"
  };
  
  // === 最终金额 ===
  grand_total: number;           // 订单总金额
  grand_total_formatted: string; // "¥5,075"
  amount_paid: number;           // 实际支付金额
  amount_paid_formatted: string; // "¥5,075"
  
  // === 节省信息 ===
  total_savings: number;         // 总节省金额
  total_savings_formatted: string; // "¥635"
  savings_percentage: number;   // 节省百分比 11.1%
}
```

### 🚚 **配送信息摘要 (ShippingSummary)**
```typescript
interface ShippingSummary {
  // === 配送方式 ===
  method: 'standard' | 'express' | 'same_day' | 'pickup'; // 配送方式
  method_display: string;        // "标准快递" | "次日达" | "当日达" | "门店自提"
  cost: number;                  // 配送费用
  cost_formatted: string;       // "¥0" | "¥30"
  
  // === 配送地址 (快递配送) ===
  delivery_address?: {
    recipient_name: string;      // 收件人姓名
    phone: string;               // 联系电话 (脱敏显示)
    address_line: string;        // 完整地址
    city: string;                // 城市
    postal_code: string;         // 邮编
    special_instructions?: string; // 配送备注
  };
  
  // === 自提门店 (门店自提) ===
  pickup_store?: {
    store_id: string;            // 门店ID
    store_name: string;          // 门店名称
    store_name_zh: string;       // 中文门店名称
    address: string;             // 门店地址
    phone: string;               // 门店电话
    pickup_instructions: string; // 自提说明
  };
  
  // === 时间信息 ===
  timing: {
    estimated_ship_date: string; // 预计发货日期
    estimated_delivery_date: string; // 预计送达日期
    delivery_window?: string;    // 配送时间窗口 "9:00-18:00"
    production_delay?: string;   // 制作延期时间 (定制商品)
  };
  
  // === 物流跟踪 ===
  tracking?: {
    carrier: string;             // 物流公司 "顺丰速运"
    tracking_number: string;     // 运单号
    tracking_url: string;        // 跟踪链接
    current_status: string;      // 当前状态 "运输中"
    last_update: string;         // 最后更新时间
  };
  
  // === 特殊说明 ===
  special_notes: string[];       // 特殊说明数组
  // 例如: ["定制商品需额外5-7个工作日", "易碎商品小心轻放", "签收时请检查包装"]
}
```

### 💳 **账单信息摘要 (BillingSummary)**
```typescript
interface BillingSummary {
  // === 支付方式 ===
  payment_method: 'wechat' | 'alipay' | 'union_pay' | 'credit_card'; // 支付方式
  payment_method_display: string; // "微信支付" | "支付宝" | "银联" | "信用卡"
  
  // === 支付状态 ===
  payment_status: 'pending' | 'paid' | 'failed' | 'refunded' | 'partially_refunded';
  payment_status_display: string; // "已支付" | "支付失败" | "已退款" | "部分退款"
  
  // === 交易信息 ===
  transaction_id: string;        // 支付交易号
  paid_at?: string;              // 支付完成时间
  amount_paid: number;           // 实际支付金额
  amount_paid_formatted: string; // "¥5,075"
  
  // === 发票信息 ===
  invoice: {
    type: 'personal' | 'company'; // 发票类型
    title: string;               // 发票抬头
    tax_number?: string;         // 税号 (企业发票)
    email: string;               // 发票邮箱
    status: 'pending' | 'issued' | 'sent'; // 开票状态
    invoice_number?: string;     // 发票号码
    download_url?: string;       // 发票下载链接
  };
  
  // === 退款信息 (如有) ===
  refund_info?: {
    refund_amount: number;       // 退款金额
    refund_amount_formatted: string; // "¥1,680"
    refund_reason: string;       // 退款原因
    refund_method: string;       // 退款方式 "原路退回"
    refund_status: 'processing' | 'completed'; // 退款状态
    processed_at?: string;       // 处理时间
    estimated_arrival: string;   // 预计到账时间
  };
  
  // === 优惠使用记录 ===
  applied_promotions: {
    coupons: Array<{
      code: string;              // 优惠券代码
      name: string;              // 优惠券名称
      discount_amount: number;   // 折扣金额
    }>;
    member_discount: {
      level: string;             // 会员等级
      discount_rate: number;     // 折扣率 0.95
      discount_amount: number;   // 折扣金额
    };
    points_used: {
      points: number;            // 使用积分数
      value: number;             // 积分价值
    };
  };
}
```

### 🎨 **个性化定制摘要 (PersonalizationSummary)**
```typescript
interface PersonalizationSummary {
  // === 定制概览 ===
  has_personalization: boolean;  // 是否包含个性化商品
  personalized_item_count: number; // 个性化商品数量
  total_personalization_cost: number; // 个性化费用总计
  total_personalization_cost_formatted: string; // "¥360"
  
  // === 定制商品详情 ===
  personalized_items: Array<{
    item_id: string;             // 订单项ID
    product_name: string;        // 产品名称 "SANTAL 33"
    capacity: string;            // 容量 "50ml"
    
    // 定制内容
    customization: {
      label_name: string;        // 标签名称 "SARAH'S SANTAL"
      label_date?: string;       // 纪念日期 "2024-12-25"
      gift_note?: string;        // 礼品寄语
      
      // 格式化显示
      label_display: string;     // 标签显示效果
      date_display?: string;     // 日期显示效果 "2024年12月25日"
      note_preview?: string;     // 寄语预览 (前50字符)
    };
    
    // 制作信息
    production: {
      status: 'pending' | 'in_production' | 'completed'; // 制作状态
      status_display: string;    // "制作中" | "已完成"
      estimated_completion: string; // 预计完成时间
      actual_completion?: string; // 实际完成时间
      production_notes?: string; // 制作备注
    };
    
    // 费用明细
    costs: {
      personalization_fee: number; // 个性化费用
      gift_wrapping?: number;    // 礼品包装费 (如有)
      expedited_fee?: number;    // 加急费用 (如有)
      total_addon_cost: number;  // 附加费用总计
    };
    
    // 预览图片
    preview_images: {
      label_preview?: string;    // 标签预览图URL
      product_preview?: string;  // 产品整体预览图URL
      gift_card_preview?: string; // 礼品卡预览图URL (如有寄语)
    };
  }>;
  
  // === 制作时间汇总 ===
  production_timeline: {
    standard_items_ship_date: string; // 标准商品发货时间
    personalized_items_completion_date: string; // 定制商品完成时间
    overall_estimated_delivery: string; // 整体预计送达时间
    production_delay_days: number; // 因定制增加的天数
  };
  
  // === 定制服务说明 ===
  service_notes: string[];       // 定制服务相关说明
  // 例如: ["定制商品制作需要5-7个工作日", "制作完成后不支持退换货", "定制效果以实物为准"]
  
  // === 质量保证 ===
  quality_assurance: {
    personalization_guarantee: string; // 个性化质量保证
    remake_policy: string;       // 重做政策
    satisfaction_guarantee: string; // 满意度保证
  };
}
```

```typescript
  status: 'pending' | 'confirmed' | 'shipped' | 'out_for_delivery' | 'delivered' | 'exception';
  status_display: string;       // "待发货" | "已发货" | "配送中" | "已送达" | "异常"
  status_updated_at: string;    // 状态更新时间
```

### 💳 **账单信息摘要 (BillingSummary)**
```typescript
interface BillingSummary {
  // === 支付方式 ===
  payment_method: 'wechat' | 'alipay' | 'union_pay' | 'credit_card';
  payment_method_display: string; // "微信支付" | "支付宝" | "银联支付" | "信用卡"
  payment_icon_url?: string;     // 支付方式图标URL
  
  // === 支付状态 ===
  payment_status: 'pending' | 'processing' | 'paid' | 'failed' | 'cancelled' | 'refunded';
  payment_status_display: string; // "待支付" | "已支付" | "支付失败" | "已退款"
  
  // === 交易详情 ===
  transaction_details: {
    transaction_id: string;      // 支付流水号
    amount: number;              // 支付金额
    amount_formatted: string;    // "¥5,075"
    currency: string;            // "CNY"
    paid_at?: string;            // 支付时间
    payment_gateway: string;     // 支付网关 "WeChat Pay" | "Alipay"
  };
  
  // === 发票信息 ===
  invoice_info: {
    requested: boolean;          // 是否申请发票
    type?: 'personal' | 'company'; // 发票类型
    title?: string;              // 发票抬头
    tax_number?: string;         // 税号
    email?: string;              // 发票邮箱
    
    // 发票状态
    status?: 'pending' | 'processing' | 'issued' | 'sent';
    status_display?: string;     // "开票中" | "已开具" | "已发送"
    invoice_number?: string;     // 发票号码
    issued_at?: string;          // 开票时间
    download_url?: string;       // 下载链接
  };
  
  // === 退款记录 (如有) ===
  refund_history?: Array<{
    refund_id: string;           // 退款ID
    amount: number;              // 退款金额
    amount_formatted: string;    // "¥1,680"
    reason: string;              // 退款原因
    status: 'processing' | 'completed' | 'failed'; // 退款状态
    initiated_at: string;       // 发起时间
    completed_at?: string;       // 完成时间
    expected_arrival: string;    // 预计到账时间
  }>;
  
  // === 积分记录 ===
  points_activity: {
    earned_points: number;       // 本单获得积分
    used_points: number;         // 本单使用积分
    points_value: number;        // 积分价值金额
    bonus_points?: number;       // 奖励积分 (评价、分享等)
    
    // 积分明细
    earning_breakdown: {
      base_earning: number;      // 基础积分 (1% 返点)
      member_bonus: number;      // 会员额外积分
      promotion_bonus: number;   // 活动奖励积分
      review_bonus: number;      // 评价奖励积分 (待发放)
    };
  };
  
  // === 优惠使用记录 ===
  discounts_applied: {
    member_discount?: {
      level: string;             // 会员等级
      rate: number;              // 折扣率
      amount: number;            // 折扣金额
    };
    coupons_used: Array<{
      code: string;              // 优惠券代码
      name: string;              // 优惠券名称
      type: 'amount' | 'percentage'; // 折扣类型
      value: number;             // 折扣值
      amount: number;            // 折扣金额
    }>;
    promotion_discounts: Array<{
      name: string;              // 活动名称
      type: string;              // 活动类型 "满减" | "折扣"
      amount: number;            // 优惠金额
    }>;
  };
}
```

### 📊 **订单展示组件示例**
```typescript
// 订单详情展示组件
export const OrderDetailDisplay: React.FC<{
  order: OrderDisplay;
}> = ({ order }) => {
  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-lelabo-border">
      {/* 订单头部信息 */}
      <div className="p-6 border-b border-lelabo-border">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-start">
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-medium text-lelabo-text-primary">
              订单 #{order.order_id}
            </h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-lelabo-text-secondary mt-1">
              下单时间: {formatDate(order.created_at)}
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="text-right">
            <div className="text-2xl font-semibold text-lelabo-text-primary">
              {order.totals.grand_total_formatted}
            </div>
            {order.totals.total_savings > 0 && (
              <div className="text-sm text-green-600">
                已节省 {order.totals.total_savings_formatted}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      {/* 订单商品列表 */}
      <div className="p-6">
        <h3 className="text-lg font-medium mb-4">订单商品</h3>
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {order.items.map((item) => (
            <OrderItemDisplay key={item.item_id} item={item} />
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
      
      {/* 个性化定制摘要 */}
      {order.personalization_summary.has_personalization && (
        <div className="p-6 bg-lelabo-bg-secondary">
          <PersonalizationSummaryDisplay 
            summary={order.personalization_summary} 
          />
        </div>
      )}
      
      {/* 配送信息 */}
      <div className="p-6 border-t border-lelabo-border">
        <ShippingSummaryDisplay summary={order.shipping_summary} />
      </div>
      
      {/* 账单信息 */}
      <div className="p-6 border-t border-lelabo-border">
        <BillingSummaryDisplay summary={order.billing_summary} />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

// 订单商品项展示组件
const OrderItemDisplay: React.FC<{ item: OrderItem }> = ({ item }) => {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-start space-x-4 p-4 rounded-lg bg-white border border-lelabo-border">
      {/* 商品图片 */}
      <img
        src={item.thumbnail_url}
        alt={`${item.product_name} ${item.capacity}`}
        className="w-20 h-20 object-cover rounded-md"
      />
      
      {/* 商品信息 */}
      <div className="flex-1">
        <h4 className="font-medium text-lelabo-text-primary">
          {item.product_name}
        </h4>
        <p className="text-sm text-lelabo-text-secondary">
          {item.product_type} - {item.capacity}
        </p>
        
        {/* 个性化信息显示 */}
        {item.personalization && (
          <div className="mt-2 p-2 bg-lelabo-bg-muted rounded">
            <div className="text-xs text-lelabo-text-secondary mb-1">个性化定制:</div>
            <div className="text-sm">
              <div>标签: "{item.personalization.label_name}"</div>
              {item.personalization.label_date && (
                <div>日期: {item.personalization.label_date}</div>
              )}
              {item.personalization.gift_note && (
                <div className="mt-1">
                  <details>
                    <summary className="cursor-pointer text-lelabo-primary">
                      礼品寄语
                    </summary>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-sm">{item.personalization.gift_note}</p>
                  </details>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
        
        <div className="mt-2 flex justify-between items-center">
          <span className="text-sm text-lelabo-text-secondary">
            数量: {item.quantity}
          </span>
          <span className="font-medium text-lelabo-text-primary">
            {item.line_total_formatted}
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
```

这个订单展示数据结构完全基于Le Labo的真实业务需求，涵盖了订单管理的所有关键信息，特别突出了个性化定制服务的完整展示，为用户提供清晰详细的订单信息查看体验。
