# 🔄 **交互逻辑（要点）** - Le Labo 小程序交互设计

## 📱 核心交互逻辑要点

基于Le Labo品牌特色和小程序用户习惯的关键交互逻辑：

### 🛍️ **产品浏览交互逻辑**
```typescript
// 产品浏览核心交互
const ProductBrowsingInteractions = {
  // === 产品卡片交互 ===
  productCard: {
    // 点击进入详情
    onTap: (productId: string) => {
      router.push(`/products/${productId}`);
      trackEvent('product_view', { productId });
    },
    
    // 长按快速预览
    onLongPress: (product: Product) => {
      showQuickPreview(product);
      hapticFeedback('medium');
      trackEvent('product_quick_preview', { productId: product.id });
    },
    
    // 左滑快速收藏
    onSwipeLeft: (productId: string) => {
      toggleWishlist(productId);
      showToast('已添加到收藏夹', 'success');
      hapticFeedback('light');
    },
    
    // 右滑快速加购 (需选择容量)
    onSwipeRight: (product: Product) => {
      if (product.variants.length === 1) {
        addToCart(product.id, product.variants[0].variant_id);
        showToast('已添加到购物车', 'success');
      } else {
        showCapacitySelector(product);
      }
      hapticFeedback('medium');
    }
  },
  
  // === 列表滚动交互 ===
  listScrolling: {
    // 下拉刷新
    onPullToRefresh: async () => {
      setRefreshing(true);
      await refreshProductList();
      setRefreshing(false);
      hapticFeedback('light');
    },
    
    // 上拉加载更多
    onReachBottom: async () => {
      if (hasMore && !loading) {
        setLoading(true);
        await loadMoreProducts();
        setLoading(false);
      }
    },
    
    // 滚动到顶部
    onScrollToTop: () => {
      showBackToTopButton(false);
    },
    
    // 滚动显示返回顶部
    onScroll: (scrollY: number) => {
      showBackToTopButton(scrollY > 800);
    }
  },
  
  // === 筛选交互 ===
  filtering: {
    // 筛选面板展开
    onFilterToggle: () => {
      setFilterPanelOpen(!filterPanelOpen);
      hapticFeedback('light');
    },
    
    // 应用筛选
    onApplyFilters: (filters: FilterState) => {
      setActiveFilters(filters);
      setFilterPanelOpen(false);
      refreshProductList(filters);
      trackEvent('filter_applied', { filters });
    },
    
    // 重置筛选
    onResetFilters: () => {
      setActiveFilters(getDefaultFilters());
      refreshProductList();
      showToast('筛选条件已重置', 'info');
    },
    
    // 筛选标签移除
    onRemoveFilterTag: (type: string, value: string) => {
      removeFilterCondition(type, value);
      refreshProductList();
      hapticFeedback('light');
    }
  }
};
```

### 🛒 **购物车交互逻辑**
```typescript
// 购物车核心交互
const CartInteractions = {
  // === 添加商品交互 ===
  addToCart: {
    // 未选择容量时的处理
    onAddWithoutCapacity: (product: Product) => {
      showCapacitySelector({
        product,
        onCapacitySelected: (variantId: string) => {
          addToCart(product.id, variantId);
          hideCapacitySelector();
          showMiniCart();
        },
        onCancel: () => {
          hideCapacitySelector();
        }
      });
    },
    
    // 直接加购 (已选容量)
    onDirectAdd: (productId: string, variantId: string, quantity: number = 1) => {
      const result = addToCart(productId, variantId, quantity);
      
      if (result.success) {
        showToast(`已添加到购物车`, 'success');
        updateCartBadge(getCartItemCount());
        showMiniCart(2000); // 2秒后自动收起
        hapticFeedback('medium');
        
        trackEvent('add_to_cart', {
          productId,
          variantId,
          quantity,
          source: 'product_page'
        });
      } else {
        showToast(result.message, 'error');
        hapticFeedback('error');
      }
    },
    
    // 库存不足处理
    onInsufficientStock: (product: Product, requestedQty: number, availableQty: number) => {
      showConfirmDialog({
        title: '库存不足',
        message: `${product.name} 仅剩 ${availableQty} 件，是否购买现有库存？`,
        confirmText: '购买现有库存',
        cancelText: '设置到货提醒',
        onConfirm: () => {
          addToCart(product.id, product.variants[0].variant_id, availableQty);
        },
        onCancel: () => {
          setStockAlert(product.id);
          showToast('已设置到货提醒', 'info');
        }
      });
    }
  },
  
  // === 购物车内商品操作 ===
  cartItemActions: {
    // 数量增减
    onQuantityChange: (itemId: string, newQuantity: number) => {
      if (newQuantity <= 0) {
        showConfirmDialog({
          title: '确认删除',
          message: '确定要从购物车移除这件商品吗？',
          onConfirm: () => removeCartItem(itemId),
          onCancel: () => {} // 保持原数量
        });
        return;
      }
      
      const result = updateCartItemQuantity(itemId, newQuantity);
      
      if (!result.success) {
        if (result.reason === 'insufficient_stock') {
          showToast(`库存不足，仅剩 ${result.availableStock} 件`, 'warning');
          // 自动调整到最大可用数量
          updateCartItemQuantity(itemId, result.availableStock);
        } else {
          showToast(result.message, 'error');
        }
      }
      
      hapticFeedback('light');
    },
    
    // 左滑显示操作菜单
    onSwipeLeft: (itemId: string) => {
      showItemActionMenu(itemId, [
        {
          label: '收藏',
          icon: 'heart',
          action: () => moveToWishlist(itemId),
          color: 'blue'
        },
        {
          label: '编辑定制',
          icon: 'edit',
          action: () => editPersonalization(itemId),
          color: 'orange',
          show: hasPersonalization(itemId)
        },
        {
          label: '删除',
          icon: 'trash',
          action: () => removeCartItem(itemId),
          color: 'red'
        }
      ]);
    },
    
    // 编辑个性化
    onEditPersonalization: (itemId: string) => {
      const currentPersonalization = getItemPersonalization(itemId);
      
      showPersonalizationPanel({
        initialData: currentPersonalization,
        onSave: (newPersonalization: Personalization) => {
          updateItemPersonalization(itemId, newPersonalization);
          showToast('个性化定制已更新', 'success');
          hapticFeedback('medium');
        },
        onCancel: () => {
          // 保持原有设置
        }
      });
    }
  },
  
  // === 批量操作交互 ===
  batchOperations: {
    // 进入批量选择模式
    onEnterBatchMode: () => {
      setBatchModeActive(true);
      setSelectedItems([]);
      showToast('长按商品进行多选', 'info');
    },
    
    // 退出批量选择模式
    onExitBatchMode: () => {
      setBatchModeActive(false);
      setSelectedItems([]);
    },
    
    // 全选/取消全选
    onToggleSelectAll: () => {
      const allItemIds = cartItems.map(item => item.id);
      
      if (selectedItems.length === allItemIds.length) {
        setSelectedItems([]); // 取消全选
      } else {
        setSelectedItems(allItemIds); // 全选
      }
      
      hapticFeedback('light');
    },
    
    // 批量删除
    onBatchDelete: () => {
      if (selectedItems.length === 0) return;
      
      showConfirmDialog({
        title: '确认删除',
        message: `确定要删除选中的 ${selectedItems.length} 件商品吗？`,
        confirmText: '确认删除',
        cancelText: '取消',
        onConfirm: () => {
          batchRemoveCartItems(selectedItems);
          showToast(`已删除 ${selectedItems.length} 件商品`, 'success');
          setSelectedItems([]);
          setBatchModeActive(false);
          hapticFeedback('medium');
        }
      });
    },
    
    // 批量移至收藏
    onBatchMoveToWishlist: () => {
      if (selectedItems.length === 0) return;
      
      batchMoveToWishlist(selectedItems);
      showToast(`已将 ${selectedItems.length} 件商品移至收藏夹`, 'success');
      setSelectedItems([]);
      setBatchModeActive(false);
      hapticFeedback('medium');
    }
  }
};
```

### 🎨 **个性化定制交互逻辑**
```typescript
// 个性化定制交互流程
const PersonalizationInteractions = {
  // === 定制面板开启 ===
  onOpenPersonalizationPanel: (product: Product, variant: ProductVariant) => {
    // 检查定制支持
    if (!variant.personalization_available) {
      showToast('该容量不支持个性化定制', 'warning');
      return;
    }
    
    // 显示定制面板
    showPersonalizationPanel({
      product,
      variant,
      initialData: getDraftPersonalization(product.id, variant.variant_id),
      onSave: handlePersonalizationSave,
      onCancel: handlePersonalizationCancel
    });
    
    trackEvent('personalization_panel_opened', { 
      productId: product.id,
      variantId: variant.variant_id 
    });
  },
  
  // === 实时预览更新 ===
  onPersonalizationChange: (data: Personalization) => {
    // 实时验证
    const validation = validatePersonalization(data);
    
    // 更新预览
    if (validation.label_name.is_valid) {
      updateLabelPreview(data.label_name, data.label_date);
    }
    
    if (data.gift_note && validation.gift_note.is_valid) {
      updateGiftCardPreview(data.gift_note);
    }
    
    // 保存草稿
    saveDraftPersonalization(data);
    
    // 更新费用显示
    updatePersonalizationCost(data);
    
    // 更新预计制作时间
    updateProductionTime(data);
  },
  
  // === 定制保存处理 ===
  handlePersonalizationSave: (data: Personalization) => {
    const validation = validatePersonalization(data);
    
    if (!validation.is_valid) {
      showToast('请检查定制信息', 'error');
      focusFirstError();
      return;
    }
    
    // 确认定制费用
    showConfirmDialog({
      title: '确认个性化定制',
      message: `定制费用 ¥${getPersonalizationCost(data)}，制作时间 5-7个工作日，确认后不可修改。`,
      confirmText: '确认定制',
      onConfirm: () => {
        savePersonalization(data);
        hidePersonalizationPanel();
        showToast('个性化定制设置成功', 'success');
        hapticFeedback('success');
        
        trackEvent('personalization_saved', {
          hasLabelName: !!data.label_name,
          hasDate: !!data.label_date,
          hasGiftNote: !!data.gift_note,
          totalCost: getPersonalizationCost(data)
        });
      }
    });
  },
  
  // === 模板选择交互 ===
  onTemplateSelect: (template: PersonalizationTemplate) => {
    showConfirmDialog({
      title: '使用模板',
      message: '使用此模板将覆盖当前内容，是否继续？',
      confirmText: '使用模板',
      onConfirm: () => {
        applyTemplate(template);
        showToast('模板已应用', 'success');
        hapticFeedback('light');
      }
    });
  }
};
```

### 🔍 **搜索筛选交互逻辑**
```typescript
// 搜索筛选交互流程
const SearchFilterInteractions = {
  // === 搜索交互 ===
  search: {
    // 搜索输入实时处理
    onSearchInput: debounce((query: string) => {
      if (query.length >= 2) {
        fetchSearchSuggestions(query);
        trackEvent('search_input', { query, length: query.length });
      } else {
        clearSuggestions();
      }
    }, 300),
    
    // 搜索建议选择
    onSuggestionSelect: (suggestion: string) => {
      setSearchQuery(suggestion);
      performSearch(suggestion);
      clearSuggestions();
      addToSearchHistory(suggestion);
      
      trackEvent('search_suggestion_selected', { 
        suggestion,
        position: getSuggestionPosition(suggestion)
      });
    },
    
    // 搜索执行
    onSearchSubmit: (query: string) => {
      if (!query.trim()) {
        showToast('请输入搜索内容', 'warning');
        return;
      }
      
      performSearch(query.trim());
      addToSearchHistory(query.trim());
      clearSuggestions();
      
      trackEvent('search_submitted', { 
        query: query.trim(),
        source: 'manual_input'
      });
    },
    
    // 清空搜索
    onSearchClear: () => {
      setSearchQuery('');
      clearSearchResults();
      showAllProducts();
      
      trackEvent('search_cleared');
    }
  },
  
  // === 筛选面板交互 ===
  filterPanel: {
    // 筛选面板开启
    onOpen: () => {
      setFilterPanelOpen(true);
      loadFilterOptions();
      
      trackEvent('filter_panel_opened', {
        currentFiltersCount: getActiveFilterCount()
      });
    },
    
    // 筛选条件变更
    onFilterChange: (filterType: string, value: any) => {
      updateFilter(filterType, value);
      
      // 实时更新结果数量
      const resultCount = getFilteredResultCount();
      updateFilterResultCount(resultCount);
      
      trackEvent('filter_changed', {
        filterType,
        value,
        resultCount
      });
    },
    
    // 应用筛选
    onApplyFilters: () => {
      const activeFilters = getActiveFilters();
      const resultCount = applyFilters(activeFilters);
      
      setFilterPanelOpen(false);
      
      if (resultCount === 0) {
        showEmptyResults();
        showFilterSuggestions();
      } else {
        showFilterResults(resultCount);
        showToast(`找到 ${resultCount} 件商品`, 'success');
      }
      
      trackEvent('filters_applied', {
        filtersUsed: Object.keys(activeFilters).filter(key => 
          activeFilters[key] && (Array.isArray(activeFilters[key]) ? activeFilters[key].length > 0 : true)
        ),
        resultCount
      });
    },
    
    // 筛选重置
    onResetFilters: () => {
      resetAllFilters();
      const totalCount = getAllProductCount();
      
      showToast('筛选已重置', 'info');
      updateFilterResultCount(totalCount);
      
      trackEvent('filters_reset', { totalCount });
    }
  }
};
```

### 💳 **结账流程交互逻辑**
```typescript
// 结账流程交互
const CheckoutInteractions = {
  // === 步骤导航交互 ===
  stepNavigation: {
    // 下一步验证
    onNextStep: (currentStep: CheckoutStep) => {
      const validation = validateCurrentStep(currentStep);
      
      if (!validation.isValid) {
        showStepErrors(validation.errors);
        focusFirstError();
        hapticFeedback('error');
        return;
      }
      
      // 保存当前步骤数据
      saveStepData(currentStep);
      
      // 进入下一步
      const nextStep = getNextStep(currentStep);
      navigateToStep(nextStep);
      
      trackEvent('checkout_step_completed', {
        completedStep: currentStep,
        nextStep,
        timeSpent: getStepTimeSpent(currentStep)
      });
    },
    
    // 返回上一步
    onPreviousStep: (currentStep: CheckoutStep) => {
      const previousStep = getPreviousStep(currentStep);
      
      // 保存当前数据 (草稿)
      saveDraftData(currentStep);
      
      // 返回上一步，数据不丢失
      navigateToStep(previousStep);
      restoreStepData(previousStep);
      
      trackEvent('checkout_step_back', {
        fromStep: currentStep,
        toStep: previousStep
      });
    },
    
    // 步骤点击跳转 (已完成步骤)
    onStepClick: (targetStep: CheckoutStep) => {
      if (!isStepCompleted(targetStep)) {
        showToast('请先完成当前步骤', 'warning');
        return;
      }
      
      navigateToStep(targetStep);
      restoreStepData(targetStep);
      
      trackEvent('checkout_step_jumped', {
        fromStep: getCurrentStep(),
        toStep: targetStep
      });
    }
  },
  
  // === 地址选择交互 ===
  addressSelection: {
    // 选择已有地址
    onSelectAddress: (addressId: string) => {
      setSelectedAddress(addressId);
      validateDeliveryToAddress(addressId);
      calculateShippingCost(addressId);
      
      trackEvent('checkout_address_selected', { addressId });
    },
    
    // 添加新地址
    onAddNewAddress: () => {
      showAddressForm({
        onSave: (address: Address) => {
          saveNewAddress(address);
          setSelectedAddress(address.id);
          showToast('地址添加成功', 'success');
          
          trackEvent('checkout_address_added', {
            addressType: address.label
          });
        },
        onCancel: () => {
          // 返回地址选择
        }
      });
    },
    
    // 编辑地址
    onEditAddress: (addressId: string) => {
      const address = getAddress(addressId);
      
      showAddressForm({
        initialData: address,
        onSave: (updatedAddress: Address) => {
          updateAddress(addressId, updatedAddress);
          showToast('地址更新成功', 'success');
          
          trackEvent('checkout_address_edited', { addressId });
        }
      });
    }
  },
  
  // === 支付流程交互 ===
  payment: {
    // 支付方式选择
    onPaymentMethodSelect: (method: PaymentMethod) => {
      setPaymentMethod(method);
      validatePaymentMethod(method);
      
      trackEvent('payment_method_selected', {
        method: method.type,
        provider: method.provider
      });
    },
    
    // 发起支付
    onInitiatePayment: async () => {
      setPaymentProcessing(true);
      
      try {
        // 最终验证
        const validation = validateCheckoutData();
        if (!validation.isValid) {
          throw new Error(validation.message);
        }
        
        // 创建订单
        const order = await createOrder(getCheckoutData());
        
        // 发起支付
        const paymentResult = await initiatePayment(order.id);
        
        if (paymentResult.success) {
          // 跳转支付确认页
          router.push(`/order-confirmation/${order.id}`);
          
          trackEvent('payment_initiated', {
            orderId: order.id,
            amount: order.total,
            method: paymentMethod.type
          });
        } else {
          throw new Error(paymentResult.message);
        }
        
      } catch (error) {
        showToast(`支付失败: ${error.message}`, 'error');
        hapticFeedback('error');
        
        trackEvent('payment_failed', {
          error: error.message,
          step: 'payment_initiation'
        });
      } finally {
        setPaymentProcessing(false);
      }
    }
  }
};
```

### 📱 **小程序特定交互模式**
```typescript
// 小程序交互优化
const MiniProgramInteractions = {
  // === 手势识别 ===
  gestures: {
    // 统一手势配置
    config: {
      tapTimeout: 300,           // 点击超时
      longPressTimeout: 500,     // 长按触发时间
      swipeThreshold: 50,        // 滑动阈值
      swipeVelocity: 0.3,        // 滑动速度阈值
      doubleClickTimeout: 300    // 双击间隔
    },
    
    // 手势识别处理
    handleTouchEvent: (event: TouchEvent, element: HTMLElement) => {
      const gestureType = recognizeGesture(event);
      
      switch (gestureType) {
        case 'tap':
          handleTap(element);
          break;
        case 'longPress':
          handleLongPress(element);
          break;
        case 'swipeLeft':
          handleSwipeLeft(element);
          break;
        case 'swipeRight':
          handleSwipeRight(element);
          break;
        case 'doubleClick':
          handleDoubleClick(element);
          break;
      }
    }
  },
  
  // === 页面转场动画 ===
  pageTransitions: {
    // 页面进入动画
    onPageEnter: (pageName: string) => {
      setPageTransition('slide-in-right');
      preloadPageResources(pageName);
      
      trackEvent('page_enter', {
        page: pageName,
        fromPage: getPreviousPage(),
        transitionType: 'slide-in-right'
      });
    },
    
    // 页面退出动画
    onPageExit: (pageName: string) => {
      setPageTransition('slide-out-left');
      cleanupPageResources(pageName);
      
      trackEvent('page_exit', {
        page: pageName,
        timeSpent: getPageTimeSpent(pageName)
      });
    },
    
    // 模态框动画
    onModalOpen: (modalType: string) => {
      setModalTransition('fade-in');
      preventBodyScroll(true);
      
      trackEvent('modal_opened', { modalType });
    },
    
    onModalClose: (modalType: string) => {
      setModalTransition('fade-out');
      preventBodyScroll(false);
      
      trackEvent('modal_closed', { modalType });
    }
  },
  
  // === 反馈机制 ===
  feedback: {
    // 触觉反馈
    haptic: {
      light: () => navigator.vibrate?.(10),    // 轻微震动
      medium: () => navigator.vibrate?.(20),   // 中等震动
      heavy: () => navigator.vibrate?.(50),    // 重震动
      success: () => navigator.vibrate?.[100, 50, 100], // 成功模式
      error: () => navigator.vibrate?.[200, 100, 200]   // 错误模式
    },
    
    // 视觉反馈
    visual: {
      // 按钮点击反馈
      buttonPress: (element: HTMLElement) => {
        element.classList.add('animate-scale-down');
        setTimeout(() => {
          element.classList.remove('animate-scale-down');
        }, 150);
      },
      
      // 成功状态反馈
      success: (element: HTMLElement) => {
        element.classList.add('animate-success-pulse');
        setTimeout(() => {
          element.classList.remove('animate-success-pulse');
        }, 600);
      },
      
      // 错误状态反馈
      error: (element: HTMLElement) => {
        element.classList.add('animate-error-shake');
        setTimeout(() => {
          element.classList.remove('animate-error-shake');
        }, 500);
      }
    },
    
    // Toast消息
    toast: {
      show: (message: string, type: 'success' | 'error' | 'warning' | 'info') => {
        createToast({
          message,
          type,
          duration: type === 'error' ? 4000 : 2000,
          position: 'top',
          dismissible: true
        });
        
        trackEvent('toast_shown', { message, type });
      }
    }
  },
  
  // === 状态管理 ===
  stateManagement: {
    // 页面状态保存
    onPageLeave: (pageData: any) => {
      savePageState(getCurrentPage(), pageData);
    },
    
    // 页面状态恢复
    onPageReturn: (pageName: string) => {
      const savedState = getSavedPageState(pageName);
      if (savedState) {
        restorePageState(savedState);
      }
    },
    
    // 购物车状态同步
    onCartStateChange: (cartData: Cart) => {
      // 本地存储同步
      saveCartToLocal(cartData);
      
      // 云端同步 (登录用户)
      if (isLoggedIn()) {
        syncCartToCloud(cartData);
      }
      
      // 更新UI状态
      updateCartBadge(cartData.items.length);
      updateCartTotal(cartData.totals.total);
    },
    
    // 网络状态处理
    onNetworkChange: (isOnline: boolean) => {
      if (!isOnline) {
        showOfflineMode();
        enableOfflineFeatures();
      } else {
        hideOfflineMode();
        syncOfflineActions();
      }
      
      trackEvent('network_status_change', { isOnline });
    }
  }
};
```

### 🎯 **错误处理交互逻辑**
```typescript
// 错误处理交互流程
const ErrorHandlingInteractions = {
  // === 表单验证错误 ===
  formValidation: {
    // 实时验证反馈
    onFieldValidation: (fieldName: string, isValid: boolean, errorMessage?: string) => {
      if (!isValid && errorMessage) {
        showFieldError(fieldName, errorMessage);
        hapticFeedback('error');
      } else if (isValid) {
        clearFieldError(fieldName);
        showFieldSuccess(fieldName);
      }
    },
    
    // 表单提交验证
    onFormSubmitValidation: (errors: ValidationError[]) => {
      if (errors.length > 0) {
        showFormErrorSummary(errors);
        focusFirstErrorField();
        hapticFeedback('error');
        
        trackEvent('form_validation_failed', {
          errorCount: errors.length,
          errorTypes: errors.map(e => e.type)
        });
      }
    }
  },
  
  // === 网络错误处理 ===
  networkErrors: {
    // API请求失败
    onApiError: (error: ApiError) => {
      switch (error.status) {
        case 400:
          showToast('请求参数错误，请重试', 'error');
          break;
        case 401:
          showToast('登录已过期，请重新登录', 'warning');
          redirectToLogin();
          break;
        case 403:
          showToast('没有访问权限', 'error');
          break;
        case 404:
          showToast('请求的资源不存在', 'error');
          break;
        case 429:
          showToast('请求过于频繁，请稍后重试', 'warning');
          break;
        case 500:
          showToast('服务器内部错误，请稍后重试', 'error');
          break;
        default:
          showToast('网络请求失败，请检查网络连接', 'error');
      }
      
      trackEvent('api_error', {
        status: error.status,
        endpoint: error.endpoint,
        message: error.message
      });
    },
    
    // 网络连接错误
    onNetworkError: () => {
      showToast('网络连接异常，请检查网络设置', 'error');
      showRetryButton();
      enableOfflineMode();
      
      trackEvent('network_error');
    },
    
    // 重试机制
    onRetry: async (action: () => Promise<any>, maxRetries: number = 3) => {
      let attempts = 0;
      
      while (attempts < maxRetries) {
        try {
          const result = await action();
          
          if (attempts > 0) {
            showToast('重试成功', 'success');
            trackEvent('retry_succeeded', { attempts });
          }
          
          return result;
        } catch (error) {
          attempts++;
          
          if (attempts < maxRetries) {
            showToast(`重试中 (${attempts}/${maxRetries})`, 'info');
            await delay(1000 * attempts); // 递增延迟
          } else {
            showToast('重试失败，请稍后再试', 'error');
            trackEvent('retry_failed', { attempts, error: error.message });
            throw error;
          }
        }
      }
    }
  },
  
  // === 业务逻辑错误 ===
  businessErrors: {
    // 库存不足
    onOutOfStock: (product: Product, requestedQty: number) => {
      showStockAlert({
        product,
        requestedQty,
        availableQty: product.availability.availableQuantity,
        onSetAlert: () => setStockAlert(product.id),
        onViewAlternatives: () => showAlternativeProducts(product)
      });
    },
    
    // 价格变动
    onPriceChange: (item: CartItem, oldPrice: number, newPrice: number) => {
      const priceChange = newPrice - oldPrice;
      const changeType = priceChange > 0 ? 'increase' : 'decrease';
      
      showPriceChangeAlert({
        item,
        oldPrice,
        newPrice,
        changeAmount: Math.abs(priceChange),
        changeType,
        onAccept: () => updateItemPrice(item.id, newPrice),
        onRemove: () => removeCartItem(item.id)
      });
    },
    
    // 区域限制
    onRegionRestriction: (product: Product, userRegion: string) => {
      showRegionRestrictionAlert({
        product,
        userRegion,
        availableRegions: product.availableRegions,
        onChangeRegion: () => showRegionSelector(),
        onViewAlternatives: () => showAvailableProducts(userRegion)
      });
    }
  }
};
```

# 🔄 **PLP 交互逻辑要点** - Le Labo 产品列表页

### 🎛️ **筛选/排序即时生效**
```typescript
// PLP 筛选排序即时交互
const PLPInteractions = {
  // === 筛选即时生效逻辑 ===
  instantFiltering: {
    // 筛选条件变更立即应用
    onFilterChange: (filterType: keyof FilterState, value: any) => {
      // 1. 立即更新筛选状态
      const newFilters = updateFilterState(filterType, value);
      
      // 2. 实时应用筛选，无需等待
      const filteredResults = applyFiltersInstantly(newFilters);
      
      // 3. 更新产品列表显示
      setProductList(filteredResults.products);
      setResultCount(filteredResults.total_count);
      
      // 4. 更新筛选选项计数
      updateFilterOptionCounts(filteredResults.facets);
      
      // 5. 保存筛选状态
      saveFiltersToURL(newFilters);
      saveFiltersToLocal(newFilters);
      
      // 6. 用户反馈
      if (filteredResults.total_count === 0) {
        showEmptyResults();
        suggestFilterAdjustments(newFilters);
      } else {
        hideEmptyResults();
        showResultCount(filteredResults.total_count);
      }
      
      // 7. 分析追踪
      trackEvent('filter_applied_instant', {
        filterType,
        value,
        resultCount: filteredResults.total_count,
        responseTime: performance.now() - startTime
      });
    },
    
    // 多筛选条件组合应用
    onMultipleFiltersChange: (filterUpdates: Partial<FilterState>) => {
      // 批量更新筛选条件
      const newFilters = { ...currentFilters, ...filterUpdates };
      
      // 立即应用所有筛选
      const results = applyFiltersInstantly(newFilters);
      
      // 一次性更新UI
      updatePLPDisplay(results, newFilters);
      
      trackEvent('multiple_filters_applied', {
        changedFilters: Object.keys(filterUpdates),
        resultCount: results.total_count
      });
    },
    
    // 筛选性能优化
    optimizedFiltering: {
      // 防抖处理快速连续操作
      debouncedFilter: debounce((filters: FilterState) => {
        applyFiltersWithAnalytics(filters);
      }, 100),
      
      // 客户端缓存筛选结果
      getCachedResults: (filterHash: string) => {
        return filterResultsCache.get(filterHash);
      },
      
      // 预加载相关筛选结果
      preloadAdjacentFilters: (currentFilters: FilterState) => {
        const adjacentFilters = generateAdjacentFilterSets(currentFilters);
        adjacentFilters.forEach(filters => {
          preloadFilterResults(filters);
        });
      }
    }
  },
  
  // === 排序即时生效逻辑 ===
  instantSorting: {
    // 排序变更立即应用
    onSortChange: (sortField: string, sortDirection: 'asc' | 'desc') => {
      // 1. 更新排序状态
      setSortState({ field: sortField, direction: sortDirection });
      
      // 2. 立即重新排序当前结果
      const sortedProducts = sortProductsInstantly(
        currentProductList, 
        sortField, 
        sortDirection
      );
      
      // 3. 更新列表显示
      setProductList(sortedProducts);
      
      // 4. 平滑滚动到顶部
      smoothScrollToTop();
      
      // 5. 保存排序偏好
      saveSortPreference(sortField, sortDirection);
      
      // 6. 视觉反馈
      showSortApplied(sortField, sortDirection);
      
      trackEvent('sort_applied_instant', {
        sortField,
        sortDirection,
        productCount: sortedProducts.length
      });
    },
    
    // 排序选项面板
    sortPanel: {
      onOpen: () => {
        showSortPanel();
        highlightCurrentSort();
      },
      
      onOptionSelect: (option: SortOption) => {
        applySortInstantly(option.field, option.direction);
        hideSortPanel();
        hapticFeedback('light');
      },
      
      onClose: () => {
        hideSortPanel();
      }
    }
  },
  
  // === 组合筛选排序逻辑 ===
  combinedFilterSort: {
    // 筛选+排序联合应用
    applyFilterAndSort: (filters: FilterState, sort: SortState) => {
      // 1. 先应用筛选
      const filteredProducts = applyFiltersInstantly(filters);
      
      // 2. 再应用排序
      const sortedProducts = sortProductsInstantly(
        filteredProducts.products,
        sort.field,
        sort.direction
      );
      
      // 3. 更新显示
      setProductList(sortedProducts);
      setResultCount(filteredProducts.total_count);
      
      // 4. 更新URL状态
      updateURLState(filters, sort);
      
      return {
        products: sortedProducts,
        totalCount: filteredProducts.total_count,
        facets: filteredProducts.facets
      };
    }
  }
};
```

### 🔄 **返回保留状态逻辑**
```typescript
// PLP 状态保持机制
const PLPStateRetention = {
  // === 页面状态保存 ===
  savePageState: () => {
    const pageState = {
      // 筛选状态
      filters: getCurrentFilters(),
      
      // 排序状态  
      sort: getCurrentSort(),
      
      // 滚动位置
      scrollPosition: window.pageYOffset,
      
      // 当前页码
      currentPage: getCurrentPage(),
      
      // 搜索查询
      searchQuery: getSearchQuery(),
      
      // 选中的产品 (如果有)
      selectedProducts: getSelectedProducts(),
      
      // 最后查看的产品
      lastViewedProduct: getLastViewedProduct(),
      
      // 时间戳
      savedAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    
    // 保存到 SessionStorage (页面会话期间)
    sessionStorage.setItem('lelabo-plp-state', JSON.stringify(pageState));
    
    // 保存关键状态到 URL
    updateURLWithState(pageState.filters, pageState.sort, pageState.searchQuery);
    
    trackEvent('plp_state_saved', {
      hasFilters: Object.keys(pageState.filters).length > 0,
      hasSearch: !!pageState.searchQuery,
      scrollPosition: pageState.scrollPosition
    });
  },
  
  // === 页面状态恢复 ===
  restorePageState: () => {
    try {
      // 1. 优先从 URL 恢复 (分享链接场景)
      const urlState = getStateFromURL();
      if (urlState.isValid) {
        restoreFromURLState(urlState);
        return;
      }
      
      // 2. 从 SessionStorage 恢复
      const savedState = sessionStorage.getItem('lelabo-plp-state');
      if (!savedState) return;
      
      const pageState = JSON.parse(savedState);
      
      // 3. 检查状态时效性 (30分钟内有效)
      const savedTime = new Date(pageState.savedAt);
      const now = new Date();
      const timeDiff = now.getTime() - savedTime.getTime();
      const maxAge = 30 * 60 * 1000; // 30分钟
      
      if (timeDiff > maxAge) {
        sessionStorage.removeItem('lelabo-plp-state');
        return;
      }
      
      // 4. 恢复筛选状态
      if (pageState.filters) {
        setFilters(pageState.filters);
        applyFiltersWithoutTracking(pageState.filters);
      }
      
      // 5. 恢复排序状态
      if (pageState.sort) {
        setSort(pageState.sort);
        applySortWithoutTracking(pageState.sort);
      }
      
      // 6. 恢复搜索状态
      if (pageState.searchQuery) {
        setSearchQuery(pageState.searchQuery);
        applySearchWithoutTracking(pageState.searchQuery);
      }
      
      // 7. 恢复滚动位置 (延迟执行，等待内容加载)
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (pageState.scrollPosition > 0) {
          window.scrollTo({
            top: pageState.scrollPosition,
            behavior: 'auto' // 不使用平滑滚动
          });
        }
      }, 100);
      
      trackEvent('plp_state_restored', {
        restoredFrom: 'session_storage',
        hasFilters: !!pageState.filters,
        hasSort: !!pageState.sort,
        hasSearch: !!pageState.searchQuery,
        scrollPosition: pageState.scrollPosition
      });
      
    } catch (error) {
      console.warn('Failed to restore PLP state:', error);
      sessionStorage.removeItem('lelabo-plp-state');
    }
  },
  
  // === 页面离开时保存 ===
  onPageLeave: () => {
    // 自动保存当前状态
    PLPStateRetention.savePageState();
    
    // 预加载可能访问的页面
    preloadLikelyNextPages();
  },
  
  // === 页面返回时恢复 ===
  onPageReturn: () => {
    // 恢复保存的状态
    PLPStateRetention.restorePageState();
    
    // 刷新数据 (如果需要)
    const lastRefresh = getLastDataRefresh();
    const now = new Date().getTime();
    const refreshThreshold = 5 * 60 * 1000; // 5分钟
    
    if (now - lastRefresh > refreshThreshold) {
      refreshDataInBackground();
    }
  }
};
```

### 🗑️ **清空筛选交互逻辑**
```typescript
// 清空筛选功能
const ClearFiltersInteractions = {
  // === 单条筛选清除 ===
  clearSingleFilter: {
    // 筛选标签移除
    onRemoveFilterTag: (filterType: keyof FilterState, filterValue?: string) => {
      const newFilters = removeSingleFilter(filterType, filterValue);
      
      // 立即应用新筛选
      const results = applyFiltersInstantly(newFilters);
      updatePLPDisplay(results, newFilters);
      
      // 用户反馈
      showToast(`已移除"${getFilterDisplayName(filterType, filterValue)}"筛选`, 'info');
      hapticFeedback('light');
      
      trackEvent('single_filter_removed', {
        filterType,
        filterValue,
        remainingFiltersCount: getActiveFilterCount(newFilters),
        newResultCount: results.total_count
      });
    },
    
    // 筛选类型全清除
    onClearFilterType: (filterType: keyof FilterState) => {
      const clearedCount = getFilterTypeCount(filterType);
      const newFilters = clearFilterType(filterType);
      
      const results = applyFiltersInstantly(newFilters);
      updatePLPDisplay(results, newFilters);
      
      showToast(`已清除所有${getFilterTypeDisplayName(filterType)}筛选`, 'info');
      
      trackEvent('filter_type_cleared', {
        filterType,
        clearedCount,
        newResultCount: results.total_count
      });
    }
  },
  
  // === 全部筛选清除 ===
  clearAllFilters: {
    // 一键清除所有筛选
    onClearAllFilters: () => {
      const currentFilterCount = getActiveFilterCount();
      
      if (currentFilterCount === 0) {
        showToast('当前没有筛选条件', 'info');
        return;
      }
      
      // 确认清除对话框 (筛选条件较多时)
      if (currentFilterCount >= 3) {
        showConfirmDialog({
          title: '清除所有筛选',
          message: `将清除 ${currentFilterCount} 个筛选条件，确定继续吗？`,
          confirmText: '确认清除',
          cancelText: '取消',
          onConfirm: () => {
            performClearAllFilters();
          }
        });
      } else {
        // 直接清除
        performClearAllFilters();
      }
    },
    
    // 执行清除操作
    performClearAllFilters: () => {
      const originalResultCount = getFilteredResultCount();
      
      // 重置为默认筛选状态
      const defaultFilters = getDefaultFilterState();
      setFilters(defaultFilters);
      
      // 显示所有产品
      const allProducts = getAllProducts();
      setProductList(allProducts);
      setResultCount(allProducts.length);
      
      // 重置排序到默认
      const defaultSort = getDefaultSortState();
      setSort(defaultSort);
      applySortInstantly(defaultSort);
      
      // 清除搜索
      if (hasActiveSearch()) {
        clearSearch();
      }
      
      // 滚动到顶部
      smoothScrollToTop();
      
      // 更新URL (移除筛选参数)
      clearFiltersFromURL();
      
      // 清除本地存储
      clearFiltersFromLocal();
      
      // 用户反馈
      showToast(`筛选已清除，显示全部 ${allProducts.length} 件商品`, 'success');
      hapticFeedback('medium');
      
      trackEvent('all_filters_cleared', {
        originalResultCount,
        finalResultCount: allProducts.length,
        filtersCleared: getCurrentActiveFilters()
      });
    },
    
    // 智能清除建议
    onSuggestFilterClear: (resultCount: number) => {
      if (resultCount === 0) {
        // 无结果时建议清除最近的筛选
        const lastAppliedFilter = getLastAppliedFilter();
        
        showClearFilterSuggestion({
          message: `没有找到符合条件的商品，尝试移除"${lastAppliedFilter.displayName}"筛选？`,
          filterToRemove: lastAppliedFilter,
          onAccept: () => {
            removeSingleFilter(lastAppliedFilter.type, lastAppliedFilter.value);
          },
          onClearAll: () => {
            performClearAllFilters();
          }
        });
      }
    }
  },
  
  // === 快捷清除操作 ===
  quickClearActions: {
    // 筛选面板顶部快捷清除
    onQuickClear: () => {
      const activeFilters = getActiveFiltersSummary();
      
      if (activeFilters.length === 0) return;
      
      // 显示快捷清除选项
      showQuickClearMenu([
        {
          label: '清除系列筛选',
          count: activeFilters.collection?.length || 0,
          action: () => clearFilterType('collection'),
          show: activeFilters.collection?.length > 0
        },
        {
          label: '清除香调筛选', 
          count: activeFilters.note_family?.length || 0,
          action: () => clearFilterType('note_family'),
          show: activeFilters.note_family?.length > 0
        },
        {
          label: '清除容量筛选',
          count: activeFilters.size?.length || 0,
          action: () => clearFilterType('size'),
          show: activeFilters.size?.length > 0
        },
        {
          label: '重置价格区间',
          action: () => resetPriceRange(),
          show: hasPriceFilter()
        },
        {
          label: '清除城市限定',
          action: () => clearCityExclusiveFilter(),
          show: activeFilters.city_exclusive
        },
        {
          label: '清除全部筛选',
          count: getTotalActiveFilterCount(),
          action: () => performClearAllFilters(),
          style: 'destructive'
        }
      ]);
    },
    
    // 双击标题栏清除
    onDoubleTapTitle: () => {
      if (hasActiveFilters()) {
        performClearAllFilters();
        showToast('已清除所有筛选条件', 'info');
      }
    },
    
    // 摇一摇清除 (可选功能)
    onShakeGesture: () => {
      if (hasActiveFilters()) {
        showConfirmDialog({
          title: '检测到摇一摇',
          message: '是否清除当前所有筛选条件？',
          confirmText: '清除筛选',
          onConfirm: () => {
            performClearAllFilters();
            hapticFeedback('heavy');
          }
        });
      }
    }
  }
};
```

### 📍 **状态保持详细逻辑**
```typescript
// PLP 状态保持系统
const PLPStateManagement = {
  // === 状态保存策略 ===
  stateSaving: {
    // 实时保存关键状态
    onStateChange: (stateChange: PLPStateChange) => {
      const currentState = getCurrentPLPState();
      const newState = { ...currentState, ...stateChange };
      
      // 1. 保存到内存 (最快)
      updateMemoryState(newState);
      
      // 2. 保存到 SessionStorage (页面刷新保持)
      saveToSessionStorage('lelabo-plp-state', newState);
      
      // 3. 更新 URL (支持分享/书签)
      updateURLState(newState);
      
      // 4. 保存用户偏好到 LocalStorage (跨会话)
      if (isSignificantChange(stateChange)) {
        saveUserPreferences({
          defaultSort: newState.sort,
          preferredFilters: extractPreferredFilters(newState.filters)
        });
      }
    },
    
    // 关键时机保存
    saveAtKeyMoments: {
      // 离开页面前保存
      onBeforeUnload: () => {
        const state = getCurrentPLPState();
        saveToSessionStorage('lelabo-plp-exit-state', {
          ...state,
          exitTime: new Date().toISOString()
        });
      },
      
      // 进入产品详情前保存
      onEnterProductDetail: (productId: string) => {
        const state = getCurrentPLPState();
        saveToSessionStorage('lelabo-plp-before-detail', {
          ...state,
          enteredProductId: productId,
          entryTime: new Date().toISOString()
        });
      },
      
      // 进入其他页面前保存
      onNavigateAway: (targetPage: string) => {
        const state = getCurrentPLPState();
        saveNavigationState({
          fromPage: 'plp',
          toPage: targetPage,
          plpState: state,
          navigationTime: new Date().toISOString()
        });
      }
    }
  },
  
  // === 状态恢复策略 ===
  stateRestoration: {
    // 页面首次进入恢复
    onPageEnter: (source: 'direct' | 'back' | 'refresh' | 'share') => {
      switch (source) {
        case 'direct':
          // 直接访问 - 使用默认状态或用户偏好
          restoreUserPreferences();
          break;
          
        case 'back':
          // 返回访问 - 恢复完整状态
          restoreCompleteState();
          break;
          
        case 'refresh':
          // 页面刷新 - 从 SessionStorage 恢复
          restoreFromSessionStorage();
          break;
          
        case 'share':
          // 分享链接 - 从 URL 参数恢复
          restoreFromURLParams();
          break;
      }
      
      trackEvent('plp_state_restored', {
        source,
        restoredState: getCurrentPLPState()
      });
    },
    
    // 完整状态恢复流程
    restoreCompleteState: () => {
      try {
        // 1. 恢复筛选状态
        const savedFilters = getStoredFilters();
        if (savedFilters) {
          setFilters(savedFilters);
          applyFiltersInstantly(savedFilters);
        }
        
        // 2. 恢复排序状态
        const savedSort = getStoredSort();
        if (savedSort) {
          setSort(savedSort);
          applySortInstantly(savedSort.field, savedSort.direction);
        }
        
        // 3. 恢复搜索状态
        const savedSearch = getStoredSearch();
        if (savedSearch) {
          setSearchQuery(savedSearch);
          applySearchInstantly(savedSearch);
        }
        
        // 4. 恢复滚动位置
        const savedScrollPosition = getStoredScrollPosition();
        if (savedScrollPosition > 0) {
          setTimeout(() => {
            scrollToPosition(savedScrollPosition);
          }, 200); // 等待列表渲染完成
        }
        
        // 5. 恢复选择状态
        const savedSelection = getStoredSelection();
        if (savedSelection) {
          restoreProductSelection(savedSelection);
        }
        
      } catch (error) {
        console.warn('State restoration failed, using defaults:', error);
        useDefaultState();
      }
    },
    
    // 智能状态恢复
    smartRestore: () => {
      const restorationStrategy = determineRestorationStrategy();
      
      switch (restorationStrategy) {
        case 'full_restore':
          restoreCompleteState();
          break;
          
        case 'partial_restore':
          restoreKeyStateOnly(); // 只恢复筛选和排序
          break;
          
        case 'preference_restore':
          restoreUserPreferences(); // 只恢复用户偏好
          break;
          
        case 'default_state':
          useDefaultState();
          break;
      }
    }
  },
  
  // === 状态同步机制 ===
  stateSynchronization: {
    // 多标签页状态同步
    onStorageChange: (event: StorageEvent) => {
      if (event.key === 'lelabo-plp-state' && event.newValue) {
        const newState = JSON.parse(event.newValue);
        
        // 检查是否需要同步
        if (shouldSyncState(newState)) {
          syncStateFromOtherTab(newState);
          showToast('已同步其他页面的筛选设置', 'info');
        }
      }
    },
    
    // 登录状态变化同步
    onAuthStateChange: (isLoggedIn: boolean) => {
      if (isLoggedIn) {
        // 登录后同步云端偏好
        syncUserPreferencesFromCloud();
      } else {
        // 登出后使用本地状态
        useLocalStateOnly();
      }
    },
    
    // 网络状态变化处理
    onNetworkStateChange: (isOnline: boolean) => {
      if (isOnline) {
        // 重新联网后同步状态
        syncStateToCloud();
        refreshDataIfStale();
      } else {
        // 离线模式只使用本地状态
        useOfflineStateOnly();
      }
    }
  }
};
```

### 🎯 **交互性能优化**
```typescript
// PLP 交互性能优化
const PLPPerformanceOptimization = {
  // === 即时响应优化 ===
  instantResponse: {
    // 筛选预计算
    precomputeFilters: () => {
      // 预计算常用筛选组合结果
      const commonFilterCombinations = [
        { collection: ['classic'] },
        { note_family: ['woody'] },
        { size: ['50ml'] },
        { collection: ['classic'], note_family: ['woody'] }
      ];
      
      commonFilterCombinations.forEach(filters => {
        const results = computeFilterResults(filters);
        cacheFilterResults(getFilterHash(filters), results);
      });
    },
    
    // 渐进式加载
    progressiveLoading: {
      // 首屏优先加载
      loadAboveFold: () => {
        const viewportHeight = window.innerHeight;
        const itemsAboveFold = Math.ceil(viewportHeight / PRODUCT_CARD_HEIGHT) * 2; // 2列
        
        loadProductBatch(0, itemsAboveFold);
      },
      
      // 虚拟滚动优化
      virtualScrolling: {
        itemHeight: 320,         // 产品卡片高度
        bufferSize: 5,           // 缓冲区大小
        threshold: 200,          // 加载阈值
        
        onScroll: (scrollTop: number) => {
          const startIndex = Math.floor(scrollTop / 320);
          const endIndex = startIndex + getVisibleItemCount() + 10; // 预加载10个
          
          loadVisibleItems(startIndex, endIndex);
        }
      }
    },
    
    // 操作防抖优化
    debouncedOperations: {
      // 快速筛选防抖
      quickFilter: debounce((filters: FilterState) => {
        applyFiltersWithAnalytics(filters);
      }, 150),
      
      // 搜索输入防抖
      searchInput: debounce((query: string) => {
        performSearch(query);
      }, 300),
      
      // 滚动位置保存防抖
      saveScrollPosition: debounce((position: number) => {
        saveToSessionStorage('lelabo-plp-scroll', position);
      }, 500)
    }
  },
  
  // === 内存管理优化 ===
  memoryManagement: {
    // 图片懒加载
    lazyLoadImages: {
      threshold: 0.1,            // 10% 进入视窗时加载
      rootMargin: '50px',        // 提前50px开始加载
      
      onImageEnterViewport: (imageElement: HTMLImageElement) => {
        if (!imageElement.dataset.loaded) {
          loadProductImage(imageElement);
          imageElement.dataset.loaded = 'true';
        }
      }
    },
    
    // 组件回收
    componentRecycling: {
      // 移出视窗的产品卡片回收
      onProductCardLeaveViewport: (cardElement: HTMLElement) => {
        recycleProductCard(cardElement);
      },
      
      // 重新进入视窗时恢复
      onProductCardEnterViewport: (cardElement: HTMLElement, productData: Product) => {
        restoreProductCard(cardElement, productData);
      }
    },
    
    // 缓存清理
    cacheCleanup: {
      // 定期清理过期缓存
      cleanExpiredCache: () => {
        const expiredKeys = getCacheManager().getExpiredKeys();
        expiredKeys.forEach(key => {
          getCacheManager().remove(key);
        });
      },
      
      // 内存压力时清理
      onMemoryPressure: () => {
        clearNonEssentialCache();
        recycleOffscreenComponents();
        triggerGarbageCollection();
      }
    }
  }
};
```

### 🔄 **状态持久化实现**
```typescript
// PLP 状态持久化实现
export const usePLPStatePersistence = () => {
  const [filters, setFilters] = useState<FilterState>(getDefaultFilterState());
  const [sort, setSort] = useState<SortState>(getDefaultSortState());
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState<string>('');
  const [scrollPosition, setScrollPosition] = useState<number>(0);
  
  // === 状态变化监听 ===
  useEffect(() => {
    const stateToSave = {
      filters,
      sort,
      searchQuery,
      scrollPosition,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    
    // 实时保存状态
    PLPStateRetention.savePageState(stateToSave);
    
  }, [filters, sort, searchQuery, scrollPosition]);
  
  // === 页面进入时恢复状态 ===
  useEffect(() => {
    const restoreState = () => {
      const navigationSource = detectNavigationSource();
      PLPStateRetention.restorePageState(navigationSource);
    };
    
    restoreState();
    
    // 监听浏览器后退/前进
    const handlePopState = () => {
      PLPStateRetention.restoreFromURLState();
    };
    
    window.addEventListener('popstate', handlePopState);
    
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('popstate', handlePopState);
      // 页面卸载时保存状态
      PLPStateRetention.savePageState();
    };
  }, []);
  
  // === 清除筛选实现 ===
  const clearFiltersWithFeedback = useCallback(() => {
    const beforeCount = getFilteredProductCount();
    
    // 执行清除
    PLPInteractions.clearAllFilters.performClearAllFilters();
    
    const afterCount = getAllProductCount();
    
    // 用户反馈
    showClearFiltersSuccess(beforeCount, afterCount);
    
    // 推荐相关操作
    suggestNextActions();
    
  }, [filters, sort]);
  
  return {
    filters,
    sort,
    searchQuery,
    setFilters: (newFilters: FilterState) => {
      setFilters(newFilters);
      PLPInteractions.instantFiltering.onFilterChange('bulk', newFilters);
    },
    setSort: (newSort: SortState) => {
      setSort(newSort);
      PLPInteractions.instantSorting.onSortChange(newSort.field, newSort.direction);
    },
    clearAllFilters: clearFiltersWithFeedback,
    clearSingleFilter: PLPInteractions.clearSingleFilter.onRemoveFilterTag
  };
};

// 导航来源检测
const detectNavigationSource = (): 'direct' | 'back' | 'refresh' | 'share' => {
  const navigation = performance.getEntriesByType('navigation')[0] as any;
  
  if (navigation.type === 'reload') return 'refresh';
  if (navigation.type === 'back_forward') return 'back';
  if (document.referrer && document.referrer.includes(window.location.origin)) return 'back';
  if (window.location.search) return 'share'; // URL中有参数
  
  return 'direct';
};
```

这个PLP交互逻辑完全基于用户体验最佳实践，确保筛选/排序即时生效、状态完整保持、清空操作便捷直观，为Le Labo用户提供流畅高效的产品浏览体验。

# 🔄 **PDP 交互逻辑要点** - Le Labo 产品详情页

## 📱 PDP 核心交互逻辑

基于Le Labo产品详情页的容量切换和个性化验证交互：

### 📦 **容量切换交互逻辑**
```typescript
// PDP 容量切换核心交互
const PDPCapacityInteractions = {
  // === 容量切换处理 ===
  onCapacityChange: (newVariantId: string, product: Product) => {
    const newVariant = product.variants.find(v => v.variant_id === newVariantId);
    if (!newVariant) return;
    
    // 1. 立即更新选中状态
    setSelectedVariant(newVariant);
    
    // 2. 同步价格显示
    updatePriceDisplay({
      price: newVariant.price,
      priceFormatted: newVariant.price_display,
      capacity: `${newVariant.size_ml}ml`,
      costPerMl: calculateCostPerMl(newVariant.price, newVariant.size_ml),
      
      // VIP价格 (如适用)
      vipPrice: calculateVipPrice(newVariant.price),
      vipPriceFormatted: formatPrice(calculateVipPrice(newVariant.price)),
      
      // 促销价格 (如适用)
      promotionalPrice: getPromotionalPrice(newVariant.variant_id),
      
      // 价格对比
      priceComparison: {
        vs15ml: newVariant.size_ml !== 15 ? calculatePriceComparison(newVariant, '15ml') : null,
        vs50ml: newVariant.size_ml !== 50 ? calculatePriceComparison(newVariant, '50ml') : null,
        vs100ml: newVariant.size_ml !== 100 ? calculatePriceComparison(newVariant, '100ml') : null
      }
    });
    
    // 3. 同步库存状态
    updateStockStatus({
      inStock: newVariant.in_stock,
      quantity: newVariant.inventory_quantity || 0,
      stockLevel: getStockLevel(newVariant.inventory_quantity),
      stockMessage: getStockMessage(newVariant),
      maxPurchaseQuantity: getMaxPurchaseQuantity(newVariant),
      restockDate: getEstimatedRestockDate(newVariant.variant_id)
    });
    
    // 4. 更新购买按钮状态
    updatePurchaseButtonState({
      variant: newVariant,
      personalizationValid: getCurrentPersonalizationValidation(),
      userEligible: checkUserPurchaseEligibility(newVariant)
    });
    
    // 5. 更新个性化选项
    updatePersonalizationAvailability({
      available: newVariant.personalization_available,
      cost: newVariant.personalization_cost,
      restrictions: getPersonalizationRestrictions(newVariant)
    });
    
    // 6. 更新配送信息
    updateShippingInfo({
      variant: newVariant,
      userAddress: getCurrentUserAddress(),
      shippingMethods: getAvailableShippingMethods(newVariant)
    });
    
    // 7. 视觉反馈
    highlightSelectedCapacity(newVariant.variant_id);
    animateContentUpdate();
    hapticFeedback('light');
    
    // 8. 分析追踪
    trackEvent('pdp_capacity_changed', {
      productId: product.id,
      fromCapacity: getPreviousVariant()?.size_ml,
      toCapacity: newVariant.size_ml,
      priceChange: newVariant.price - (getPreviousVariant()?.price || 0),
      stockStatus: newVariant.in_stock ? 'in_stock' : 'out_of_stock'
    });
  },
  
  // === 价格同步详细逻辑 ===
  priceSync: {
    // 价格计算与显示
    updatePriceCalculation: (variant: ProductVariant) => {
      const basePrice = variant.price;
      const capacity = variant.size_ml;
      
      // 基础价格信息
      const priceInfo = {
        basePrice,
        basePriceFormatted: variant.price_display,
        capacity: `${capacity}ml`,
        costPerMl: Math.round(basePrice / capacity * 100) / 100,
        costPerMlFormatted: `¥${Math.round(basePrice / capacity * 100) / 100}/ml`
      };
      
      // 会员价格 (如适用)
      if (isVipMember()) {
        const vipDiscount = getVipDiscountRate(); // 0.95 = 95折
        priceInfo.vipPrice = Math.round(basePrice * vipDiscount);
        priceInfo.vipPriceFormatted = `¥${priceInfo.vipPrice}`;
        priceInfo.vipSavings = basePrice - priceInfo.vipPrice;
        priceInfo.vipSavingsFormatted = `¥${priceInfo.vipSavings}`;
      }
      
      // 促销价格 (如适用)
      const promotion = getCurrentPromotion(variant.variant_id);
      if (promotion) {
        priceInfo.promotionalPrice = calculatePromotionalPrice(basePrice, promotion);
        priceInfo.promotionalPriceFormatted = formatPrice(priceInfo.promotionalPrice);
        priceInfo.promotionSavings = basePrice - priceInfo.promotionalPrice;
        priceInfo.promotionName = promotion.name;
      }
      
      // 个性化费用预览
      if (variant.personalization_available) {
        priceInfo.personalizationCost = variant.personalization_cost;
        priceInfo.totalWithPersonalization = basePrice + variant.personalization_cost;
        priceInfo.totalWithPersonalizationFormatted = formatPrice(priceInfo.totalWithPersonalization);
      }
      
      // 价值对比 (显示最划算提示)
      const allVariants = getProductVariants(variant.product_id);
      priceInfo.valueComparison = calculateValueComparison(variant, allVariants);
      
      return priceInfo;
    },
    
    // 价格变更动画
    animatePriceChange: (oldPrice: number, newPrice: number) => {
      const priceElement = document.querySelector('[data-price-display]');
      if (!priceElement) return;
      
      // 价格变化动画
      priceElement.classList.add('animate-price-change');
      
      // 数字计数动画
      animateNumber(priceElement, oldPrice, newPrice, 300);
      
      setTimeout(() => {
        priceElement.classList.remove('animate-price-change');
      }, 300);
    }
  },
  
  // === 库存状态同步 ===
  stockStatusSync: {
    // 库存状态更新
    updateStockDisplay: (variant: ProductVariant) => {
      const stockInfo = analyzeStockStatus(variant);
      
      // 更新库存显示
      setStockDisplay({
        status: stockInfo.status,
        message: stockInfo.message,
        quantity: stockInfo.availableQuantity,
        urgencyLevel: stockInfo.urgencyLevel,
        restockInfo: stockInfo.restockInfo
      });
      
      // 更新购买控件状态
      updatePurchaseControls({
        canPurchase: stockInfo.canPurchase,
        maxQuantity: stockInfo.maxQuantity,
        quantityStep: stockInfo.quantityStep,
        defaultQuantity: stockInfo.recommendedQuantity
      });
      
      // 库存紧张提醒
      if (stockInfo.urgencyLevel === 'high') {
        showLowStockAlert(stockInfo);
      }
      
      // 缺货处理
      if (!stockInfo.canPurchase) {
        showOutOfStockOptions({
          variant,
          alternativeVariants: getAlternativeVariants(variant),
          onStockAlert: () => setStockAlert(variant.variant_id),
          onViewAlternatives: () => showAlternativeProducts(variant.product_id)
        });
      }
    },
    
    // 实时库存检查
    realTimeStockCheck: debounce(async (variantId: string) => {
      try {
        const stockData = await checkRealTimeStock(variantId);
        
        if (stockData.hasChanged) {
          updateVariantStock(variantId, stockData);
          
          if (!stockData.inStock) {
            showStockChangedAlert('该容量刚刚售罄');
          } else if (stockData.quantity <= 3) {
            showStockChangedAlert(`仅剩 ${stockData.quantity} 件`);
          }
        }
      } catch (error) {
        console.warn('Real-time stock check failed:', error);
      }
    }, 2000)
  }
};
```

### 🎨 **个性化验证交互逻辑**
```typescript
// 个性化验证与购买控制
const PersonalizationValidationInteractions = {
  // === 个性化实时验证 ===
  realTimeValidation: {
    // 标签名称验证
    onLabelNameChange: (labelName: string) => {
      const validation = PersonalizationValidation.labelName.validateLength(labelName);
      const charValidation = PersonalizationValidation.labelName.validateCharacters(labelName);
      
      const isValid = validation.is_valid && charValidation.is_valid;
      const errorMessage = validation.error_message || charValidation.error_message;
      
      // 更新验证状态
      setPersonalizationValidation(prev => ({
        ...prev,
        labelName: {
          isValid,
          errorMessage,
          characterCount: labelName.length,
          maxCharacters: /[\u4e00-\u9fa5]/.test(labelName) ? 11 : 22
        }
      }));
      
      // 实时更新预览
      if (isValid) {
        updateLabelPreview(labelName);
      }
      
      // 更新购买按钮状态
      updatePurchaseButtonValidation();
    },
    
    // 日期验证
    onDateChange: (date: string) => {
      const validation = date ? 
        PersonalizationValidation.labelDate.validateFormat(date) : 
        { is_valid: true };
      
      setPersonalizationValidation(prev => ({
        ...prev,
        date: {
          isValid: validation.is_valid,
          errorMessage: validation.error_message,
          value: date
        }
      }));
      
      // 更新预览
      if (validation.is_valid) {
        updateDatePreview(date);
      }
      
      updatePurchaseButtonValidation();
    },
    
    // 寄语验证
    onGiftNoteChange: (giftNote: string) => {
      const lengthValidation = PersonalizationValidation.giftNote.validateLength(giftNote);
      const contentValidation = PersonalizationValidation.giftNote.validateContent(giftNote);
      
      const isValid = lengthValidation.is_valid && contentValidation.is_valid;
      const errorMessage = lengthValidation.error_message || contentValidation.error_message;
      
      setPersonalizationValidation(prev => ({
        ...prev,
        giftNote: {
          isValid,
          errorMessage,
          characterCount: giftNote.length,
          maxCharacters: 120
        }
      }));
      
      // 更新礼品卡预览
      if (isValid && giftNote) {
        updateGiftCardPreview(giftNote);
      }
      
      updatePurchaseButtonValidation();
    }
  },
  
  // === 购买按钮状态控制 ===
  purchaseButtonControl: {
    // 购买按钮状态计算
    calculateButtonState: (): PurchaseButtonState => {
      const selectedVariant = getSelectedVariant();
      const personalizationData = getCurrentPersonalizationData();
      const personalizationValidation = getCurrentPersonalizationValidation();
      
      // 检查容量选择
      if (!selectedVariant) {
        return {
          state: 'disabled',
          primaryText: '请选择容量',
          secondaryText: '立即购买',
          disabledReason: 'NO_CAPACITY_SELECTED',
          allowAddToCart: false
        };
      }
      
      // 检查库存状态
      if (!selectedVariant.in_stock) {
        return {
          state: 'disabled',
          primaryText: '暂时缺货',
          secondaryText: '设置到货提醒',
          disabledReason: 'OUT_OF_STOCK',
          allowAddToCart: false,
          alternativeAction: {
            text: '到货提醒',
            action: () => setStockAlert(selectedVariant.variant_id)
          }
        };
      }
      
      // 检查个性化验证 (如果用户填写了个性化信息)
      if (hasPersonalizationData(personalizationData)) {
        const isPersonalizationValid = validatePersonalizationData(personalizationData);
        
        if (!isPersonalizationValid.isValid) {
          return {
            state: 'disabled',
            primaryText: '个性化信息有误',
            secondaryText: '请检查定制信息',
            disabledReason: 'INVALID_PERSONALIZATION',
            allowAddToCart: false,
            errorDetails: isPersonalizationValid.errors,
            suggestedAction: '点击检查定制信息'
          };
        }
      }
      
      // 检查购买限制 (City Exclusive 等)
      const purchaseEligibility = checkPurchaseEligibility(selectedVariant);
      if (!purchaseEligibility.eligible) {
        return {
          state: 'disabled',
          primaryText: purchaseEligibility.reason,
          secondaryText: purchaseEligibility.suggestion,
          disabledReason: 'PURCHASE_RESTRICTED',
          allowAddToCart: false,
          restrictionDetails: purchaseEligibility.details
        };
      }
      
      // 正常可购买状态
      const stockQuantity = selectedVariant.inventory_quantity || 0;
      let primaryText = '立即购买';
      
      if (stockQuantity <= 3 && stockQuantity > 0) {
        primaryText = `立即购买 (仅剩${stockQuantity}件)`;
      }
      
      return {
        state: 'enabled',
        primaryText,
        secondaryText: '加入购物车',
        disabledReason: null,
        allowAddToCart: true,
        urgencyMessage: stockQuantity <= 3 ? `库存紧张，仅剩${stockQuantity}件` : null
      };
    },
    
    // 购买按钮状态实时更新
    updatePurchaseButtonState: () => {
      const buttonState = PDPCapacityInteractions.purchaseButtonControl.calculateButtonState();
      
      // 更新主要购买按钮
      setPrimaryPurchaseButton({
        text: buttonState.primaryText,
        enabled: buttonState.state === 'enabled',
        variant: buttonState.state === 'enabled' ? 'primary' : 'disabled',
        loading: false,
        onClick: buttonState.state === 'enabled' ? handleDirectPurchase : null
      });
      
      // 更新加入购物车按钮
      setAddToCartButton({
        text: buttonState.secondaryText,
        enabled: buttonState.allowAddToCart,
        variant: buttonState.allowAddToCart ? 'secondary' : 'disabled',
        onClick: buttonState.allowAddToCart ? handleAddToCart : null
      });
      
      // 显示禁用原因提示
      if (buttonState.state === 'disabled' && buttonState.disabledReason) {
        showDisabledReason({
          reason: buttonState.disabledReason,
          message: getDisabledReasonMessage(buttonState.disabledReason),
          suggestion: buttonState.suggestedAction,
          alternativeAction: buttonState.alternativeAction
        });
      } else {
        hideDisabledReason();
      }
      
      // 紧急提醒
      if (buttonState.urgencyMessage) {
        showUrgencyMessage(buttonState.urgencyMessage);
      }
    },
    
    // 容量推荐逻辑
    showCapacityRecommendation: (product: Product) => {
      const recommendations = generateCapacityRecommendations(product);
      
      if (recommendations.length > 0) {
        showCapacityRecommendationBanner({
          recommendations,
          onSelect: (variantId: string) => {
            PDPCapacityInteractions.onCapacityChange(variantId, product);
            hideCapacityRecommendationBanner();
          }
        });
      }
    }
  },
  
  // === 个性化验证控制 ===
  personalizationValidationControl: {
    // 个性化开启检查
    onEnablePersonalization: (variant: ProductVariant) => {
      if (!variant.personalization_available) {
        showToast('该容量不支持个性化定制', 'warning');
        return false;
      }
      
      // 显示个性化面板
      showPersonalizationPanel({
        variant,
        onValidationChange: (isValid: boolean) => {
          updatePersonalizationValidationStatus(isValid);
          updatePurchaseButtonState();
        }
      });
      
      return true;
    },
    
    // 个性化数据验证
    validatePersonalizationInput: (data: PersonalizationInput): PersonalizationValidationResult => {
      const results = {
        isValid: true,
        errors: [] as string[],
        warnings: [] as string[]
      };
      
      // 标签名称验证 (必填)
      if (!data.label_name || !data.label_name.trim()) {
        results.isValid = false;
        results.errors.push('请输入标签名称');
      } else {
        const labelValidation = PersonalizationValidation.labelName.validateLength(data.label_name);
        const charValidation = PersonalizationValidation.labelName.validateCharacters(data.label_name);
        
        if (!labelValidation.is_valid) {
          results.isValid = false;
          results.errors.push(labelValidation.error_message!);
        }
        
        if (!charValidation.is_valid) {
          results.isValid = false;
          results.errors.push(charValidation.error_message!);
        }
      }
      
      // 日期验证 (可选)
      if (data.label_date) {
        const dateValidation = PersonalizationValidation.labelDate.validateFormat(data.label_date);
        const rangeValidation = PersonalizationValidation.labelDate.validateRange(data.label_date);
        
        if (!dateValidation.is_valid) {
          results.isValid = false;
          results.errors.push(dateValidation.error_message!);
        }
        
        if (!rangeValidation.is_valid) {
          results.isValid = false;
          results.errors.push(rangeValidation.error_message!);
        }
        
        // 未来日期警告
        if (rangeValidation.warning_message) {
          results.warnings.push(rangeValidation.warning_message);
        }
      }
      
      // 礼品寄语验证 (可选)
      if (data.gift_note) {
        const noteValidation = PersonalizationValidation.giftNote.validateLength(data.gift_note);
        const contentValidation = PersonalizationValidation.giftNote.validateContent(data.gift_note);
        
        if (!noteValidation.is_valid) {
          results.isValid = false;
          results.errors.push(noteValidation.error_message!);
        }
        
        if (!contentValidation.is_valid) {
          results.isValid = false;
          results.errors.push(contentValidation.error_message!);
        }
      }
      
      return results;
    },
    
    // 购买按钮禁用控制
    onPersonalizationInvalid: (validationResult: PersonalizationValidationResult) => {
      // 禁用购买按钮
      setPurchaseButtonsDisabled(true);
      
      // 显示验证错误
      showPersonalizationErrors({
        errors: validationResult.errors,
        warnings: validationResult.warnings,
        onFixErrors: () => {
          focusFirstPersonalizationError();
        }
      });
      
      // 更新按钮文案
      updateButtonText({
        primary: '请完善个性化信息',
        secondary: '检查定制内容'
      });
      
      trackEvent('personalization_validation_failed', {
        errorCount: validationResult.errors.length,
        errorTypes: validationResult.errors.map(getErrorType)
      });
    },
    
    // 个性化验证通过
    onPersonalizationValid: (personalizationData: PersonalizationInput) => {
      // 启用购买按钮
      setPurchaseButtonsDisabled(false);
      
      // 隐藏错误提示
      hidePersonalizationErrors();
      
      // 更新按钮文案 (包含个性化费用)
      const variant = getSelectedVariant();
      const totalCost = variant.price + variant.personalization_cost;
      
      updateButtonText({
        primary: `立即购买 ¥${totalCost}`,
        secondary: '加入购物车'
      });
      
      // 显示个性化预览
      showPersonalizationPreview(personalizationData);
      
      // 显示制作时间提醒
      showProductionTimeInfo({
        standardTime: '正常制作需要 5-7个工作日',
        expeditedOption: getExpeditedProductionOption(variant),
        estimatedCompletion: calculateCompletionDate(5, 7)
      });
      
      trackEvent('personalization_validation_passed', {
        hasLabelName: !!personalizationData.label_name,
        hasDate: !!personalizationData.label_date,
        hasGiftNote: !!personalizationData.gift_note,
        totalCost: totalCost
      });
    }
  },
  
  // === 购买流程控制 ===
  purchaseFlowControl: {
    // 立即购买处理
    onDirectPurchase: async () => {
      const variant = getSelectedVariant();
      const personalizationData = getCurrentPersonalizationData();
      
      // 最终验证
      const finalValidation = performFinalValidation(variant, personalizationData);
      
      if (!finalValidation.isValid) {
        showValidationErrors(finalValidation.errors);
        hapticFeedback('error');
        return;
      }
      
      try {
        // 设置加载状态
        setPurchaseLoading(true);
        
        // 创建购物车项
        const cartItem = createCartItem(variant, personalizationData);
        
        // 直接跳转到结账
        router.push(`/checkout?direct_purchase=true&item=${cartItem.id}`);
        
        trackEvent('direct_purchase_initiated', {
          productId: variant.product_id,
          variantId: variant.variant_id,
          hasPersonalization: !!personalizationData,
          totalAmount: calculateTotalAmount(variant, personalizationData)
        });
        
      } catch (error) {
        showToast('购买失败，请重试', 'error');
        hapticFeedback('error');
        
        trackEvent('direct_purchase_failed', {
          error: error.message,
          variantId: variant.variant_id
        });
      } finally {
        setPurchaseLoading(false);
      }
    },
    
    // 加入购物车处理
    onAddToCart: async () => {
      const variant = getSelectedVariant();
      const personalizationData = getCurrentPersonalizationData();
      const quantity = getSelectedQuantity();
      
      try {
        // 添加到购物车
        const result = await addToCart({
          productId: variant.product_id,
          variantId: variant.variant_id,
          quantity,
          personalization: personalizationData
        });
        
        if (result.success) {
          // 成功反馈
          showToast('已添加到购物车', 'success');
          hapticFeedback('success');
          
          // 更新购物车徽章
          updateCartBadge(result.cartItemCount);
          
          // 显示 Mini Cart
          showMiniCart({
            autoHide: 3000,
            highlightNewItem: result.addedItemId
          });
          
          // 推荐相关商品
          showRelatedProductsRecommendation(variant.product_id);
          
          trackEvent('add_to_cart_success', {
            productId: variant.product_id,
            variantId: variant.variant_id,
            quantity,
            hasPersonalization: !!personalizationData,
            cartTotal: result.cartTotal
          });
        } else {
          throw new Error(result.message);
        }
        
      } catch (error) {
        showToast(`添加失败: ${error.message}`, 'error');
        hapticFeedback('error');
        
        trackEvent('add_to_cart_failed', {
          error: error.message,
          variantId: variant.variant_id
        });
      }
    },
    
    // 购买限制检查
    checkPurchaseRestrictions: (variant: ProductVariant): PurchaseEligibility => {
      const restrictions = [];
      
      // City Exclusive 限制检查
      if (variant.is_city_exclusive) {
        const cityExclusiveStatus = checkCityExclusiveEligibility(variant);
        if (!cityExclusiveStatus.eligible) {
          restrictions.push({
            type: 'CITY_EXCLUSIVE_RESTRICTED',
            message: cityExclusiveStatus.reason,
            suggestion: cityExclusiveStatus.suggestion
          });
        }
      }
      
      // 门店限定检查
      if (variant.is_store_only) {
        const storeOnlyStatus = checkStoreOnlyEligibility(variant);
        if (!storeOnlyStatus.eligible) {
          restrictions.push({
            type: 'STORE_ONLY_RESTRICTED',
            message: storeOnlyStatus.reason,
            suggestion: storeOnlyStatus.suggestion
          });
        }
      }
      
      // 用户购买限制检查
      const userLimits = checkUserPurchaseLimits(variant);
      if (!userLimits.eligible) {
        restrictions.push({
          type: 'USER_LIMIT_EXCEEDED',
          message: userLimits.reason,
          suggestion: userLimits.suggestion
        });
      }
      
      return {
        eligible: restrictions.length === 0,
        restrictions
      };
    }
  }
};
```

### 🔄 **状态同步实现**
```typescript
// PDP 状态同步实现
export const usePDPStateSynchronization = (productId: string) => {
  const [selectedVariant, setSelectedVariant] = useState<ProductVariant | null>(null);
  const [personalizationData, setPersonalizationData] = useState<PersonalizationInput>({
    label_name: '',
    label_date: undefined,
    gift_note: undefined
  });
  const [validationState, setValidationState] = useState<PersonalizationValidationState>({
    isValid: false,
    errors: [],
    warnings: []
  });
  
  // === 容量切换效果 ===
  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedVariant) {
      // 同步价格显示
      PDPCapacityInteractions.priceSync.updatePriceCalculation(selectedVariant);
      
      // 同步库存状态
      PDPCapacityInteractions.stockStatusSync.updateStockDisplay(selectedVariant);
      
      // 实时库存检查
      PDPCapacityInteractions.stockStatusSync.realTimeStockCheck(selectedVariant.variant_id);
      
      // 更新个性化选项
      updatePersonalizationOptions(selectedVariant);
      
      // 保存选择状态
      saveVariantSelection(productId, selectedVariant.variant_id);
    }
  }, [selectedVariant, productId]);
  
  // === 个性化验证效果 ===
  useEffect(() => {
    const validation = PDPCapacityInteractions.personalizationValidationControl
      .validatePersonalizationInput(personalizationData);
    
    setValidationState(validation);
    
    // 更新购买按钮状态
    PDPCapacityInteractions.purchaseButtonControl.updatePurchaseButtonState();
    
    // 保存个性化草稿
    if (selectedVariant) {
      savePersonalizationDraft(
        selectedVariant.variant_id,
        personalizationData,
        validation
      );
    }
    
  }, [personalizationData, selectedVariant]);
  
  // === 组合状态变化处理 ===
  useEffect(() => {
    // 容量 + 个性化组合验证
    if (selectedVariant && personalizationData) {
      const combinedValidation = validateCombinedState(selectedVariant, personalizationData);
      
      if (combinedValidation.hasWarnings) {
        showCombinedStateWarnings(combinedValidation.warnings);
      }
      
      if (combinedValidation.hasConflicts) {
        resolveCombinedStateConflicts(combinedValidation.conflicts);
      }
    }
  }, [selectedVariant, personalizationData]);
  
  return {
    selectedVariant,
    personalizationData,
    validationState,
    
    // 操作方法
    onCapacityChange: (variantId: string, product: Product) => 
      PDPCapacityInteractions.onCapacityChange(variantId, product),
    
    onPersonalizationChange: (data: PersonalizationInput) => {
      setPersonalizationData(data);
      PDPCapacityInteractions.personalizationValidationControl
        .realTimeValidation.onLabelNameChange(data.label_name);
    },
    
    onDirectPurchase: PDPCapacityInteractions.purchaseButtonControl.onDirectPurchase,
    onAddToCart: PDPCapacityInteractions.purchaseButtonControl.onAddToCart
  };
};

// 禁用原因提示消息
const getDisabledReasonMessage = (reason: string): string => {
  const messages = {
    'NO_CAPACITY_SELECTED': '请先选择您需要的容量规格，然后即可购买',
    'OUT_OF_STOCK': '该容量暂时缺货，您可以设置到货提醒或选择其他容量',
    'INVALID_PERSONALIZATION': '个性化定制信息不完整或格式错误，请检查并修正',
    'PURCHASE_RESTRICTED': '该商品有购买限制，请查看详细说明',
    'CITY_EXCLUSIVE_RESTRICTED': '城市限定商品仅在特定时间和地区销售',
    'STORE_ONLY_RESTRICTED': '该商品仅在Le Labo实验室门店有售'
  };
  
  return messages[reason] || '暂时无法购买，请联系客服';
};

// 类型定义
interface PurchaseButtonState {
  state: 'enabled' | 'disabled' | 'loading';
  primaryText: string;
  secondaryText: string;
  disabledReason: string | null;
  allowAddToCart: boolean;
  urgencyMessage?: string;
  alternativeAction?: {
    text: string;
    action: () => void;
  };
}

interface PersonalizationValidationResult {
  isValid: boolean;
  errors: string[];
  warnings: string[];
}

interface PurchaseEligibility {
  eligible: boolean;
  restrictions: Array<{
    type: string;
    message: string;
    suggestion: string;
  }>;
}
```

这个PDP交互逻辑确保了容量切换的即时响应、价格库存的准确同步，以及个性化验证的严格控制，为Le Labo用户提供清晰可靠的产品购买体验。
