Use tool [write_file] with arguments: {"file_path": "/Users/moonshot/Desktop/Moonshot AI/Agent Team/e-commerce/ensoul-env_副本/medusa/my-medusa-store-storefront/src/lib/utils.ts", "content": "import { type ClassValue, clsx } from \"clsx\"\nimport { twMerge } from \"tailwind-merge\"\n\nexport function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]) {\n  return twMerge(clsx(inputs))\n}\n\n// Le Labo specific utility functions\nexport const formatPrice = (amount: number, currency: string = 'CNY'): string => {\n  if (currency === 'CNY') {\n    return `¥${amount.toLocaleString('zh-CN')}`\n  }\n  return `$${amount.toLocaleString('en-US')}`\n}\n\nexport const formatProductName = (name: string): string => {\n  return name.toUpperCase()\n}\n\nexport const getScentFamilyColor = (family: string): string => {\n  const colorMap = {\n    woody: '#8B4513',\n    floral: '#DDA0DD', \n    citrus: '#FFD700',\n    oriental: '#CD853F',\n    fresh: '#40E0D0'\n  }\n  return colorMap[family as keyof typeof colorMap] || '#9CA3AF'\n}\n\nexport const validatePersonalizationLabel = (\n  input: string\n): { isValid: boolean; error?: string; characterCount: number; maxCharacters: number } => {\n  const isChinese = /[\\u4e00-\\u9fa5]/.test(input)\n  const maxLength = isChinese ? 11 : 22\n  const currentLength = input.length\n  \n  // Check length\n  if (currentLength > maxLength) {\n    return {\n      isValid: false,\n      error: `标签名称过长，${isChinese ? '中文' : '英文'}模式最多${maxLength}个字符，当前${currentLength}个`,\n      characterCount: currentLength,\n      maxCharacters: maxLength\n    }\n  }\n  \n  // Check allowed characters\n  const allowedPattern = /^[a-zA-Z0-9\\s\\u4e00-\\u9fa5']*$/\n  if (!allowedPattern.test(input)) {\n    return {\n      isValid: false,\n      error: '只允许使用字母、数字、空格、中文和单引号',\n      characterCount: currentLength,\n      maxCharacters: maxLength\n    }\n  }\n  \n  // Check for consecutive spaces\n  if (/\\s{2,}/.test(input)) {\n    return {\n      isValid: false,\n      error: '不允许连续空格',\n      characterCount: currentLength,\n      maxCharacters: maxLength\n    }\n  }\n  \n  // Check leading/trailing spaces\n  if (input !== input.trim()) {\n    return {\n      isValid: false,\n      error: '开头和结尾不能有空格',\n      characterCount: currentLength,\n      maxCharacters: maxLength\n    }\n  }\n  \n  return {\n    isValid: true,\n    characterCount: currentLength,\n    maxCharacters: maxLength\n  }\n}\n\nexport const validateDate = (dateString: string): { isValid: boolean; error?: string } => {\n  if (!dateString) return { isValid: true }\n  \n  const datePattern = /^\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}$/\n  if (!datePattern.test(dateString)) {\n    return {\n      isValid: false,\n      error: '日期格式错误，请使用 YYYY-MM-DD 格式'\n    }\n  }\n  \n  const date = new Date(dateString)\n  const isValidDate = date instanceof Date && !isNaN(date.getTime())\n  \n  if (!isValidDate) {\n    return {\n      isValid: false,\n      error: '无效的日期'\n    }\n  }\n  \n  // Check date range\n  const minDate = new Date('1900-01-01')\n  const maxDate = new Date('2099-12-31')\n  \n  if (date < minDate || date > maxDate) {\n    return {\n      isValid: false,\n      error: '日期超出有效范围 (1900-2099)'\n    }\n  }\n  \n  return { isValid: true }\n}\n\nexport const validateGiftNote = (input: string): { isValid: boolean; error?: string; characterCount: number } => {\n  if (!input) return { isValid: true, characterCount: 0 }\n  \n  const maxLength = 120\n  const currentLength = input.length\n  \n  if (currentLength > maxLength) {\n    return {\n      isValid: false,\n      error: `寄语过长，最多${maxLength}个字符，当前${currentLength}个字符`,\n      characterCount: currentLength\n    }\n  }\n  \n  // Check for sensitive words (simplified)\n  const sensitiveWords = ['广告', '推广', '联系方式', '电话', '微信']\n  const foundSensitiveWords = sensitiveWords.filter(word => \n    input.toLowerCase().includes(word.toLowerCase())\n  )\n  \n  if (foundSensitiveWords.length > 0) {\n    return {\n      isValid: false,\n      error: `寄语包含不当内容: ${foundSensitiveWords.join(', ')}，请修改`,\n      characterCount: currentLength\n    }\n  }\n  \n  return {\n    isValid: true,\n    characterCount: currentLength\n  }\n}\n\n// Generate Le Labo style product handle\nexport const generateProductHandle = (name: string): string => {\n  return name\n    .toLowerCase()\n    .replace(/\\s+/g, '-')\n    .replace(/[^a-z0-9-]/g, '')\n}\n\n// Format capacity display\nexport const formatCapacity = (sizeML: number): string => {\n  return `${sizeML}ml`\n}\n\n// Calculate cost per ml\nexport const calculateCostPerML = (price: number, sizeML: number): string => {\n  const costPerML = (price / sizeML).toFixed(1)\n  return `¥${costPerML}/ml`\n}"}
